# 16. The Mark of the Beast

> _The mark of the beast is the badge of submission to the beast's authority in the very point where he exalted himself against God — His law and His holy day. The little horn "thought to change times and laws"; the change of the seventh-day Sabbath to Sunday is therefore the sign of his assumed authority, and its acceptance, after the truth is known and Sunday is enforced, is the mark. Over against it stands the seal of God — the fourth commandment, the only precept bearing the Lawgiver's name, title, and dominion. No man has yet received the mark; it is the final test, when Christendom will be divided into just two classes. (DAR 624, 628-630; GC 446-449, 604-605.)_

### The power that thought to change God's law

- **Daniel 7:25** — The little horn shall "speak great words against the most High, and wear out the saints," and "think to change times and laws." The papacy's one attempt to alter the decalogue — striking out the second commandment and changing the fourth from the seventh day to the first — exactly fulfills this; only by changing God's law could it exalt itself above God.

  > "Says Daniel, of the little horn, the papacy: 'He shall think to change times and the law.'… Only by changing God's law could the papacy exalt itself above God; whoever should understandingly keep the law as thus changed would be giving supreme honor to that power by which the change was made. Such an act of obedience to papal laws would be a mark of allegiance to the pope in the place of God." — GC 446.

  > "The 'mark of the beast' is that institution which this power has set up as proof of its authority to legislate for the church… It consists in a change of the law of God, by which the signature of royalty is taken from the law, — the seventh-day Sabbath, the great memorial of Jehovah's creative work, is torn from its place in the decalogue, and a false and counterfeit Sabbath, the first day of the week, is set up in its stead." — DAR 624.

- **Revelation 13:16-17** — The two-horned beast "causeth all, both small and great, rich and poor, free and bond, to receive a mark in their right hand, or in their foreheads," so "that no man might buy or sell, save he that had the mark, or the name of the beast." The mark is enforced by the United States in homage to the papacy.

  > "Of all the symbols mentioned, one alone, the two-horned beast of Revelation 13, is left; and of all the countries of the earth respecting which any reason exists why they should be mentioned in prophecy, one alone, the United States government, remains." — DAR 533-534.

  > "The two-horned beast enforces the mark of the beast; that is, he establishes by law the observance of the first day of the week, or Sunday Sabbath." — DAR 624.

  > "The statement that the beast with two horns 'causeth the earth and them which dwell therein to worship the first beast' indicates that the authority of this nation is to be exercised in enforcing some observance which shall be an act of homage to the papacy." — GC 442.

- **Revelation 13:18** — "Let him that hath understanding count the number of the beast: for it is the number of a man; and his number is Six hundred threescore and six." The number marks the beast as a man-power exalting human authority over God's.

  > "The 'mark of the beast' is that institution which this power has set up as proof of its authority to legislate for the church, and command the consciences of men under sin. It consists in a change of the law of God, by which the signature of royalty is taken from the law." — DAR 624.

### What constitutes the mark — Sunday for the Sabbath

- **Revelation 14:9-11** — The third angel warns "with a loud voice, If any man worship the beast and his image, and receive his mark," he "shall drink of the wine of the wrath of God." The most fearful threatening in Scripture is leveled at this one act of homage.

  > "And now another proclamation is sent forth, announcing in solemn tones that if any man shall do this, he shall drink of the wine of the wrath of God… This is as severe a threatening as God can make to be inflicted in this life, and it is the last." — DAR 620.

  > "The most fearful threatening ever addressed to mortals is contained in the third angel's message. That must be a terrible sin which calls down the wrath of God unmingled with mercy." — GC 449.

- **Revelation 14:12** — Over against the mark stands the remnant: "Here is the patience of the saints: here are they that keep the commandments of God, and the faith of Jesus." The opposite of receiving the mark is keeping the very commandment the beast changed.

  > "The message, presenting as its chief burden a warning against receiving the mark of the beast, will of course bring its adherents to discard that practice which constitutes the mark, and to adopt the opposite. It does lead them to discard the observance of the first day of the week, and adopt that of the seventh day." — DAR 627-628.

  > "While one class, by accepting the sign of submission to earthly powers, receive the mark of the beast, the other, choosing the token of allegiance to divine authority, receive the seal of God." — GC 605.

- **Daniel 7:25 with Exodus 20:8-11** — The only "time" and "law" the papacy claims to have changed is the fourth commandment, moving the Sabbath of the LORD to the first day. Roman writers cite "the very act of changing the Sabbath into Sunday" as "the sign, or mark, of the authority of the Roman Church."

  > "As the sign of the authority of the Catholic Church, papist writers cite 'the very act of changing the Sabbath into Sunday, which Protestants allow of'… What then is the change of the Sabbath, but the sign, or mark, of the authority of the Roman Church—'the mark of the beast'?" — GC 446-448.

- **Romans 6:16** — "To whom ye yield yourselves servants to obey, his servants ye are to whom ye obey." Keeping Sunday in deference to the church's authority is yielding obedience to that power in the place of God.

  > "The two-horned beast enforces the mark of the beast; that is, he establishes by law the observance of the first day of the week, or Sunday Sabbath." — DAR 624.

  > "So this movement explains how this beast-power has brought into Christendom certain institutions which antagonize the requirements of the Most High, and shows that if we yield to these, we worship this power. 'Know ye not,' says Paul, 'that to whom ye yield yourselves servants to obey, his servants ye are to whom ye obey?' Romans 6:16." — DAR 626.

- **The mark defined** — "The mark of the beast is that institution which this power has set up as proof of its authority... It consists in a change of the law of God, by which the seventh-day Sabbath... is torn from its place in the decalogue, and a false and counterfeit Sabbath, the first day of the week, is set up in its stead."

  > "The 'mark of the beast' is that institution which this power has set up as proof of its authority to legislate for the church, and command the consciences of men under sin. It consists in a change of the law of God, by which the signature of royalty is taken from the law, — the seventh-day Sabbath, the great memorial of Jehovah's creative work, is torn from its place in the decalogue, and a false and counterfeit Sabbath, the first day of the week, is set up in its stead." — DAR 624.

### The Sabbath is the seal of God — His name, title, and dominion

- **Exodus 31:13** — "Verily my sabbaths ye shall keep: for it is a sign between me and you... that ye may know that I am the LORD that doth sanctify you." God Himself names the Sabbath His sign; sign, seal, token, and mark are synonymous in Scripture usage.

  > "We have seen above that in Scripture usage, sign, seal, token, and mark are synonymous terms. Now, the Lord expressly says that the Sabbath is a sign between him and his people. 'Verily my Sabbaths ye shall keep; for it is a sign between me and you throughout your generations; that ye may know that I am the Lord that doth sanctify you.' Exodus 31:13." — DAR 440.

  > "The keeping of the true Sabbath, in obedience to God's law, is an evidence of loyalty to the Creator." — GC 605.

- **Exodus 31:17** — "It is a sign between me and the children of Israel for ever: for in six days the LORD made heaven and earth... and on the seventh day he rested." The sign rests on creation — the Sabbath identifies the LORD as Creator, the only ground of His right to rule.

  > "On my part it is the seal of my authority, the sign that I have the right to command obedience; on your part it is a token that you take me to be your God." — DAR 440-441.

- **Ezekiel 20:12** — "I gave them my sabbaths, to be a sign between me and them, that they might know that I am the LORD that sanctify them." The same divine seal is repeated by the prophet.

  > "Here the Lord told his people that the very object of their keeping the Sabbath, that is, observing the fourth commandment, was that they might know that he was the true God." — DAR 440.

- **Ezekiel 20:20** — "Hallow my sabbaths; and they shall be a sign between me and you, that ye may know that I am the LORD your God." The Sabbath is the appointed token by which God's people know and avow their God.

  > "The same fact is again stated by the prophet Ezekiel 20:12, 20. Here the Lord told his people that the very object of their keeping the Sabbath, that is, observing the fourth commandment, was that they might know that he was the true God." — DAR 440.

- **Exodus 20:8-11** — The fourth commandment alone contains the name ("the LORD thy God"), His title and creative work ("the LORD made heaven and earth"), and His dominion ("heaven and earth, the sea, and all that in them is"). A seal must carry the name, office, and territory of the lawgiver — the fourth commandment alone supplies all three, and so is the seal of the law.

  > "As we examine this commandment, which contains the declaration, 'For in six days the Lord made heaven and earth, the sea, and all that in them is,' etc., we see at once that we are reading the requirements of Him who created all things… Now the author of this law has declared who he is, the extent of his dominion, and his right to rule… Thus with the fourth commandment in its place, this wonderful document, the decalogue… has a signature; it has that which renders it intelligible and authentic; it has a seal. But without the fourth commandment, it lacks all these things." — DAR 438-440.

- **Genesis 2:1-3** — "God blessed the seventh day, and sanctified it: because that in it he had rested." The Sabbath was sanctified at creation, before sin, and so is primary, immutable, and eternal — not a ceremonial shadow that ended at the cross.

  > "The laws and institutions which existed before man's fall were primary in their nature… In other words, they were, in the very nature of things, immutable and eternal… These [ceremonial and typical laws], and these only, were abolished at the cross. The Sabbath law was a primary law, and therefore immutable and eternal." — DAR 630.

- **A seal authenticates a law** — "A seal is used to render valid or authentic any enactments... the name must always be used." Passing through the decalogue, only the fourth commandment shows _who_ the Lawgiver is and _by what right_ He commands — it is the seal of God.

  > "A seal is used to render valid or authentic any enactments, or laws, which a person or power may promulgate… Even with a literal seal, the name must always be used… affix the signature of royalty, showing who it is that demands obedience, and his right to demand it." — DAR 438-439.

### The seal of God in the forehead — the contrast company

- **Revelation 7:2-3** — "Another angel ascending from the east, having the seal of the living God... Hurt not the earth... till we have sealed the servants of our God in their foreheads." The sealing is the binding of the Sabbath truth upon God's people before the winds of trouble are loosed.

  > "By the scenes introduced in the verses before us, namely, the four winds apparently about to blow, bringing war and trouble upon the land, and this work restrained till the servants of God should be sealed… we conclude that the seal of God, here placed upon his servants, is some distinguishing mark, or religious characteristic, through which they will be exempted from the judgments of God that fall on the wicked around them." — DAR 442.

  > "He who reads every heart and tries every motive will leave none who desire a knowledge of the truth, to be deceived as to the issues of the controversy… Everyone is to have sufficient light to make his decision intelligently." — GC 605.

- **Revelation 7:4** — "There were sealed an hundred and forty and four thousand of all the tribes of the children of Israel" — the same company developed by the third angel's message.

  > "He is thus addressing the last generation of Christians, the Christians of our own day; and he calls them the twelve tribes scattered abroad… so, as belonging to these twelve tribes, will all the redeemed be reckoned, whether on earth they were Jews or Gentiles." — DAR 446.

- **Revelation 14:1** — The 144,000 stand with the Lamb "having his Father's name written in their foreheads." The seal in the forehead (Rev 7:3) is the Father's name (Rev 14:1) — and the name is found in the fourth commandment.

  > "Again, the 144,000, who in the chapter before us are said to be sealed with the seal of God in their foreheads, are again brought to view in Revelation 14:1, where they are said to have the Father's name written in their foreheads." — DAR 439.

- **Ezekiel 9:4** — "Set a mark upon the foreheads of the men that sigh and that cry for all the abominations." The slaughter is stayed for those who bear God's distinguishing mark — the type of the final sealing.

  > "We are reminded… of the mark made by the man with a writer's ink-horn (Ezekiel 9) upon all those who were to be spared by the men with the slaughtering weapons who followed after; and we conclude that the seal of God, here placed upon his servants, is some distinguishing mark, or religious characteristic, through which they will be exempted from the judgments of God." — DAR 442.

- **Isaiah 8:16** — "Bind up the testimony, seal the law among my disciples." The closing work seals the law — and the seal of the law is the Sabbath.

  > "Having now ascertained that the seal of God is his holy Sabbath, having his name, we are prepared to proceed with the application." — DAR 442.

- **Revelation 22:14** — "Blessed are they that do his commandments, that they may have right to the tree of life, and may enter in through the gates into the city." Only the commandment-keepers — those bearing the seal, not the mark — enter the city.

  > "It brings out a company of whom it can be said, 'Here are they that keep the commandments of God and the faith of Jesus.'… No plainer line of demarcation could be drawn between two classes." — DAR 627.

  > "The keeping of the true Sabbath, in obedience to God's law, is an evidence of loyalty to the Creator. While one class, by accepting the sign of submission to earthly powers, receive the mark of the beast, the other, choosing the token of allegiance to divine authority, receive the seal of God." — GC 605.

### Seal versus mark — the two opposing signs

|            | **Seal of God**                       | **Mark of the beast**                     |
| ---------- | ------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------- |
| Sign       | The seventh-day Sabbath               | The first-day (Sunday) sabbath            |
| Authority  | The Creator, by His own law           | The papacy, by changing God's law         |
| Placed     | "In their foreheads" (Rev 7:3)        | In forehead or hand (Rev 13:16)           |
| Located in | The fourth commandment (Ex 20:8-11)   | The change of that commandment (Dan 7:25) |
| Result     | Right to the tree of life (Rev 22:14) | The wine of God's wrath (Rev 14:10)       |

- **The two classes** — "Christendom will at last be divided into just two classes... those who are sealed with the seal of the living God... and those who are sealed with the seal of the beast." One keeps the seventh day, the other the first — opposite extremities of the week.

  > "One is the seventh day; the other is just as far removed from it as possible, even to the other extremity of the week, namely, the first day. Christendom will at last be divided into just two classes; to wit, those who are sealed with the seal of the living God, that is, have his mark, or keep his Sabbath; and those who are sealed with the seal of the beast, that is, have his mark, or keep his Sabbath." — DAR 628.

  > "In the issue of the contest all Christendom will be divided into two great classes—those who keep the commandments of God and the faith of Jesus, and those who worship the beast and his image and receive his mark." — GC 450.

- **Revelation 12:17** with **Revelation 14:12** — The dragon makes war on "the remnant... which keep the commandments of God, and have the testimony of Jesus Christ." The mark divides the world; the commandment-keeping remnant is the company that refuses it.

  > "It brings out a company of whom it can be said, 'Here are they that keep the commandments of God and the faith of Jesus.' In the very heart of Christendom this work is done; and those who receive the message are rendered peculiar by their practice in reference to the commandments of God." — DAR 627.

### The mark is not yet received — it is the final test

- **Revelation 14:9-10** — "But not one is made to suffer the wrath of God until the truth has been brought home to his mind and conscience, and has been rejected." The mark cannot be received in ignorance; everyone is given sufficient light to decide intelligently.

  > "But not one is made to suffer the wrath of God until the truth has been brought home to his mind and conscience, and has been rejected… The decree is not to be urged upon the people blindly. Everyone is to have sufficient light to make his decision intelligently." — GC 605.

- **Isaiah 58:12-14** — The remnant are "the repairer of the breach, the restorer of paths to dwell in," who "turn away thy foot from the sabbath." The breach is the gap left in God's law by the change of the Sabbath; the last message repairs it.

  > "This is in harmony with the argument on the seal of God… It was there shown that sign, seal, mark, and token are synonymous terms, and that God takes his Sabbath to be his sign, mark, or seal, in reference to his people." — DAR 628.

- **The final test** — "The Sabbath will be the great test of loyalty, for it is the point of truth especially controverted." Only when Sunday observance is enforced by law, and the truth of the Sabbath is set before all, will the line be drawn: one class receiving the mark of the beast, the other the seal of God.

  > "The Sabbath will be the great test of loyalty, for it is the point of truth especially controverted. When the final test shall be brought to bear upon men, then the line of distinction will be drawn between those who serve God and those who serve Him not." — GC 605.

  > "The third message utters a solemn protest against all this evil. It exposes the work of the beast, shows the nature of its opposition to the law of God, warns the people against compliance with its demands, and points out to all the way of truth." — DAR 624-625.

- **The decisive choice** — "Whoever shall trample upon God's law to obey a human enactment receives the mark of the beast; he accepts the sign of allegiance to the power which he chooses to obey instead of God." The mark is finally received in the act of choosing man's enforced sabbath over God's.

  > "With the issue thus clearly brought before him, whoever shall trample upon God's law to obey a human enactment receives the mark of the beast; he accepts the sign of allegiance to the power which he chooses to obey instead of God." — GC 604.

  > "It is shown that the mark of the beast is an institution which has been arrayed in Christian garb, and insidiously introduced into the Christian church in such a way as to nullify the authority of Jehovah and enthrone that of the beast. Stripped of all disguises, it is simply setting up a counterfeit Sabbath of its own on the first day of the week, in place of the Sabbath of the Lord on the seventh day." — DAR 626.

- **James 2:10-12** — "Whosoever shall keep the whole law, and yet offend in one point, he is guilty of all." To break the fourth commandment for the sake of the beast's institution is to break the whole law — there is no neutral ground in the final test.

  > "The Sabbath is a part of that law which our Lord openly declared that he came not to destroy. On the other hand, he most solemnly affirmed that it should endure in every jot and tittle while the earth should continue. Matthew 5:17-20." — DAR 630.

# 17. The Three Angels' Messages (Revelation 14)

> Here is the present-truth climax of all prophecy: three angels flying in the midst of heaven with the final warning to the world, immediately followed by the Son of man on the white cloud reaping the harvest of the earth. The first angel proclaims the everlasting gospel and announces that "the hour of His judgment is come" — the 1844 judgment-hour message — and calls men back to worship the Creator, which is the Sabbath. The second cries "Babylon is fallen." The third utters the most fearful threatening in all Scripture against the worship of the beast, his image, and his mark; and over against that warning stand "they that keep the commandments of God, and the faith of Jesus." This threefold message is the last to be given before Christ comes. (DAR 582-637; GC 311, 355-390, 435-453.)

### The Three Messages at a Glance

| Angel              | Cry (Rev 14)                                                               | Historicist meaning                                                         | Pioneer ref          |
| ------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------- |
| First (v. 6-7)     | "Fear God... the hour of His judgment is come... worship Him that made..." | The 1844 advent / judgment-hour message; call back to the Creator's Sabbath | DAR 585; GC 355      |
| Second (v. 8)      | "Babylon is fallen, is fallen"                                             | Moral fall of the apostate churches; call to come out                       | DAR 600, 607; GC 381 |
| Third (v. 9-12)    | "If any man worship the beast and his image..."                            | The mark warning; the commandment-keeping remnant                           | DAR 619, 626; GC 438 |
| The Reaper (v. 14) | "Thrust in thy sickle... the harvest of the earth is ripe"                 | The second coming and the harvest                                           | DAR 635; GC 311      |

### The First Angel — The Everlasting Gospel and the Judgment Hour

- **Rev. 14:6** — "And I saw another angel fly in the midst of heaven, having the everlasting gospel to preach unto them that dwell on the earth, and to every nation, and kindred, and tongue, and people." A worldwide religious movement, not a single preacher; the same gospel that has saved men in every age now sounds its final call.

  > "These angels are evidently symbolic; for the work assigned them is that of preaching the everlasting gospel to the people. But the preaching of the gospel has not been intrusted to literal angels; it has been committed unto men… Each of these three angels, therefore, symbolizes a body of religious teachers, who are commissioned to make known to their fellow men the special truths which constitute the burden of these messages respectively." — DAR 586.

  > "And the angel's flight 'in the midst of heaven,' the 'loud voice' with which the warning is uttered, and its promulgation to all 'that dwell on the earth,'—'to every nation, and kindred, and tongue, and people,'—give evidence of the rapidity and world-wide extent of the movement." — GC 355.

- **Rev. 14:7** — "Fear God, and give glory to him; for the hour of his judgment is come." Not the gospel in general, but the gospel tied to a definite hour — the opening of the investigative judgment in 1844. The message itself fixes the time.

  > "The messages described in these verses are known as 'the three angels' messages of Revelation 14.' We are justified in applying to them the ordinals, first, second, and third, by the prophecy itself; for the last one is distinctly called 'the third angel.'" — DAR 586.

  > "The message itself sheds light as to the time when this movement is to take place. It is declared to be a part of the 'everlasting gospel;' and it announces the opening of the judgment… this message is a part of the gospel which could be proclaimed only in the last days, for only then would it be true that the hour of judgment had come." — GC 355.

- **Dan. 8:14** — "Unto two thousand and three hundred days; then shall the sanctuary be cleansed." The 2300 years (Num. 14:34; Eze. 4:6) reach from 457 B.C. to A.D. 1844; the cleansing of the sanctuary is the judgment hour the first angel announces.

  > "It brings to view a period of making ready the guests for the marriage of the Lamb, which is the work of Judgment to which the message brings us when it declares, 'The hour of his Judgment is come.'… It went forth with the power thus indicated between the years 1840-44… bringing us to the end of the 2300 days, when the work of Judgment commenced as Christ began the work of cleansing the sanctuary." — DAR 600.

  > "The prophecies present a succession of events leading down to the opening of the judgment. This is especially true of the book of Daniel. But that part of his prophecy which related to the last days, Daniel was bidden to close up and seal 'to the time of the end.'" — GC 355.

- **Dan. 7:9-10** — "The Ancient of days did sit... the judgment was set, and the books were opened." The same judgment scene the angel proclaims as "come"; heaven's court convenes before Christ returns.

  > "A great religious awakening under the proclamation of Christ's soon coming is foretold in the prophecy of the first angel's message of Revelation 14… 'With a loud voice' he proclaims the message: 'Fear God, and give glory to Him; for the hour of His judgment is come.'" — GC 355.

  > "Thus was presented to the prophet's vision the great and solemn day when the characters and the lives of men should pass in review before the Judge of all the earth… It is this coming, and not His second advent to the earth, that was foretold in prophecy to take place at the termination of the 2300 days in 1844." — GC 479-480.

- **Eccl. 12:13-14** — "Fear God, and keep his commandments: for this is the whole duty of man. For God shall bring every work into judgment." The exact echo of the first angel: fear God, give Him glory, for the judgment is come.

  > "We are justified in applying to them the ordinals, first, second, and third, by the prophecy itself; for the last one is distinctly called 'the third angel,' from which it follows that the one preceding was the second angel; and the one before that, the first angel." — DAR 586.

- **Matt. 24:14** — "This gospel of the kingdom shall be preached in all the world for a witness unto all nations; and then shall the end come." The everlasting gospel of the first angel is the world-wide witness that immediately precedes the end.

  > "By the purity, the glory, and the power of the heavenly messenger, divine wisdom has been pleased to represent the exalted character of the work to be accomplished by the message and the power and glory that were to attend it." — GC 355.

- **Mark 16:15** — "Go ye into all the world, and preach the gospel to every creature." The commission that the first angel carries to "every nation, and kindred, and tongue, and people."

  > "VERSE 6. And I saw another angel fly in the midst of heaven, having the everlasting gospel to preach unto them that dwell on the earth, and to every nation, and kindred, and tongue, and people." — DAR 585.

### The First Message Calls Men to Worship the Creator — the Sabbath

- **Rev. 14:7** — "...and worship him that made heaven, and earth, and the sea, and the fountains of waters." The closing summons is a call back to the Creator, in the very language of the fourth commandment — the Sabbath is the badge of allegiance to the Maker.

  > "The keeping of the Sabbath is a sign of loyalty to the true God, 'Him that made heaven, and earth, and the sea, and the fountains of waters.' It follows that the message which commands men to worship God and keep His commandments will especially call upon them to keep the fourth commandment." — GC 437.

- **Exo. 20:8-11** — "Remember the sabbath day... For in six days the LORD made heaven and earth, the sea, and all that in them is, and rested the seventh day." The Sabbath commandment and the first angel's call use the same words ("made heaven, and earth, and the sea"); to worship the Creator is to keep His memorial day.

  > "The Sabbath is not indefinite, any seventh day after six of labor. The law from Sinai (Exodus 20:8-11) makes it as definite as language can make it; the events that gave it birth (Genesis 2:1-3) confine it to the definite seventh day." — DAR 630.

  > "In Revelation 14, men are called upon to worship the Creator; and the prophecy brings to view a class that, as the result of the threefold message, are keeping the commandments of God. One of these commandments points directly to God as the Creator. The fourth precept declares: 'The seventh day is the Sabbath of the Lord thy God: ... for in six days the Lord made heaven and earth, the sea, and all that in them is.'" — GC 437.

- **Rev. 4:11** — "Thou art worthy, O Lord, to receive glory and honour and power: for thou hast created all things." Worship is due to God precisely because He is the Creator — the ground on which the first angel summons the world.

  > "'The importance of the Sabbath as the memorial of creation is that it keeps ever present the true reason why worship is due to God'—because He is the Creator, and we are His creatures… The true ground of divine worship… is found in the distinction between the Creator and His creatures." — GC 437.

- **Ezek. 20:12** — "I gave them my sabbaths, to be a sign between me and them, that they might know that I am the LORD that sanctify them." The Sabbath is the appointed sign of the Creator-God; it is the very point the first angel revives.

  > "Concerning the Sabbath, the Lord says, further, that it is 'a sign, ... that ye may know that I am the Lord your God.' Ezekiel 20:20. And the reason given is: 'For in six days the Lord made heaven and earth, and on the seventh day He rested, and was refreshed.'" — GC 437.

- **Acts 17:24-31** — "God that made the world and all things therein... now commandeth all men every where to repent: because he hath appointed a day, in the which he will judge the world." Paul joins the same two truths the first angel joins: the Creator and the coming judgment.

  > "These angels are evidently symbolic; for the work assigned them is that of preaching the everlasting gospel to the people… Each of these three angels, therefore, symbolizes a body of religious teachers, who are commissioned to make known to their fellow men the special truths which constitute the burden of these messages respectively." — DAR 586.

- **Psa. 96:5** — "All the gods of the nations are idols: but the LORD made the heavens." The Maker alone is to be worshipped; the first message restores the worship of the true Creator-God in an idolatrous world.

  > "Had the Sabbath been universally kept, man's thoughts and affections would have been led to the Creator as the object of reverence and worship, and there would never have been an idolater, an atheist, or an infidel." — GC 437.

### The Second Angel — "Babylon Is Fallen"

- **Rev. 14:8** — "Babylon is fallen, is fallen, that great city, because she made all nations drink of the wine of the wrath of her fornication." The apostate church, fallen from her first purity, makes the nations drunk with false doctrine; first preached in the summer of 1844.

  > "The Second Message. — This message, following the first, is announced (verse 8) in these few words: 'And there followed another angel, saying, Babylon is fallen, is fallen, that great city, because she made all nations drink of the wine of the wrath of her fornication.'… It is therefore a part of that religious movement which takes place in the last days with especial reference to the coming of Christ." — DAR 600.

  > "The reason given why Babylon meets with this moral fall is 'because she made all nations drink of the wine of the wrath … of her fornication.' There is but one thing to which this can refer, and that is false doctrines. She has corrupted the pure truths of God's word, and made the nations drunken with pleasing fables." — DAR 607-608.

  > "The second angel's message of Revelation 14 was first preached in the summer of 1844, and it then had a more direct application to the churches of the United States, where the warning of the judgment had been most widely proclaimed and most generally rejected." — GC 389.

  > "Not until this condition shall be reached, and the union of the church with the world shall be fully accomplished throughout Christendom, will the fall of Babylon be complete. The change is a progressive one, and the perfect fulfillment of Revelation 14:8 is yet future." — GC 389.

- **Rev. 17:1-6** — "Babylon the Great, the Mother of Harlots... drunken with the blood of the saints." Babylon is the great harlot riding the beast — the union of apostate religion with the world; her "daughters" are the fallen Protestant churches.

  > "The true church is a chaste virgin. 2 Corinthians 11:2. The church that is joined with the world in friendship, is a harlot. It is this unlawful connection with the kings of the earth that constitutes her the great harlot of the Apocalypse." — DAR 607.

  > "Babylon is said to be 'the mother of harlots.' By her daughters must be symbolized churches that cling to her doctrines and traditions, and follow her example of sacrificing the truth and the approval of God, in order to form an unlawful alliance with the world." — GC 381-382.

- **Isa. 21:9** — "Babylon is fallen, is fallen; and all the graven images of her gods he hath broken." The prophetic phrase the second angel borrows; the doom of literal Babylon foreshadows the moral fall of mystic Babylon.

  > "As Babylon is not a literal city, the fall cannot be a literal overthrow… The fall is therefore a moral fall; for after the fall, the voice is addressed to the people of God who are still in her connection, 'Come out of her, my people.'" — DAR 607.

- **Rev. 18:1-3** — "Babylon the great is fallen, is fallen, and is become the habitation of devils... For all nations have drunk of the wine of the wrath of her fornication." The fuller "loud cry" repetition of the second angel's message under the mighty angel whose glory lightens the earth.

  > "The fall of Babylon will next claim attention. Having now learned what constitutes Babylon, it will not be difficult to decide what is meant by the declaration that Babylon is fallen." — DAR 607.

  > "'I saw another angel come down from heaven, having great power; and the earth was lightened with his glory. And he cried mightily with a strong voice, saying, Babylon the great is fallen, is fallen.'… This scripture points forward to a time when the announcement of the fall of Babylon, as made by the second angel of Revelation 14 (verse 8), is to be repeated." — GC 603.

- **Rev. 18:4** — "Come out of her, my people, that ye be not partakers of her sins, and that ye receive not of her plagues." Because Babylon is fallen, God calls His honest-hearted people out of her before the plagues fall.

  > "The second message announces the fall of Babylon, and a voice is heard from heaven after that, saying, 'Come out of her, my people.'… They cannot be called out of Babylon after this. Christ does not take them to Babylon, but to the Father's house, where there are many mansions." — DAR 590.

  > "Revelation 18 points to the time when, as the result of rejecting the threefold warning of Revelation 14:6-12, the church will have fully reached the condition foretold by the second angel, and the people of God still in Babylon will be called upon to separate from her communion… all the children of the Lord that remain in Babylon will heed the call: 'Come out of her, My people' (Revelation 18:4)." — GC 390.

  > "'And I heard another voice from heaven, saying, Come out of her, My people, that ye be not partakers of her sins, and that ye receive not of her plagues.' Revelation 18:1, 2, 4." — GC 603.

- **Rev. 18:5** — "For her sins have reached unto heaven, and God hath remembered her iniquities." The measure of her cup is full; her judgment is certain — the reason the call to separate is so urgent.

  > "This message is the last that will ever be given to the world; and it will accomplish its work. When those that 'believed not the truth, but had pleasure in unrighteousness'… shall be left to receive strong delusion and to believe a lie, then the light of truth will shine upon all whose hearts are open to receive it." — GC 390.

- **2 Cor. 6:17** — "Come out from among them, and be ye separate, saith the Lord... and I will receive you." The standing principle behind the second angel's call: God's people must not remain in fellowship with a fallen communion.

  > "As these bodies depart further and further from the truth, and ally themselves more closely with the world, the difference between the two classes will widen, and it will finally result in separation. The time will come when those who love God supremely can no longer remain in connection with such as are 'lovers of pleasures more than lovers of God.'" — GC 390.

### The Third Angel — The Mark Warning and the Commandment-Keeping Remnant

- **Rev. 14:9-10** — "If any man worship the beast and his image, and receive his mark... the same shall drink of the wine of the wrath of God, which is poured out without mixture into the cup of his indignation." The most fearful threatening ever addressed to mortals; the only sin that calls down wrath unmingled with mercy.

  > "This is a message of most fearful import. No severer threatening of divine wrath can be found in all the Bible. The sin against which it warns must be a terrible sin, and it must be one so plainly defined that all who will may understand it, and thus know how to avoid the judgments denounced against it." — DAR 619.

  > "The most fearful threatening ever addressed to mortals is contained in the third angel's message. That must be a terrible sin which calls down the wrath of God unmingled with mercy." — GC 449.

- **Rev. 14:11** — "And the smoke of their torment ascendeth up for ever and ever: and they have no rest day nor night, who worship the beast and his image." The third angel's solemn warning against receiving the mark; the final, irrevocable separation of the lost.

  > "You rejected the first message, and met with a moral fall; continue to reject truth and disregard the warnings sent out, and you will exhaust God's last means of grace, and by and by meet with a literal destruction for which there will be no remedy. This is as severe a threatening as God can make to be inflicted in this life, and it is the last." — DAR 619-620.

  > "Men are not to be left in darkness concerning this important matter; the warning against this sin is to be given to the world before the visitation of God's judgments, that all may know why they are to be inflicted, and have opportunity to escape them." — GC 449.

- **Rev. 13:15-17** — "...cause that as many as would not worship the image of the beast should be killed... receive a mark in their right hand, or in their foreheads." The mark the third angel warns against is the enforced worship of the beast through his image — apostate Protestantism (the two-horned beast / United States) compelling allegiance to Rome.

  > "The powers of darkness will press forward their work, and if the churches still persist in this course of shunning light and rejecting truth, they will soon find themselves worshiping the beast and receiving his mark. This will be the logical sequence of that course of action which commenced with the rejection of the first message." — DAR 620.

  > "When the leading churches of the United States, uniting upon such points of doctrine as are held by them in common, shall influence the state to enforce their decrees and to sustain their institutions, then Protestant America will have formed an image of the Roman hierarchy… The 'image to the beast' represents that form of apostate Protestantism which will be developed when the Protestant churches shall seek the aid of the civil power for the enforcement of their dogmas." — GC 445.

- **Dan. 7:25** — "He shall... think to change times and laws." The little horn (the Papacy) presumed to change God's law, transferring the Sabbath to Sunday; the third message restores the trampled commandment, and Sunday-keeping enforced becomes the mark.

  > "Stripped of all disguises, it is simply setting up a counterfeit Sabbath of its own on the first day of the week, in place of the Sabbath of the Lord on the seventh day, — a usurpation which the great God cannot tolerate, and from which the remnant church must fully clear itself before it will be prepared for the coming of Christ." — DAR 626.

  > "The special characteristic of the beast, and therefore of his image, is the breaking of God's commandments. Says Daniel, of the little horn, the papacy: 'He shall think to change times and the law.'… The papacy has attempted to change the law of God… the fourth commandment has been so changed as to authorize the observance of the first instead of the seventh day as the Sabbath." — GC 446-447.

- **Rev. 14:12** — "Here is the patience of the saints: here are they that keep the commandments of God, and the faith of Jesus." Over against the worshippers of the beast stand the remnant — keeping all ten commandments (including the Sabbath) and holding the faith of Jesus.

  > "This movement follows the two previously mentioned. It takes up and continues the promulgation of the truths they uttered, and adds to them what the third message involves besides… It is shown that the mark of the beast is an institution which has been arrayed in Christian garb, and insidiously introduced into the Christian church in such a way as to nullify the authority of Jehovah and enthrone that of the beast." — DAR 626.

  > "After the warning against the worship of the beast and his image the prophecy declares: 'Here are they that keep the commandments of God, and the faith of Jesus.' Since those who keep God's commandments are thus placed in contrast with those that worship the beast and his image and receive his mark, it follows that the keeping of God's law, on the one hand, and its violation, on the other, will make the distinction between the worshipers of God and the worshipers of the beast." — GC 445.

- **Rev. 12:17** — "The dragon was wroth with the woman, and went to make war with the remnant of her seed, which keep the commandments of God, and have the testimony of Jesus Christ." The same commandment-keeping remnant; the third angel's people are the object of the dragon's last wrath.

  > "The third message warns all against receiving the mark of the beast. So this movement makes it the burden of its work to show what the mark of the beast is, and to warn against its reception… a usurpation which the great God cannot tolerate, and from which the remnant church must fully clear itself before it will be prepared for the coming of Christ." — DAR 626.

  > "The beast with two horns 'causeth [commands] all, both small and great… to receive a mark in their right hand, or in their foreheads'… The third angel's warning is: 'If any man worship the beast and his image, and receive his mark in his forehead, or in his hand, the same shall drink of the wine of the wrath of God.'" — GC 445.

- **Rev. 22:14** — "Blessed are they that do his commandments, that they may have right to the tree of life, and may enter in through the gates into the city." The closing benediction of the Bible matches the third angel: only the commandment-keepers enter the city.

  > "The third message warns all against worshiping the beast. So this movement explains how this beast-power has brought into Christendom certain institutions which antagonize the requirements of the Most High, and shows that if we yield to these, we worship this power." — DAR 626.

- **Rev. 14:1-5** — "A Lamb stood on the mount Sion, and with him an hundred forty and four thousand, having his Father's name written in their foreheads... without fault before the throne of God." The fruit of the threefold message: the sealed, victorious company that stands with the Lamb — the firstfruits redeemed from the earth.

  > "Glance forward with the apostle to the very next scene in the program, and what do we behold? — The very same company standing on Mount Zion with the Lamb, — a victorious company, harping on symphonious harps their triumph in the court of heaven. Thus are we assured that when the time of our conflict with the powers of darkness comes, deliverance is not only certain, but will immediately be given." — DAR 582-585.

  > "With the Lamb upon Mount Zion, 'having the harps of God,' they stand, the hundred and forty and four thousand that were redeemed from among men… These, having been translated from the earth, from among the living, are counted as 'the first fruits unto God and to the Lamb.'… 'In their mouth was found no guile: for they are without fault' before God." — GC 648-649.

### A Blessing on the Dead Who Die in the Lord

- **Rev. 14:13** — "Blessed are the dead which die in the Lord from henceforth... that they may rest from their labours; and their works do follow them." A special benediction pronounced "from henceforth" — from the time the third message begins; those who die in this last truth are blessed.

  > "In verse 18 of Revelation 14, a blessing is pronounced upon the dead which die in the Lord 'from henceforth;' that is, from the time the third message begins to be given. This is a complete demonstration of the fact that the message must be given prior to the first resurrection." — DAR 590.

### The Harvest of the Earth — The Second Coming

- **Rev. 14:14** — "And I looked, and behold a white cloud, and upon the cloud one sat like unto the Son of man, having on his head a golden crown, and in his hand a sharp sickle." Immediately after the threefold message comes Christ on the cloud — crowned, with the sickle of harvest; the messages are the last warning before His appearing.

  > "The message of the fourth angel is evidently to be uttered after the Son of man, having finished his priestly work, takes his seat upon the white cloud, but before he appears in the clouds of heaven… He that is seated upon the cloud will thrust in his sickle, and the saints, under the figure of the wheat of the earth, will be gathered into the heavenly garner." — DAR 635.

  > "Here is a threefold message represented as proclaimed by heavenly beings and immediately followed by the coming of the Son of man to reap 'the harvest of the earth.' The first of these warnings announces the approaching judgment." — GC 311.

  > "And this message is the last to be given before the coming of the Lord. Immediately following its proclamation the Son of man is seen by the prophet, coming in glory to reap the harvest of the earth." — GC 453.

- **Rev. 14:15-16** — "Thrust in thy sickle, and reap: for the time is come for thee to reap; for the harvest of the earth is ripe... and the earth was reaped." The ingathering of the righteous (the wheat) at the second advent.

  > "The Wheat Garnered. — 'And he that sat on the cloud,' says the prophecy, 'thrust in his sickle on the earth; and the earth was reaped.' By this language we are carried down past the second Advent, with its accompanying scenes of destruction to the wicked and salvation to the righteous." — DAR 635.

- **Matt. 13:39** — "The harvest is the end of the world; and the reapers are the angels." Christ's own key: the harvest of Rev. 14 is the end of the world.

  > "It is doubtless the day-and-night cry spoken of by our Lord in Luke 18:7, 8 in connection with the coming of the Son of man. And this prayer will be answered; the elect will be avenged… He that is seated upon the cloud will thrust in his sickle, and the saints, under the figure of the wheat of the earth, will be gathered into the heavenly garner." — DAR 635.

- **Rev. 14:17-18** — "Another angel came out from the altar... Thrust in thy sharp sickle, and gather the clusters of the vine of the earth; for her grapes are fully ripe." The second, terrible harvest — the gathering of the wicked for destruction.

  > "The last two angels have to do with the wicked, — the wicked, most fitly represented by the bloated and purple clusters of the vine of the earth… the wicked perishing around the city at the time of its ultimate location upon the earth." — DAR 635-636.

- **Rev. 14:19-20** — "The angel thrust in his sickle... and cast it into the great winepress of the wrath of God... blood came out of the winepress, even unto the horse bridles, by the space of a thousand and six hundred furlongs." The treading of the winepress: the slaying of the lost when Christ appears in vengeance.

  > "To carry out the figure, the wicked, having been likened to the clusters of the vine of the earth, are said to be cast into the great winepress, which is trodden without the city. And blood comes out of the winepress, even to the horses' bridles… It is not improbable that this language will be literally fulfilled." — DAR 636.

  > "At the coming of Christ the wicked are blotted from the face of the whole earth—consumed with the spirit of His mouth and destroyed by the brightness of His glory. Christ takes His people to the City of God, and the earth is emptied of its inhabitants." — GC 657.

- **Joel 3:13** — "Put ye in the sickle, for the harvest is ripe... for the press is full, the fats overflow; for their wickedness is great." The Old Testament source of the winepress imagery — the day of the Lord's vengeance.

  > "This passage brings to view the time when they perish in the 'winepress' of his wrath, which is said to be trodden 'without the city,' answering completely to the description of Revelation 20:9; and this latter expression would more naturally denote their complete and final destruction." — DAR 636.

- **Rev. 19:11-15** — "He was clothed with a vesture dipped in blood... and he treadeth the winepress of the fierceness and wrath of Almighty God." The Faithful and True on the white horse treads the same winepress; the harvest of Rev. 14 and the advent of Rev. 19 are one event — the close of the three angels' work.

  > "We know that the wicked are doomed to be swallowed up at last in a flood of all-devouring flame descending from God out of heaven… As the first four angels of this series denoted a marked movement on the part of the people of God, the last two may denote the same; for the saints are to have some part to act in meting out and executing the final punishment of the wicked." — DAR 636.

  > "The King of kings descends upon the cloud, wrapped in flaming fire. The heavens are rolled together as a scroll, the earth trembles before Him, and every mountain and island is moved out of its place. 'Our God shall come, and shall not keep silence: a fire shall devour before Him.'" — GC 641-642.

---

# 18. The 144,000 and the Great Multitude

> _Revelation brings to view two companies among the redeemed. The 144,000 are the living saints sealed in the forehead and translated without seeing death — the firstfruits, who stand with the Lamb on Mount Zion bearing the Father's name, follow Him whithersoever He goeth, and sing a song of deliverance no other host can learn. The great multitude is the larger, uncountable throng of the redeemed of all ages, raised from the dead at the second advent, who came out of great tribulation and washed their robes white in the blood of the Lamb. The sealing of the 144,000 is the last work done for God's people before translation. (DAR 446-451; DAR 582-584; GC 648-649; EW 15-18.)_

### The Sealing of the 144,000 (Revelation 7:1-8)

- **Revelation 7:1-3** — Four angels hold the four winds till "we have sealed the servants of our God in their foreheads." The sealing of God's living people is the work that must be finished before the winds of strife are loosed.

  > "The Seal of the Living God. — This is the distinguishing characteristic of the ascending angel; he bears with him the seal of the living God… The nature of his work is evidently embraced in his having the seal of the living God." — DAR 437-446.

- **Revelation 7:4** — "I heard the number of them which were sealed: an hundred and forty and four thousand of all the tribes of the children of Israel." A definite number, drawn from the living, sealed in the last days.

  > "Those whom James here addresses are (1) Christians; for they are his brethren; (2) They are not the converts to Christianity from the Jews, the twelve tribes of his own day; for he addresses them in view of the coming of the Lord… He is thus addressing the last generation of Christians, the Christians of our own day; and he calls them the twelve tribes scattered abroad." — DAR 446.1-2.

- **Revelation 7:5-8** — Twelve thousand sealed "out of" each tribe — Dan and Ephraim omitted, Levi and Joseph put in their place. The enumeration differs from every other in Scripture; not all once written among these tribes endure to the end.

  > "But in the passage before us, Ephraim and Dan are omitted, and Levi and Joseph put in their places. The omission of Dan is accounted for by commentators on the ground that that tribe was the one chiefly addicted to idolatry… Twelve thousand were sealed 'out of' each of the twelve tribes, showing that not all who in the records of heaven had a place among these tribes when this sealing work commenced, stood the test, and were overcomers at last." — DAR 447-448.1.

- **Ezekiel 9:4** — "Set a mark upon the foreheads of the men that sigh and that cry for all the abominations." The Old-Testament type of the sealing — a mark in the forehead distinguishing God's faithful from the apostate mass.

  > "The seal of God, as brought to view in our text, is to be applied to the servants of God. We are not, of course, to suppose that in this case it is some literal mark to be made in the flesh, but that it is some institution or observance having special reference to God, which will serve as a 'mark of distinction' between the worshipers of God and those who are not in truth his servants." — DAR 437-446.

- **Ephesians 4:30** — "Ye are sealed unto the day of redemption." The seal is the impress of the Holy Spirit, set on the character of those who are ready for translation.

  > "The seal of God, as brought to view in our text, is to be applied to the servants of God… some institution or observance having special reference to God, which will serve as a 'mark of distinction' between the worshipers of God and those who are not in truth his servants, though they may profess to follow him." — DAR 437.3.

- **Revelation 22:4** — "His name shall be in their foreheads." The Father's name is His seal; the commandment that bears His descriptive title — the Sabbath — is the seal of His law.

  > "They have the name of the Lamb's Father in their foreheads… we at once perceive that the Father regards his name as his seal. That commandment of the law which contains God's name is therefore the seal of the law. The Sabbath commandment is the only one which has this." — DAR 584.2.

### Spiritual Israel — Who the Sealed Tribes Are (Galatians 3:29)

- **Galatians 3:29** — "If ye be Christ's, then are ye Abraham's seed, and heirs according to the promise." The twelve tribes of Revelation 7 are not the literal Jewish nation but spiritual Israel — believers in Christ of every nation.

  > "Through faith in Christ the wild olive scions, the Gentiles, are grafted into the tame olive stock, and thus the twelve tribes are perpetuated… as belonging to these twelve tribes, will all the redeemed be reckoned, whether on earth they were Jews or Gentiles." — DAR 446.2-447.

- **Galatians 3:7-9** — "They which are of faith, the same are the children of Abraham... blessed with faithful Abraham." Faith, not lineage, makes one a true Israelite and heir of the sealing.

  > "Through faith in Christ the wild olive scions, the Gentiles, are grafted into the tame olive stock, and thus the twelve tribes are perpetuated. And here we find an explanation of the language of the same apostle: 'They are not all Israel which are of Israel,' and 'he is not a Jew which is one outwardly, ... but he is a Jew which is one inwardly.'" — DAR 446.2.

- **Romans 2:28-29** — "He is not a Jew which is one outwardly... but he is a Jew which is one inwardly." Uriah Smith cites this very text to prove the sealed twelve tribes are Christians of the last generation.

  > "And here we find an explanation of the language of the same apostle: 'They are not all Israel which are of Israel,' and 'he is not a Jew which is one outwardly, ... but he is a Jew which is one inwardly.' Romans 9:6-8; 2:28, 29." — DAR 447.

- **James 1:1** — James writes "to the twelve tribes which are scattered abroad," yet addresses them "in view of the coming of the Lord" — therefore the last generation of Christians, called the twelve tribes.

  > "They are not the converts to Christianity from the Jews, the twelve tribes of his own day; for he addresses them in view of the coming of the Lord… He is thus addressing the last generation of Christians, the Christians of our own day; and he calls them the twelve tribes scattered abroad." — DAR 446.2-447.

- **Revelation 21:12** — The gates of the New Jerusalem — a New-Testament city — bear "the names of the twelve tribes of the children of Israel," and through them all the redeemed pass; so all the saved, Jew or Gentile, are reckoned among these tribes.

  > "So we find on the gates of the New Jerusalem — which is a New Testament or Christian, not a Jewish, city — the names of the twelve tribes of the children of Israel… And as through these gates, so inscribed, all the redeemed hosts will go in and out, so, as belonging to these twelve tribes, will all the redeemed be reckoned, whether on earth they were Jews or Gentiles." — DAR 447.

### The 144,000 on Mount Zion — Translated Without Death (Revelation 14:1-5)

- **Revelation 14:1** — "A Lamb stood on the mount Sion, and with him an hundred forty and four thousand, having his Father's name written in their foreheads." The very company hunted by the beast in chapter 13 stands victorious on the heavenly Zion.

  > "The 13th chapter closed with the people of God, a small and apparently weak and defenseless company, in deadly conflict with the mightiest powers of earth… Glance forward with the apostle to the very next scene in the program, and what do we behold? — The very same company standing on Mount Zion with the Lamb, — a victorious company." — DAR 582.2-583.

- **Revelation 14:2-3** — They sing "as it were a new song... and no man could learn that song but the hundred forty and four thousand." It is the song of Moses and the Lamb — the song of their own deliverance, which no other company can learn.

  > "They sing a new song which no other company is able to learn. In chapter 15:3, it is called the song of Moses and the song of the Lamb… Therefore the song of the 144,000, is the song of their deliverance. No others can join in it; for no other company will have had an experience like theirs." — DAR 584.3.

  > "And they sing 'a new song' before the throne, a song which no man can learn save the hundred and forty and four thousand. It is the song of Moses and the Lamb—a song of deliverance… for it is the song of their experience—an experience such as no other company have ever had." — GC 648-649.

- **Revelation 14:4** — "These are they which follow the Lamb whithersoever he goeth. These were redeemed from among men, being the firstfruits unto God and to the Lamb." "Redeemed from among men" — from among the living — means translation; the 144,000 are the living saints changed at the second coming.

  > "They are 'redeemed from among men' (verse 4), an expression which can be applicable only to those who are translated from among the living… A redemption from among men, from among the living, must mean a different thing, and can mean only one thing, and that is translation. Hence the 144,000 are the living saints, who will be translated at the second coming of Christ." — DAR 583.4.

  > "And the Lamb Shall Lead Them. — Another testimony concerning the same company, and applying at the same time, says, 'These are they which follow the Lamb whithersoever he goeth.' Revelation 14:4." — DAR 451.1.

- **Revelation 14:5** — "In their mouth was found no guile: for they are without fault before the throne of God." A character without falsehood, perfected through the final crisis.

  > "VERSE 1… And in their mouth was found no guile: for they are without fault before the throne of God." — DAR 582.2.

  > "'In their mouth was found no guile: for they are without fault' before God. 'Therefore are they before the throne of God, and serve Him day and night in His temple.'" — GC 648.3.

- **Revelation 3:10** — "Because thou hast kept the word of my patience, I also will keep thee from the hour of temptation, which shall come upon all the world." The promise to Philadelphia — the church state to which the 144,000 belong — kept through the last trial.

  > "They are the overcomers in the sixth or Philadelphian state of the church. (See Revelation 3:11, 12.)" — DAR 583.3.

- **Revelation 3:11-12** — "Hold that fast which thou hast, that no man take thy crown... I will write upon him the name of my God, and the name of the city of my God." The overcomers of Philadelphia receive the Father's name written in the forehead — the very mark of the 144,000.

  > "They are the overcomers in the sixth or Philadelphian state of the church. (See Revelation 3:11, 12.)" — DAR 583.3.

- **Daniel 12:1** — "There shall be a time of trouble, such as never was since there was a nation... and at that time thy people shall be delivered." The 144,000 pass through this unequaled trouble, standing without a mediator through the seven last plagues, yet are delivered.

  > "They pass through the great time of trouble such as never was since there was a nation. Daniel 12:1… They stand without a mediator through the terrific scenes of the seven last plagues, those exhibitions of God's unmingled wrath in the earth… They pass through the severest time of trouble the world has ever known, although they are delivered out of it." — DAR 450.1.

  > "'These are they which came out of great tribulation;' they have passed through the time of trouble such as never was since there was a nation… they have stood without an intercessor through the final outpouring of God's judgments. But they have been delivered." — GC 648-649.

- **Revelation 14:12** — "Here are they that keep the commandments of God, and the faith of Jesus." Accused of legalism for refusing the mark, the 144,000 are shown to rest wholly on the merits of the Lamb's blood.

  > "And though the 144,000 are accused of rejecting Christ, and trusting to their own works for salvation, because they refuse to violate the commandments of God (Revelation 14:1, 12), in the great day that calumny will be wiped off. It will be seen that they have rested their hope of life on the merits of the shed blood of their divine Redeemer." — DAR 450.2.

### The Great Multitude — The Redeemed of All Ages (Revelation 7:9-17)

- **Revelation 7:9** — "A great multitude, which no man could number, of all nations, and kindreds, and people, and tongues, stood before the throne... clothed with white robes, and palms in their hands." The countless host of the saved of every age, raised at the second advent — proof the sealing is the last work before translation.

  > "This vast throng are undoubtedly the saved out of every nation, kindred, tribe, and tongue, raised from the dead at the second coming of Christ, showing that the sealing is the last work accomplished for the people of God prior to translation." — DAR 448.2-3.

- **Revelation 7:10-12** — They cry "Salvation to our God which sitteth upon the throne, and unto the Lamb," and all the angels fall and worship. The redeemed ascribe their victory wholly to God and the Lamb.

  > "And cried with a loud voice, saying, Salvation to our God which sitteth upon the throne, and unto the Lamb… And all the angels stood round about the throne, and about the elders and the four beasts, and fell before the throne on their faces, and worshiped God." — DAR 448.2.

- **Revelation 7:13-14** — The elder asks "What are these... and whence came they?" — and John's "Sir, thou knowest" implies he did not know. Smith holds that this question singles out a special company within the throng: the 144,000, so transformed John no longer recognizes them.

  > "But if a special company in this vast throng were referred to, distinguished by some special mark or position, then it might not be so evident who they were… We therefore incline to the view that attention is called to a special company by the questions which were proposed by one of the elders; and no company is brought to view to which special allusion would more naturally be made than to the company spoken of in the first part of the chapter; namely, the 144,000." — DAR 449.2-450.

- **Revelation 7:14** — "These... came out of great tribulation, and have washed their robes, and made them white in the blood of the Lamb." True of all the redeemed, but emphatically of the 144,000, who pass through the great time of trouble.

  > "While it is true in some degree of all Christians that they must 'through much tribulation enter into the kingdom of God,' it is true in a very emphatic sense of the 144,000. They pass through the great time of trouble such as never was since there was a nation… There is peculiar force in saying of these that they have washed their robes, and made them white in the blood of the Lamb." — DAR 450.1-2.

  > "'These are they which came out of great tribulation;' they have passed through the time of trouble such as never was since there was a nation… But they have been delivered, for they have 'washed their robes, and made them white in the blood of the Lamb.'" — GC 648-649.

- **Revelation 7:15-17** — "They shall hunger no more, neither thirst any more; neither shall the sun light on them, nor any heat... God shall wipe away all tears from their eyes." The reward answers to their suffering in the plagues, when the sun scorched men and the rivers turned to blood.

  > "May it not be that when the pastures, with all fruits and vegetation, are dried up… and the rivers and fountains are turned to blood… the saints who pass through that time will be brought occasionally to the extreme degrees of hunger and thirst? But the kingdom once gained, 'they shall hunger no more, neither thirst any more.'… We remember that the 144,000 live through the time when power is given unto the sun 'to scorch men with fire.'" — DAR 450.4-451.

### The Song and the Sea of Glass — The Victors over the Beast (Revelation 15:2-3)

- **Revelation 15:2** — "Them that had gotten the victory over the beast, and over his image, and over his mark... stand on the sea of glass, having the harps of God." The same 144,000, now standing victorious on the sea of glass before the throne.

  > "Upon the crystal sea before the throne… are gathered the company that have 'gotten the victory over the beast, and over his image, and over his mark, and over the number of his name.' With the Lamb upon Mount Zion, 'having the harps of God,' they stand, the hundred and forty and four thousand that were redeemed from among men." — GC 648.3-649.

- **Revelation 15:3** — "They sing the song of Moses... and the song of the Lamb." The song of deliverance — Moses' song was the song of his own experience and rescue (Exodus 15); so this is the song of the saints' deliverance, learnable by none but them.

  > "The song of Moses, as may be seen by reference to Exodus 15, was the song of his experience and deliverance. Therefore the song of the 144,000, is the song of their deliverance. No others can join in it; for no other company will have had an experience like theirs." — DAR 584.3.

  > "It is the song of Moses and the Lamb—a song of deliverance. None but the hundred and forty-four thousand can learn that song; for it is the song of their experience—an experience such as no other company have ever had." — GC 649.

### Their Eternal Reward — God Dwells with Them (Revelation 21:3-4)

- **Revelation 21:3** — "Behold, the tabernacle of God is with men, and he will dwell with them, and they shall be his people." The final union of the redeemed with God — the consummation of the covenant.

  > "Friends long separated by death are united, nevermore to part, and with songs of gladness ascend together to the City of God." — GC 645.

  > "'Therefore are they before the throne of God, and serve Him day and night in His temple: and He that sitteth on the throne shall dwell among them.'" — GC 648-649.

- **Revelation 21:4** — "God shall wipe away all tears from their eyes; and there shall be no more death, neither sorrow, nor crying, neither shall there be any more pain." Smith joins this with Isaiah 25:8 — "He will swallow up death in victory" — as the promise fulfilled to the delivered saints.

  > "The phraseology of this promise to the 144,000 is also partially found in the following glowing prophecy from the pen of Isaiah: 'He will swallow up death in victory; and the Lord God will wipe away tears from off all faces… for the Lord hath spoken it.' Isaiah 25:8." — DAR 451.2.

- **1 Thessalonians 4:16-17** — "The dead in Christ shall rise first: then we which are alive and remain shall be caught up together with them... to meet the Lord in the air." The resurrection of the great multitude and the translation of the living 144,000 in one event at Christ's coming.

  > "The living righteous are changed 'in a moment, in the twinkling of an eye.' At the voice of God they were glorified; now they are made immortal and with the risen saints are caught up to meet their Lord in the air." — GC 645.

  > "The graves opened, and the dead came up clothed with immortality. The 144,000 shouted, 'Alleluia!' as they recognized their friends who had been torn from them by death, and in the same moment we were changed and caught up together with them to meet the Lord in the air." — EW 16.1.

### Witness of the Spirit of Prophecy

- **The sealed company translated.** "The 144,000 were all sealed and perfectly united. On their foreheads was written, God, New Jerusalem, and a glorious star containing Jesus' new name." Sealed, they stand through the last crisis and are changed at the advent.

  > "The 144,000 were all sealed and perfectly united. On their foreheads was written, God, New Jerusalem, and a glorious star containing Jesus' new name… Then it was that the synagogue of Satan knew that God had loved us who could wash one another's feet and salute the brethren with a holy kiss, and they worshiped at our feet." — EW 15.1.

- **Caught up at the resurrection.** "The graves opened, and the dead came up clothed with immortality. The 144,000 shouted, 'Alleluia!'... and in the same moment we were changed and caught up together with them to meet the Lord in the air." The living translated join the risen dead.

  > "The graves opened, and the dead came up clothed with immortality. The 144,000 shouted, 'Alleluia!' as they recognized their friends who had been torn from them by death, and in the same moment we were changed and caught up together with them to meet the Lord in the air." — EW 16.1.

  > "The living righteous are changed 'in a moment, in the twinkling of an eye.' At the voice of God they were glorified; now they are made immortal and with the risen saints are caught up to meet their Lord in the air." — GC 645.1.

- **On the sea of glass in a perfect square.** "Here on the sea of glass the 144,000 stood in a perfect square... Jesus brought the crowns, and with His own right hand placed them on our heads. He gave us harps of gold and palms of victory."

  > "We all entered the cloud together, and were seven days ascending to the sea of glass, when Jesus brought the crowns, and with His own right hand placed them on our heads. He gave us harps of gold and palms of victory. Here on the sea of glass the 144,000 stood in a perfect square." — EW 16.2.

  > "The glittering ranks are drawn up in the form of a hollow square about their King… Upon the heads of the overcomers, Jesus with His own right hand places the crown of glory… In every hand are placed the victor's palm and the shining harp." — GC 645.3.

- **The deliverance at midnight.** "It was at midnight that God chose to deliver His people... there was a mighty shout of victory over the beast and over his image." The 144,000 delivered out of the time of trouble by the voice of God.

  > "It was at midnight that God chose to deliver His people… And when the never-ending blessing was pronounced on those who had honored God in keeping His Sabbath holy, there was a mighty shout of victory over the beast and over his image." — EW 285.

- **The final scene by the great pen.** Upon the sea of glass "are gathered the company that have gotten the victory over the beast... with the Lamb upon Mount Zion... the hundred and forty and four thousand that were redeemed from among men... having been translated from the earth, from among the living... the first fruits unto God and to the Lamb." The whole prophetic chain resolved in one tableau.

  > "Upon the crystal sea before the throne… are gathered the company that have 'gotten the victory over the beast, and over his image, and over his mark, and over the number of his name.' With the Lamb upon Mount Zion, 'having the harps of God,' they stand, the hundred and forty and four thousand that were redeemed from among men… These, having been translated from the earth, from among the living, are counted as 'the first fruits unto God and to the Lamb.'" — GC 648.3-649.

---

# 19. The Woman Who Rides the Beast — Babylon

> _In prophecy a woman is the symbol of a church: a pure woman, the true church of Christ; a corrupt woman, an apostate church. The woman of Revelation 17 is the great apostasy — Papal Rome, "Babylon the Great, the Mother of Harlots" — arrayed in purple and scarlet, holding a golden cup of abominations, drunken with the blood of the saints, and seated upon a scarlet beast (the civil power) that she controls and guides "as a rider controls the animal upon which he is seated." Her seven heads are the seven mountains, or successive forms of government, of Rome; she is "that great city which reigneth over the kings of the earth." From her come the apostate daughters — fallen Protestantism — and against the whole family heaven cries, "Babylon is fallen... come out of her, my people." (DAR 657-663; GC 381-383.)_

### The Symbol-Key: A Woman = A Church (Pure or Corrupt)

- **2 Corinthians 11:2** — Paul has "espoused" the church "to one husband, that I may present you as a chaste virgin to Christ." The faithful church is a chaste woman betrothed to the Lord — the standard against which the harlot is read.

  > "In the Bible the sacred and enduring character of the relation that exists between Christ and His church is represented by the union of marriage.… And Paul employs the same figure in the New Testament when he says: 'I have espoused you to one husband, that I may present you as a chaste virgin to Christ.' 2 Corinthians 11:2." — GC 381.

- **Ephesians 5:23-27** — Christ is "the head of the church," who gave Himself to "present it to himself a glorious church, not having spot, or wrinkle... holy and without blemish." The marriage figure: Christ the husband, the pure church His bride.

  > "In Revelation 17 Babylon is represented as a woman—a figure which is used in the Bible as the symbol of a church, a virtuous woman representing a pure church, a vile woman an apostate church." — GC 381.

- **Jeremiah 31:32** — "I was an husband unto them, saith the LORD." God binds His people to Himself by covenant as a husband to a wife; apostasy is therefore adultery.

  > "The Lord has joined His people to Himself by a solemn covenant, He promising to be their God, and they pledging themselves to be His and His alone.… And, again: 'I am married unto you.' Jeremiah 3:14." — GC 381.

- **Revelation 12:1** — The true church is "a woman clothed with the sun, and the moon under her feet, and upon her head a crown of twelve stars" — pure, radiant, heavenly. The harlot of chapter 17 is her counterfeit.

  > "'A woman,' the true church. A corrupt woman is used to represent an apostate or corrupt church. Ezekiel 23:2-4; Revelation 17:3-6, 15, 18. By parity of reasoning, a pure woman, as in this instance, would represent the true church." — DAR 509.

- **Jeremiah 6:2** — The LORD "likened the daughter of Zion to a comely and delicate woman." Scripture itself supplies the symbol: the people of God pictured as a woman.

  > "In Revelation 17 Babylon is represented as a woman—a figure which is used in the Bible as the symbol of a church, a virtuous woman representing a pure church, a vile woman an apostate church." — GC 381.

### A Corrupt Woman = An Apostate Church; Fornication = Spiritual Idolatry

- **Isaiah 1:21** — "How is the faithful city become an harlot! it was full of judgment; righteousness lodged in it; but now murderers." Departure from God turns the once-pure church into a harlot — the exact transformation pictured in Revelation 17.

  > "The unfaithfulness of the church to Christ in permitting her confidence and affection to be turned from Him, and allowing the love of worldly things to occupy the soul, is likened to the violation of the marriage vow.… 'But thou didst trust in thine own beauty, and playedst the harlot because of thy renown.'" — GC 381-382.

- **Jeremiah 3:6-9** — "Backsliding Israel" went up "upon every high mountain... and there hath played the harlot," and "treacherous" Judah "went and played the harlot also." Unfaithfulness to God is fornication; here are two sister-harlots — mother and daughter apostasy foreshadowed.

  > "'As a wife treacherously departeth from her husband, so have ye dealt treacherously with Me, O house of Israel, saith the Lord;' 'as a wife that committeth adultery, which taketh strangers instead of her husband!' Ezekiel 16:8, 13-15, 32; Jeremiah 3:20." — GC 381.

- **Ezekiel 23:2-4** — "Two women, the daughters of one mother... Aholah the elder, and Aholibah her sister," who "committed whoredoms" — Samaria and Jerusalem. The pattern of a mother-apostasy with harlot daughters; the very framework of Babylon "the mother of harlots."
