  > "The man who thinks that he himself can get righteousness out of the law is in reality trying to substitute his own righteousness for God's righteousness. … 'Where no law is, there is no transgression;' but there is law everywhere, and therefore transgression. All have sinned, so that the inheritance can not be by the law." — WOR 77.

  > "It is not faith that claims the favor of Heaven without complying with the conditions upon which mercy is to be granted… for genuine faith has its foundation in the promises and provisions of the Scriptures." — GC 472.

- **Romans 4:13-16** — "the promise... was not to Abraham, or to his seed, through the law, but through the righteousness of faith... Therefore it is of faith, that it might be by grace; to the end the promise might be sure to all the seed." If the inheritance were by law, faith is void; it is by grace so the promise cannot fail.

  > "'Therefore it is of faith, that it might be by grace; to the end the promise might be sure to all the seed; not to that only which is of the law, but to that also which is of the faith of Abraham, who is the father of us all.'" — WOR 77-78.

  > "The promise to Abraham was a Gospel promise, and nothing else, and the covenant was the everlasting covenant, of which Christ is Mediator. Its scope is identical with that of the new covenant." — EVCO 84.

- **Galatians 3:6-9** — "they which be of faith are blessed with faithful Abraham." Those of faith — Jew or Gentile — are the true children of Abraham and heirs of the same gospel-promise.

  > "God promises us everything that we need, and more than we can ask or think, as a gift. We give Him ourselves, that is, nothing, and He gives us Himself, that is, everything." — GTI 132.

  > "So we see that the making of this everlasting covenant with Abraham was simply the preaching of the everlasting Gospel of the kingdom, and the assuring to him of a part in its blessings." — EVCO 84.

### The Covenant Is in the Seed — Christ, and a Testament in His Blood

- **Galatians 3:16** — "to Abraham and his seed were the promises made... not... seeds, as of many; but as of one... which is Christ." The whole covenant centers in one Seed; all the promises are Yea and Amen in Him.

  > "God's covenants with men can be nothing else than promises to them… God promises us everything that we need, and more than we can ask or think, as a gift." — GTI 132.

  > "The promise to Abraham was a Gospel promise, and nothing else, and the covenant was the everlasting covenant, of which Christ is Mediator." — EVCO 84.

- **Galatians 3:17-18** — "the covenant, that was confirmed before of God in Christ, the law, which was four hundred and thirty years after, cannot disannul." The law given at Sinai could not annul or replace the earlier covenant of promise — proof the everlasting covenant antedates and outranks Sinai.

  > "That the covenant and promise of God are one and the same thing, is clearly seen from Galatians 3:17, where it appears that to disannul the covenant would be to make void the promise." — GTI 132.

  > "It is the one everlasting covenant, which is so often spoken of in the Bible. … The promise to Abraham was a Gospel promise, and nothing else, and the covenant was the everlasting covenant, of which Christ is Mediator." — EVCO 83-84.

- **Genesis 22:18** — "in thy seed shall all the nations of the earth be blessed." The promise reaches every nation through the Seed — the same scope as the everlasting gospel of Revelation 14:6.

  > "The making of this everlasting covenant with Abraham was simply the preaching of the everlasting Gospel of the kingdom… Its scope is identical with that of the new covenant." — EVCO 84.

- **Hebrews 9:15-17** — "he is the mediator of the new testament, that by means of death... they which are called might receive the promise of eternal inheritance... For where a testament is, there must also... be the death of the testator." The new covenant becomes "of force" only by Christ's death — it is sealed in His blood.

  > "It is the one everlasting covenant, which is so often spoken of in the Bible. It is 'through the blood of the everlasting covenant' that men are made perfect in every good work to do the will of God." — EVCO 83-84.

- **Hebrews 9:11-14** — Christ "by his own blood... entered in once into the holy place, having obtained eternal redemption." The blood of the everlasting covenant ministered in the heavenly sanctuary — the gospel foundation under the whole prophetic chain.

  > "It is 'through the blood of the everlasting covenant' that men are made perfect in every good work to do the will of God. … The promise to Abraham was a Gospel promise, and nothing else, and the covenant was the everlasting covenant, of which Christ is Mediator." — EVCO 83-84.

### The Law Established by Faith, Not Made Void

- **Galatians 3:10-13** — "Christ hath redeemed us from the curse of the law, being made a curse for us." The curse falls only on those under the old covenant's "works of the law"; Christ bears it so the blessing of Abraham reaches the Gentiles.

  > "The law worketh wrath: for by the law is the knowledge of sin. … Their making it was simply a promise to make themselves righteous by their own works, and man in himself is 'without strength.'" — GTI 184.

  > "The law reveals to man his sins, but it provides no remedy. While it promises life to the obedient, it declares that death is the portion of the transgressor. The gospel of Christ alone can free him from the condemnation or the defilement of sin." — GC 467-468.

- **Galatians 3:19, 24** — the law "was added because of transgressions, till the seed should come"; it is "our schoolmaster to bring us unto Christ, that we might be justified by faith." The law drives the sinner to the promise — it never was a rival way of salvation.

  > "But Christ is the only Light of the world, the Light of life; so that the true and living law is found only in Him. … Of this, the law on tables was but the shadow, albeit an exact and perfect shadow. It tells us exactly what we shall find in Christ." — EVCO 344.

  > "The first step in reconciliation to God is the conviction of sin. … In order to see his guilt, the sinner must test his character by God's great standard of righteousness. It is a mirror which shows the perfection of a righteous character." — GC 467.

- **Galatians 3:21** — "Is the law then against the promises of God? God forbid." Law and gospel never conflict; the law cannot give life, so life must come by the promise.

  > "There are two laws just as there are two covenants, occupying the same relation to each other that the two covenants do to each other. One is the shadow of the other, the result of placing the veil of unbelief before the light of life." — EVCO 344.

- **Romans 3:31** — "Do we then make void the law through faith? God forbid: yea, we establish the law." Faith does not abolish the law but writes it in the heart — the new covenant keeps the very commandments of God.

  > "Says Paul: 'Do we then make void the law through faith? God forbid: yea, we establish the law.' … In the new birth the heart is brought into harmony with God, as it is brought into accord with His law." — GC 468.

- **Galatians 3:26-29** — "ye are all the children of God by faith... if ye be Christ's, then are ye Abraham's seed, and heirs according to the promise." All barriers fall in Christ; faith makes us heirs of the everlasting covenant.

  > "The two covenants are not matters of time, but of condition. … The difference is just the difference between a freewoman and a slave… while those of Sarah would necessarily be free." — GTI 187.

  > "The promise to Abraham was a Gospel promise, and nothing else, and the covenant was the everlasting covenant, of which Christ is Mediator." — EVCO 84.

### The Everlasting Gospel — the Faith of the Remnant

- **Revelation 14:6** — "another angel... having the everlasting gospel to preach unto them that dwell on the earth." The first angel proclaims the same everlasting gospel preached to Abraham — righteousness by faith in the everlasting covenant — to every nation before the end.

  > "So we see that the making of this everlasting covenant with Abraham was simply the preaching of the everlasting Gospel of the kingdom… Its scope is identical with that of the new covenant." — EVCO 84.

  > "The law reveals to man his sins, but it provides no remedy. … The gospel of Christ alone can free him from the condemnation or the defilement of sin. … It is only as the law of God is restored to its rightful position that there can be a revival of primitive faith and godliness among His professed people." — GC 467-478.

- **Revelation 14:12** — "Here are they that keep the commandments of God, and the faith of Jesus." The remnant unites the law written in the heart (new covenant) with the faith of Jesus — the living proof that faith establishes the law.

  > "Says Paul: 'Do we then make void the law through faith? God forbid: yea, we establish the law.' … When this mighty change has taken place in the sinner, he has passed from death unto life, from sin unto holiness, from transgression and rebellion to obedience and loyalty." — GC 468.

  > "We may go to Jesus and be cleansed, and stand before the law without shame and remorse. 'There is therefore now no condemnation to them which are in Christ Jesus, who walk not after the flesh, but after the Spirit.'" — GC 477.

  > "But Christ is the only Light of the world, the Light of life; so that the true and living law is found only in Him. It is His life, because it is in His heart." — EVCO 344.

- **Hebrews 8:10 with Revelation 22:14** — the law written within (new covenant) produces those who "do his commandments, that they may have right to the tree of life." The covenant promise reaches its consummation in the restored Eden inheritance promised to Abraham.

  > "It is the one everlasting covenant, which is so often spoken of in the Bible. … Mark well that Abraham himself, as well as his seed, was promised the land for an everlasting possession." — EVCO 83-84.

  > "Here are revealed the heights of attainment that we may reach through faith in the promises of our heavenly Father, when we fulfill His requirements. Through the merits of Christ we have access to the throne of Infinite Power." — GC 477.

- **Galatians 4:30-31** — "Cast out the bondwoman and her son... we are not children of the bondwoman, but of the free." The final separation: those who live by their own promise (Babylon's bondage) are cast out; the free children of the promise stand. This is the experience of righteousness by faith that prepares the remnant for translation.

  > "So then the two covenants existed in every essential particular in the days of Abraham. Even so they do to-day; for the Scripture says now as well as then, 'Cast out the bondwoman and her son.' We see then that the two covenants are not matters of time, but of condition." — GTI 187.

  > "It is not faith that claims the favor of Heaven without complying with the conditions upon which mercy is to be granted, it is presumption… Such are the fruits of Bible conversion and sanctification… It is only as the law of God is restored to its rightful position that there can be a revival of primitive faith and godliness among His professed people." — GC 472-478.

---

# 23. The State of the Dead and the Last Deception (Spiritualism)

> _The truth about death is the bulwark against the final deception. The dead sleep an unconscious sleep, knowing nothing, awaiting the call of the resurrection morning; there is no immortal soul that survives the body, for God alone hath immortality. This one truth shuts the door on spiritualism — Satan's masterpiece, by which he personates the dead, counterfeits departed friends, and works lying miracles to deceive the world. The first lie, "Ye shall not surely die," laid the foundation of spiritualism, and at the end binds Romanism, apostate Protestantism, and spiritualism into the threefold union of Revelation 16:13-14. "To the law and to the testimony" is the only test. (GC 531-562; GC 588; DAR 646-648; EW 262-263.)_

### The dead sleep — they know nothing

- **Ecclesiastes 9:5-6** — "The living know that they shall die: but the dead know not any thing." Their love, hatred, and envy have perished; they have no more portion under the sun. The plainest statement of Scripture flatly contradicts the doctrine of conscious survival.

  > "What say the Scriptures concerning these things? David declares that man is not conscious in death. … Solomon bears the same testimony: 'The living know that they shall die: but the dead know not anything.' 'Their love, and their hatred, and their envy, is now perished; neither have they any more a portion forever in anything that is done under the sun.'" — GC 545.

  > "The doctrine of natural immortality, first borrowed from the pagan philosophy, and in the darkness of the great apostasy incorporated into the Christian faith, has supplanted the truth, so plainly taught in Scripture, that 'the dead know not anything.'" — GC 551.

- **Ecclesiastes 9:10** — "There is no work, nor device, nor knowledge, nor wisdom, in the grave, whither thou goest." The grave is a place of silence and inactivity, not of conscious bliss or torment.

  > "'There is no work, nor device, nor knowledge, nor wisdom, in the grave, whither thou goest.' Ecclesiastes 9:5, 6, 10." — GC 545.

- **Psalm 146:3-4** — "His breath goeth forth, he returneth to his earth; in that very day his thoughts perish." David declares man is not conscious in death — the very text Sister White cites to overthrow the immortality error.

  > "David declares that man is not conscious in death. 'His breath goeth forth, he returneth to his earth; in that very day his thoughts perish.' Psalm 146:4." — GC 545.3.

- **Psalm 115:17** — "The dead praise not the LORD, neither any that go down into silence." The dead are silent; they are not in heaven praising God.

  > "The Bible clearly teaches that the dead do not go immediately to heaven. They are represented as sleeping until the resurrection. … They that go down to the grave are in silence. They know no more of anything that is done under the sun." — GC 549-550.

- **Psalm 6:5** — "In death there is no remembrance of thee: in the grave who shall give thee thanks?" There is no remembrance, no consciousness, in death.

  > "In the very day when the silver cord is loosed and the golden bowl broken (Ecclesiastes 12:6), man's thoughts perish. They that go down to the grave are in silence. They know no more of anything that is done under the sun." — GC 549-550.

- **Job 14:21** — "His sons come to honour, and he knoweth it not... but he perceiveth it not of them." The dead do not know what passes among the living — therefore they cannot return to "minister" to them.

  > "The doctrine of man's consciousness in death, especially the belief that spirits of the dead return to minister to the living, has prepared the way for modern spiritualism. … The fallen angels who do his bidding appear as messengers from the spirit world." — GC 551-552.

- **Job 7:9-10** — "He that goeth down to the grave shall come up no more. He shall return no more to his house." The dead do not haunt the living or revisit their homes; any "spirit" claiming to do so is a deceiver.

  > "The Bible declares that the dead know not anything, that their thoughts have perished; they have no part in anything that is done under the sun; they know nothing of the joys or sorrows of those who were dearest to them on earth." — GC 556.

### Man is mortal — God alone hath immortality

- **Genesis 2:7** — "Man became a living soul." Man does not _have_ a soul; the union of body and breath _is_ the living soul. The soul is not a separate immortal entity.

  > "Immortality, promised to man on condition of obedience, had been forfeited by transgression. Adam could not transmit to his posterity that which he did not possess; and there could have been no hope for the fallen race had not God, by the sacrifice of His Son, brought immortality within their reach." — GC 533.

- **Ecclesiastes 12:7** — "The dust return to the earth as it was: and the spirit shall return unto God who gave it." At death the breath (spirit) of life returns to God; the man returns to dust — he does not float on as a conscious ghost.

  > "And only through Christ can immortality be obtained. … 'He that believeth on the Son hath everlasting life: and he that believeth not the Son shall not see life.' John 3:36." — GC 533.

- **1 Timothy 6:15-16** — God "only hath immortality." Immortality belongs to God alone; man does not possess it inherently.

  > "While 'death passed upon all men, for that all have sinned,' Christ 'hath brought life and immortality to light through the gospel.' Romans 5:12; 2 Timothy 1:10. And only through Christ can immortality be obtained." — GC 533.

  > "Upon the fundamental error of natural immortality rests the doctrine of consciousness in death—a doctrine, like eternal torment, opposed to the teachings of the Scriptures, to the dictates of reason, and to our feelings of humanity." — GC 545.2.

- **Genesis 3:24** — Cherubim and a flaming sword kept the way of the tree of life, that fallen man should not "live forever" in sin. Immortality, forfeited by transgression, is bestowed only through Christ.

  > "Had man after his fall been allowed free access to the tree of life, he would have lived forever, and thus sin would have been immortalized. But cherubim and a flaming sword kept 'the way of the tree of life' (Genesis 3:24)… Therefore there is not an immortal sinner." — GC 533.3.

### Death is a sleep — Christ taught it so

- **John 11:11-14** — "Our friend Lazarus sleepeth... Lazarus is dead." Christ called death a sleep; the disciples thought He spoke of rest in sleep, but He meant Lazarus had died. Death is sleep, not conscious life.

  > "They sleep; they are awakened by the trump of God to a glorious immortality. … As they are called forth from their deep slumber they begin to think just where they ceased. The last sensation was the pang of death; the last thought, that they were falling beneath the power of the grave." — GC 549-550.

- **John 11:43-44** — "Lazarus, come forth." Lazarus, four days dead, was called _back_ — not down from heaven nor up from torment. Had he been in bliss, Christ would have done him no kindness recalling him.

  > "Nowhere in the Sacred Scriptures is found the statement that the righteous go to their reward or the wicked to their punishment at death. … The Bible clearly teaches that the dead do not go immediately to heaven. They are represented as sleeping until the resurrection." — GC 549-550.

- **Job 14:12** — "Man lieth down, and riseth not: till the heavens be no more, they shall not awake, nor be raised out of their sleep." The dead sleep until the final waking — the resurrection, not death, restores them.

  > "Blessed rest for the weary righteous! Time, be it long or short, is but a moment to them. They sleep; they are awakened by the trump of God to a glorious immortality." — GC 549.

- **Acts 2:29** — "David... is both dead and buried, and his sepulchre is with us unto this day." Peter testifies that even David, a man after God's own heart, lay in his tomb.

  > "Nowhere in the Sacred Scriptures is found the statement that the righteous go to their reward or the wicked to their punishment at death. The patriarchs and prophets have left no such assurance. Christ and His apostles have given no hint of it." — GC 545-549.

- **Acts 2:34** — "David is not ascended into the heavens." The plainest apostolic word: the righteous dead have not yet gone to heaven; they await the resurrection.

  > "The Bible clearly teaches that the dead do not go immediately to heaven. They are represented as sleeping until the resurrection. 1 Thessalonians 4:14; Job 14:10-12." — GC 549-550.

### The hope of the sleeping saints — the resurrection

- **1 Thessalonians 4:13-17** — "Them which sleep in Jesus will God bring with him... the dead in Christ shall rise first." The sleeping saints are awakened at the descent of the Lord with a shout and the trump of God; only then are they caught up — proof they were not previously in heaven.

  > "They sleep; they are awakened by the trump of God to a glorious immortality. 'For the trumpet shall sound, and the dead shall be raised incorruptible.…' 1 Corinthians 15:52-54." — GC 549-550.

- **1 Corinthians 15:51-54** — "We shall all be changed, in a moment... at the last trump... the dead shall be raised incorruptible." Mortals put on immortality _at the resurrection_, not at death — "then shall be brought to pass... Death is swallowed up in victory."

  > "'For the trumpet shall sound, and the dead shall be raised incorruptible.… So when this corruptible shall have put on incorruption, and this mortal shall have put on immortality, then shall be brought to pass the saying that is written, Death is swallowed up in victory.'" — GC 549-550.

- **John 5:28-29** — "All that are in the graves shall hear his voice, and shall come forth." The dead are _in their graves_ awaiting His voice — the resurrection of life and the resurrection of damnation.

  > "The patriarchs and prophets have left no such assurance. Christ and His apostles have given no hint of it. The Bible clearly teaches that the dead do not go immediately to heaven. They are represented as sleeping until the resurrection." — GC 545-549.

- **Daniel 12:2** — "Many of them that sleep in the dust of the earth shall awake." The dead sleep in the dust until they awake — some to everlasting life, some to shame and contempt.

  > "The Bible clearly teaches that the dead do not go immediately to heaven. They are represented as sleeping until the resurrection. … They sleep; they are awakened by the trump of God to a glorious immortality." — GC 545-549.

- **Job 19:25-26** — "I know that my redeemer liveth... in my flesh shall I see God." Job's hope is not a disembodied soul but the redeemer standing at the latter day and flesh restored — resurrection hope.

  > "When they arise from the tomb, their first glad thought will be echoed in the triumphal shout: 'O death, where is thy sting? O grave, where is thy victory?'" — GC 549-550.

### The first lie — foundation of spiritualism

- **Genesis 3:4** — "Ye shall not surely die." The serpent's contradiction of God was "the first sermon ever preached upon the immortality of the soul," and the foundation of all spiritualism was laid in that assurance given to Eve in Eden.

  > "The only one who promised Adam life in disobedience was the great deceiver. And the declaration of the serpent to Eve in Eden—'Ye shall not surely die'—was the first sermon ever preached upon the immortality of the soul." — GC 533.2.

  > "Satan has long been preparing for his final effort to deceive the world. The foundation of his work was laid by the assurance given to Eve in Eden: 'Ye shall not surely die.' … Little by little he has prepared the way for his masterpiece of deception in the development of spiritualism." — GC 561.2.

- **1 Timothy 4:1** — "In the latter times some shall depart from the faith, giving heed to seducing spirits, and doctrines of devils." Paul foretells a last-day apostasy under demon teaching — the very spiritualism now spreading.

  > "Furthermore, God has expressly forbidden all pretended communication with departed spirits. … But the 'familiar spirits,' as these visitants from other worlds were called, are declared by the Bible to be 'the spirits of devils.'" — GC 556.

  > "I saw that the saints must have a thorough understanding of present truth, which they will be obliged to maintain from the Scriptures. They must understand the state of the dead; for the spirits of devils will yet appear to them, professing to be beloved relatives or friends, who will declare to them unscriptural doctrines." — EW 262.2.

- **The Roman dunghill** — The immortality of the soul is a false doctrine Rome borrowed from paganism and incorporated into Christendom; Martin Luther classed it among the "monstrous fables" of the Roman decretals.

  > "The theory of the immortality of the soul was one of those false doctrines that Rome, borrowing from paganism, incorporated into the religion of Christendom. Martin Luther classed it with the 'monstrous fables that form part of the Roman dunghill of decretals.'" — GC 549.2.

### Satan personates the dead — the masterpiece of deception

- **2 Corinthians 11:14** — "Satan himself is transformed into an angel of light." The arch-deceiver disguises himself; "Satan is converted, after the modern order of things," appearing as an angel of light through spiritualism.

  > "As spiritualism more closely imitates the nominal Christianity of the day, it has greater power to deceive and ensnare. Satan himself is converted, after the modern order of things. He will appear in the character of an angel of light." — GC 588.2.

  > "The rapping delusion was presented before me, and I saw that Satan has power to bring before us the appearance of forms purporting to be our relatives or friends who sleep in Jesus. … All this is to deceive the world and ensnare them into the belief of this delusion." — EW 262.1.

- **Matthew 24:24** — "False Christs, and false prophets... shall shew great signs and wonders; insomuch that, if it were possible, they shall deceive the very elect." Satan's spirits personating "beloved relatives or friends" will work miracles to sustain their pretensions.

  > "These persons overlook the testimony of the Scriptures concerning the wonders wrought by Satan and his agents. … 'The coming of the Lord is to be preceded by the working of Satan with all power and signs and lying wonders.' … Men are deceived by the miracles which Satan's agents have power to do, not which they pretend to do." — GC 553.2.

  > "Many will be confronted by the spirits of devils personating beloved relatives or friends and declaring the most dangerous heresies. These visitants will appeal to our tenderest sympathies and will work miracles to sustain their pretensions." — GC 560.1.

- **Revelation 13:13-14** — The two-horned beast "doeth great wonders... and deceiveth them that dwell on the earth by the means of those miracles." Lying wonders, not truth, are the engine of the final deception.

  > "But this prophecy is not fulfilled in the great advancement in knowledge… for the wonders to which the prophet had reference are evidently wrought for the purpose of deceiving the people, as we read in verse 14: 'And deceiveth them that dwell on the earth by the means of those miracles which he had power to do in the sight of the beast.'" — DAR 542.

  > "Many endeavor to account for spiritual manifestations by attributing them wholly to fraud and sleight of hand on the part of the medium. … there have been, also, marked exhibitions of supernatural power. … was the direct work of evil angels, who thus introduced one of the most successful of soul-destroying delusions." — GC 553.

- **Counterfeit of the dead** — "He has power to bring before men the appearance of their departed friends. The counterfeit is perfect; the familiar look, the words, the tone, are reproduced with marvelous distinctness." Knowing the dead are _asleep and unconscious_ is the only safeguard.

  > "He has power to bring before men the appearance of their departed friends. The counterfeit is perfect; the familiar look, the words, the tone, are reproduced with marvelous distinctness. … they give ear 'to seducing spirits, and doctrines of devils.'" — GC 552.1.

  > "I saw that Satan has power to bring before us the appearance of forms purporting to be our relatives or friends who sleep in Jesus. It will be made to appear as if these friends were actually present… and the same tone of voice that they had while living will fall upon the ear." — EW 262.1.

### "Regard not them that have familiar spirits"

- **Leviticus 19:31** — "Regard not them that have familiar spirits, neither seek after wizards." God expressly forbids all pretended communion with the dead — these "familiar spirits" are agents of Satan.

  > "Furthermore, God has expressly forbidden all pretended communication with departed spirits. … the 'familiar spirits' … are declared by the Bible to be 'the spirits of devils.' … The work of dealing with familiar spirits was pronounced an abomination to the Lord, and was solemnly forbidden under penalty of death. Leviticus 19:31; 20:27." — GC 556.2.

- **Isaiah 8:19** — "Should not a people seek unto their God? for the living to the dead?" To consult the dead on behalf of the living is folly and rebellion; the living should seek the living God.

  > "But none need be deceived by the lying claims of spiritualism. God has given the world sufficient light to enable them to discover the snare. As already shown, the theory which forms the very foundation of spiritualism is at war with the plainest statements of Scripture." — GC 556.

- **Isaiah 8:20** — "To the law and to the testimony: if they speak not according to this word, it is because there is no light in them." This is THE test. Every spirit, vision, and wonder is to be measured by the written Word; what contradicts the law of God is darkness.

  > "The Holy Scriptures are to be accepted as an authoritative, infallible revelation of His will. They are the standard of character, the revealer of doctrines, and the test of experience." — GC vii.

  > "'To the law and to the testimony.' While conflicting doctrines and theories abound, the law of God is the one unerring rule by which all opinions, doctrines, and theories are to be tested. Says the prophet: 'If they speak not according to this word, it is because there is no light in them.'" — GC 452.2.

### The threefold union and the lying wonders of the last days

- **Revelation 16:13** — "Three unclean spirits like frogs come out of the mouth of the dragon, and out of the mouth of the beast, and out of the mouth of the false prophet." Spiritualism (dragon), the Papacy (beast), and apostate Protestantism (false prophet) unite in one threefold confederacy against God's people.

  > "The sources from which these spirits issue, denote that they will work among three great religious divisions of mankind, represented by the dragon, the beast, and the false prophet, or Paganism, Catholicism, and Protestantism." — DAR 650.2.

  > "The Protestants of the United States will be foremost in stretching their hands across the gulf to grasp the hand of spiritualism; they will reach over the abyss to clasp hands with the Roman power; and under the influence of this threefold union, this country will follow in the steps of Rome in trampling on the rights of conscience." — GC 588.

- **Revelation 16:14** — "They are the spirits of devils, working miracles... to gather them to the battle of that great day of God Almighty." These working-miracle demons go forth to the kings of the earth; the supernatural fires this last alliance.

  > "But it is one province of these spirits of devils to deceive, for they go forth working miracles, and thereby deceive the kings of the earth, that they should believe a lie." — DAR 650.1.

  > "Says the prophet: 'I saw three unclean spirits like frogs; ... they are the spirits of devils, working miracles, which go forth unto the kings of the earth and of the whole world, to gather them to the battle of that great day of God Almighty.' … Except those who are kept by the power of God, through faith in His word, the whole world will be swept into the ranks of this delusion." — GC 561.

- **Two errors bind the union** — "Through the two great errors, the immortality of the soul and Sunday sacredness, Satan will bring the people under his deceptions." The former lays the foundation of spiritualism; the latter creates the bond of sympathy with Rome.

  > "Through the two great errors, the immortality of the soul and Sunday sacredness, Satan will bring the people under his deceptions. While the former lays the foundation of spiritualism, the latter creates a bond of sympathy with Rome." — GC 588.1.

- **Romanism + Protestantism + spiritualism** — Papists and Protestants and spiritualists alike will receive the form of godliness without the power, and Satan determines to sweep all into the ranks of spiritualism — the grand uniting of the three.

  > "Satan determines to unite them in one body and thus strengthen his cause by sweeping all into the ranks of spiritualism. … Papists, Protestants, and worldlings will alike accept the form of godliness without the power, and they will see in this union a grand movement for the conversion of the world." — GC 588.3.

- **Revelation 18:23** — "By thy sorceries were all nations deceived." Babylon's fall is bound up with her sorceries — the spiritualistic enchantments by which she deceives the nations at the end.

  > "Little by little he has prepared the way for his masterpiece of deception in the development of spiritualism. … Except those who are kept by the power of God, through faith in His word, the whole world will be swept into the ranks of this delusion. The people are fast being lulled to a fatal security." — GC 561.

  > "And I saw three unclean spirits like frogs come out of the mouth of the dragon, and out of the mouth of the beast, and out of the mouth of the false prophet. … For they are the spirits of devils, working miracles, which go forth unto the kings of the earth and of the whole world." — DAR 646.

### The conflict just before us

- **Revelation 1:18** — "I am he that liveth, and was dead... and have the keys of hell and of death." Christ alone holds the keys of death and the grave; resurrection is His prerogative, not the dead's natural estate — our anchor against every counterfeit.

  > "They sleep; they are awakened by the trump of God to a glorious immortality. 'For the trumpet shall sound, and the dead shall be raised incorruptible.…' … When they arise from the tomb, their first glad thought will be echoed in the triumphal shout: 'O death, where is thy sting? O grave, where is thy victory?'" — GC 549-550.

- **The saints must know the truth of the dead** — "The saints must have a thorough understanding of present truth... They must understand the state of the dead; for the spirits of devils will yet appear to them, professing to be beloved relatives or friends." This delusion will be met face to face; only the prepared will stand.

  > "I saw that the saints must have a thorough understanding of present truth, which they will be obliged to maintain from the Scriptures. They must understand the state of the dead… The people of God must be prepared to withstand these spirits with the Bible truth that the dead know not anything, and that they who thus appear are the spirits of devils. … This delusion will spread, and we shall have to contend with it face to face." — EW 262.2-262.3.

- **The train of cars** — The rapidity of the delusion was shown as "a train of cars going with the speed of lightning... the whole world was on board," with Satan as the stately conductor; only a little company on the narrow path, "firmly united by the truth," escapes.

  > "I saw the rapidity with which this delusion was spreading. A train of cars was shown me, going with the speed of lightning. … It seemed that the whole world was on board. Then he showed me the conductor, a fair, stately person… He said, 'It is Satan. He is the conductor, in the form of an angel of light.'" — EW 263.1-263.2.

  > "I saw a little company traveling a narrow pathway. All seemed to be firmly united by the truth. This little company looked careworn, as if they had passed through severe trials and conflicts. … causing them to look triumphant as if their victories were nearly won." — EW 263.1-263.2.

- **Stand on the Word alone** — "All whose faith is not firmly established upon the word of God will be deceived and overcome." Against the lying wonders of the last conflict, the Bible — and the Bible only — is the safeguard of God's people.

  > "Just before us is 'the hour of temptation, which shall come upon all the world, to try them that dwell upon the earth.' … All whose faith is not firmly established upon the word of God will be deceived and overcome." — GC 560.2.

  > "Except those who are kept by the power of God, through faith in His word, the whole world will be swept into the ranks of this delusion. The people are fast being lulled to a fatal security, to be awakened only by the outpouring of the wrath of God." — GC 561.

---

# 24. Comparing the Sevens — Churches, Seals, Trumpets, Plagues

> _The book of Revelation runs four "sevens" over the very same stretch of
> history, each viewing it from a different watch-tower. The seven_ **churches**
> _give the church's inward, religious state; the seven_ **seals** _her outward
> providential fortunes; the seven_ **trumpets** _the political and warlike
> judgments on the nations among whom she dwells; and the seven_ **plagues**
> _the final, unmingled wrath poured out on the wicked AFTER probation has
> forever closed. Three of the lines run together through the gospel
> dispensation; the fourth begins only when mercy has ceased to plead. (DAR
> 345, 402, 452, 638-642; GC 627-629.)_

### The four lines at a glance

| Series             | Standpoint           | Subject viewed                     | Reaches                                    | Key text          |
| ------------------ | -------------------- | ---------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------ | ----------------- |
| **Seven Churches** | Religious            | Spiritual state of the church      | Ephesus (apostolic) → Laodicea (last days) | Rev. 1:11, 20     |
| **Seven Seals**    | Providential         | Outward fortunes of the church     | First century → second coming              | Rev. 6:1-2, 12-14 |
| **Seven Trumpets** | Political / military | Wars and judgments on the nations  | Rome's fall → 1840 → end                   | Rev. 8:2; 9:15    |
| **Seven Plagues**  | Final wrath          | The wicked, after probation closes | Wholly future, "without mixture"           | Rev. 15:1; 16:1-2 |

### Series 1 — The Seven CHURCHES: the religious standpoint

- **Revelation 1:11** — "What thou seest, write in a book, and send it unto the seven churches... Ephesus... Smyrna... Pergamos... Thyatira... Sardis... Philadelphia... Laodicea." Seven literal Asian congregations chosen because their names and conditions sketch seven successive states of the whole church to the end.

  > "Having, in the first chapter, mapped out the subject by a general reference to the seven churches, represented by the seven candlesticks, and to the ministry of the churches, represented by the seven stars, John now takes up each church particularly, and writes the message designed for it, addressing the epistle in every case to the angel, or pastors, of the church." — DAR 345.1.

  > "Under this emblematical representation of the seven churches of Asia, the Holy Spirit has delineated seven different states of the Christian church, which would appear in succession, extending to the coming of our Lord and the consummation of all things; that this is given in descriptions taken from the names, states, and conditions of these churches…" — DAR 346.3.

- **Revelation 1:20** — "The seven stars are the angels of the seven churches: and the seven candlesticks... are the seven churches." The candlestick is the church; the star (angel) is her ministry — Scripture interprets its own symbols.

  > "Having, in the first chapter, mapped out the subject by a general reference to the seven churches, represented by the seven candlesticks, and to the ministry of the churches, represented by the seven stars, John now takes up each church particularly…" — DAR 345.1.

  > "The angel of a church must denote a messenger, or minister, of that church; and as these churches each cover a period of time, the angel of each church must denote the ministry, or all the true ministers of Christ during the period covered by that church." — DAR 347.2.

- **Revelation 2:1-5** — Ephesus, "the apostolic age," had received the doctrine of Christ in its purity, yet "left thy first love." This is the church's _inner_ condition, not her outward battles.

  > "The first church named is Ephesus. According to the application here made, this would cover the first, or apostolic age of the church… Those early Christians had received the doctrine of Christ in its purity. They enjoyed the benefits and blessings of the gifts of the Holy Spirit. They were noted for works, labor, and patience." — DAR 347.1.

  > "The Cause of Complaint. — 'I have somewhat against thee,' says Christ, 'because thou hast left thy first love.' … The charge here is not that of falling from grace, nor that love is extinguished, but diminished. No zeal, no suffering can atone for the want of first love." — DAR 347.3.

- **Revelation 1:19** — "Write the things which thou hast seen, and the things which are, and the things which shall be hereafter." The threefold division that frames the whole prophecy as successive periods, not a single moment.

  > "Under this emblematical representation of the seven churches of Asia, the Holy Spirit has delineated seven different states of the Christian church, which would appear in succession, extending to the coming of our Lord and the consummation of all things." — DAR 346.3.

> **Bullet — the Churches' standpoint.** The seven churches give the spiritual,
> religious history of the church — her love, zeal, doctrine, and decline —
> from apostolic Ephesus to lukewarm Laodicea. Many expositors (Vitringa,
> Matthew Henry) held that the Spirit here "delineated seven different states
> of the Christian church, which would appear in succession, extending to the
> coming of our Lord." _(DAR 346.3; 347.1.)_

### Series 2 — The Seven SEALS: the providential standpoint

- **Revelation 6:1-2** — The Lamb opens the first seal; "behold a white horse... he went forth conquering, and to conquer." The white horse is "a fit emblem of the triumphs of the gospel in the first century," its whiteness the purity of faith — the church's outward fortunes, not her trumpet-wars.

  > "VERSE 1. And I saw when the Lamb opened one of the seals… And I saw, and behold a white horse; and he that sat on him had a bow; and a crown was given unto him: and he went forth conquering, and to conquer." — DAR 402.1.

  > "The first symbol, a white horse, and the rider who bears a bow, and to whom a crown is given, and who goes forth conquering and to conquer, is a fit emblem of the triumphs of the gospel in the first century of this dispensation. The whiteness of the horse denotes the purity of faith in that age." — DAR 403.1.

- **Revelation 6:8** — The pale horse: "his name that sat on him was Death... to kill with sword, and with hunger, and with death." The fortunes of the church darken through the centuries of decline and persecution.

  > "The rider on this horse is named Death; and Hell (ᾅδης, the grave) follows with him. The mortality is so great during this period that it would seem as if 'the pale nations of the dead' had come upon earth… It must refer to the time in which the papacy bore its unrebuked, unrestrained, and persecuting rule, commencing about A. D. 538." — DAR 408.2.

- **Revelation 6:9-11** — The fifth seal: souls "under the altar... slain for the word of God... How long, O Lord, dost thou not judge and avenge our blood?" The era of papal martyrdom; white robes given, and a command to "rest yet for a little season" — the church's persecuted providential lot.

  > "The seals denote events of a religious character, and contain the history of the church from the opening of the Christian era to the coming of Christ." — DAR 402.2.

  > "Then their praises were sung, their virtues admired, their fortitude applauded, their names honored, and their memories cherished. White robes were thus given unto every one of them." — DAR 413.

- **Revelation 6:12-14** — The sixth seal: "a great earthquake; and the sun became black... the moon became as blood... the stars of heaven fell." The Lisbon earthquake (1755), the Dark Day (1780), the falling stars (1833) — public providences marking the approach of the end. We "stand between the 13th and 14th verses."

  > "VERSE 1. And I saw when the Lamb opened one of the seals… And I saw, and behold a white horse; and he that sat on him had a bow." — DAR 402.1.

  > "From looking at the past, and beholding the word of God fulfilled, we are now called to look at events in the future, which are no less sure to come. Here is our position, unmistakably defined. We stand between the 13th and 14th verses of this chapter." — DAR 432.2.

- **Revelation 8:1** — The seventh seal: "silence in heaven about the space of half an hour." When heaven empties at Christ's coming; "the world's dream of carnal security" is broken. The seals run from the first century to the second advent.

  > "Isaiah (24: 19, 20) says: 'The earth is utterly broken down, the earth is clean dissolved… The earth shall reel to and fro like a drunkard, and shall be removed like a cottage; and the transgression thereof shall be heavy upon it; and it shall fall, and not rise again.'" — DAR 432.4.

  > "Then will the world's dream of carnal security be effectually broken. Kings, who, intoxicated with their own earthly authority, have never dreamed of a higher power than themselves, now realize that there is One who reigns King of kings." — DAR 433.1.

> **Bullet — the Seals' standpoint.** The seven seals embrace the outward,
> providential history of the same church — her conquests, her decline, her
> martyrdoms, and the signs preceding her Lord — "reaching down to the close of
> probationary time." Where the churches show the heart, the seals show the
> fortunes. _(DAR 402.2; 403.1.)_

### Series 3 — The Seven TRUMPETS: the political standpoint

- **Revelation 8:2** — "I saw the seven angels which stood before God; and to them were given seven trumpets." A new and distinct series. "In the seals we have had the history of the church... In the seven trumpets... we have the principal political and warlike events which were to transpire during the same time."

  > "VERSE 2. And I saw the seven angels which stood before God; and to them were given seven trumpets." — DAR 453.2.

  > "This verse introduces a new and distinct series of events. In the seals we have had the history of the church during what is called the gospel dispensation. In the seven trumpets, now introduced, we have the principal political and warlike events which were to transpire during the same time." — DAR 453.3.

- **Revelation 8:7** — The first trumpet: "hail and fire mingled with blood... the third part of trees was burnt up." The Gothic invasions that broke Western Rome — judgments on the _nations_, not on the church's spiritual state.

  > "'Hail and fire, mingled with blood,' were cast upon the earth. The terrible effects of this Gothic invasion are represented as 'hail,' from the fact of the northern origin of the invaders; 'fire,' from the destruction by flame of both city and country; and 'blood,' from the terrible slaughter of the citizens of the empire…" — DAR 455.6.

- **Revelation 9:1, 5, 10** — The fifth trumpet (first woe): a fallen star, the bottomless pit, and power "to hurt men five months." The 150 years (a prophetic five months) of Ottoman tormenting — day-for-a-year (Num. 14:34; Eze. 4:6).

  > "'Five months,' thirty days to a month, give us one hundred and fifty days; and these days, being symbolic, signify one hundred and fifty years. Commencing July 27, 1299, the one hundred and fifty years reach to 1449. During that whole period the Turks were engaged in an almost perpetual warfare with the Greek empire, but yet without conquering it." — DAR 479.5.

- **Revelation 9:15** — The sixth trumpet: "the four angels were loosed, which were prepared for an hour, and a day, and a month, and a year, for to slay the third part of men." The four sultanies on the Euphrates (Aleppo, Iconium, Damascus, Bagdad) loosed to overthrow the Eastern empire.

  > "When the sixth angel sounded, it was commanded to take off the restraints which had been imposed on the nation, by which they were restricted to the work of tormenting men, and their commission was enlarged so as to permit them to slay the third part of men." — DAR 480.2.

  > "The Four Angels. — These were the four principal sultanies of which the Ottoman empire was composed, located in the country watered by the great river Euphrates. These sultanies were situated at Aleppo, Iconium, Damascus, and Bagdad. Previously they had been restrained; but God commanded, and they were loosed." — DAR 480.3.

- **Revelation 9:15 (the time computed)** — An hour + day + month + year = 391 years and 15 days. Beginning July 27, 1449 (when Constantine reigned only by the Turk's permission), the period "would end Aug. 11, 1840."

  > "The four angels were loosed for an hour, a day, a month, and a year, to slay the third part of men. This period amounts to three hundred ninety-one years and fifteen days, during which Ottoman supremacy was to exist in Constantinople." — DAR 481.1.

  > "Commencing when the one hundred and fifty years ended, July 27, 1449, the period would end Aug. 11, 1840. Judging from the manner of the commencement of the Ottoman supremacy, that it was by a voluntary acknowledgment on the part of the Greek emperor that he reigned only by permission of the Turkish sultan…" — DAR 485.1.

- **Revelation 9:18-21** — "By these three was the third part of men killed, by the fire, and by the smoke, and by the brimstone." The gunpowder and cannon by which Constantinople fell (1453) and Ottoman supremacy was finally broken — "yet repented not."

  > "These verses express the deadly effect of the new mode of warfare introduced. It was by means of these agents, — gunpowder, firearms, and cannon, — that Constantinople was finally overcome, and given into the hands of the Turks." — DAR 484.2.

  > "The events that transpired under the sixth trumpet constituted the second woe; yet these judgments led to no improvement in the manners and morals of men. Those who escaped them learned nothing by their manifestation in the earth." — DAR 487.3.

> **The 1840 test (sixth trumpet).** Josiah Litch, "in 1838, two years before
> the predicted event," published from Revelation 9 that Ottoman independence
> would depart in "A.D. 1840, sometime in the month of August." On the
> **eleventh day of August, 1840**, the sultan's ultimatum passed into foreign
> hands — "the matter would be forever beyond the control" of the Turk — and
> Mohammedan independence in Constantinople departed on the very day. "The
> exact accomplishment... gave a mighty impetus to the great Advent movement."
> _(DAR 485.2; 486.2; 487.1; GC 334.4.)_

> **Bullet — the Trumpets' standpoint.** The seven trumpets give the political
> and military judgments on the nations among whom the church dwells — Rome's
> fall under the four winds of barbarian invasion, the Saracen and Ottoman
> woes, ending in the verifiable date of August 11, 1840. They are God's
> scourges on apostate Christendom, not the spiritual record of the churches.
> _(DAR 453.3; 487.4.)_

### Series 4 — The Seven PLAGUES: the final, unmingled wrath

- **Revelation 15:1** — "Seven angels having the seven last plagues; for in them is filled up the wrath of God." Called the **last** plagues because in them God's wrath is "filled up" — "a manifestation of Heaven's unmingled wrath... for the last generation of the wicked. The work of mercy is then forever past."

  > "This chapter introduces the seven last plagues, a manifestation of Heaven's unmingled wrath, and the fullness of its measure, for the last generation of the wicked. The work of mercy is then forever past." — DAR 638.1.

  > "This chapter gives a description of the seven vials of the unmingled wrath of God, and the effects that follow as they are poured upon the earth." — DAR 641.1.

- **Revelation 15:8** — "The temple was filled with smoke from the glory of God... and no man was able to enter into the temple, till the seven plagues... were fulfilled." No mediation during the plagues — the door of the sanctuary is shut; probation is over before the first vial falls.

  > "This chapter gives a description of the seven vials of the unmingled wrath of God, and the effects that follow as they are poured upon the earth." — DAR 641.

  > "When Christ ceases His intercession in the sanctuary, the unmingled wrath threatened against those who worship the beast and his image and receive his mark (Revelation 14:9, 10), will be poured out." — GC 627.3.

- **Revelation 16:1-2** — "Go your ways, and pour out the vials of the wrath of God upon the earth. And the first... poured out his vial... and there fell a noisome and grievous sore upon the men which had the mark of the beast." The plague falls on the very class warned by the third angel — proof it follows the close of his work.

  > "The Chronology of the Plagues. — The description of this plague clearly reveals at once their chronology; for it is poured out upon those who have the mark of the beast, and who worship his image, — the identical work against which the third angel warns us. This is conclusive proof that these judgments are not poured out till after this angel closes his work." — DAR 641.3.

- **Revelation 14:9-10** — "If any man worship the beast... The same shall drink of the wine of the wrath of God, which is poured out **without mixture** into the cup of his indignation." The plagues are this very wine — unmixed with the mercy that has tempered every prior judgment.

  > "These plagues are the wine of God's wrath without mixture, threatened by the third angel… Such language cannot be applied to any judgments visited upon the earth while Christ pleads between his Father and our fallen race; hence we must locate them in the future, when probation shall have closed." — DAR 642.3.

  > "When Christ ceases His intercession in the sanctuary, the unmingled wrath threatened against those who worship the beast and his image and receive his mark (Revelation 14:9, 10), will be poured out." — GC 627.3.

- **Revelation 16:10-12** — The fifth vial fills the beast's kingdom "with darkness," the sixth dries up "the great river Euphrates" — yet men "blasphemed... and repented not." Because mercy has withdrawn, the plagues harden rather than convert.

  > "The Sixth Plague. — What is the great River Euphrates, upon which this vial is poured out? … One [view] is that it is a symbol of the nation occupying the territory through which that river flows. The latter opinion is preferable… [for] it should be noticed that the drying up takes place to prepare the way of the kings of the East; that is, regular military organizations." — DAR 641-650.

- **Revelation 16:17** — The seventh vial poured "into the air; and there came a great voice out of the temple of heaven, from the throne, saying, It is done." The wrath finished; the voice of God shaking earth and heaven (cf. Rev. 6:14).

  > "From Hebrews 12:25-27, Joel 3:16, Jeremiah 25:30-33, Revelation 16:17, we learn that it is the voice of God, as he speaks in terrible majesty from his throne in heaven, that causes this fearful commotion in earth and sky… and not only the earth will shake, but the heavens also." — DAR 432.3.

- **Revelation 22:11** — "He that is unjust, let him be unjust still... and he that is holy, let him be holy still." The decree that fixes every destiny — the close of probation — spoken just before the plagues; after it, no man's character can change and mercy no longer pleads.

  > "When the third angel's message closes, mercy no longer pleads for the guilty inhabitants of the earth… Then Jesus ceases His intercession in the sanctuary above… 'He that is unjust, let him be unjust still:… and he that is holy, let him be holy still.' … Every case has been decided for life or death." — GC 613.2.

  > "When Christ ceases His intercession in the sanctuary, the unmingled wrath threatened against those who worship the beast and his image and receive his mark… will be poured out." — GC 627.3.

> **Bullet — the Plagues' standpoint.** The seven last plagues stand alone:
> they fall only on the wicked, only on those who have the mark of the beast,
> and only **after probation has closed** — "when Christ ceases His
> intercession in the sanctuary." "All the judgments upon men, prior to the
> close of probation, have been mingled with mercy... but in the final
> judgment, wrath is poured out unmixed with mercy." This is what makes the
> plagues unique among the four sevens. _(GC 627.3; 628.2; DAR 642.3.)_

### Why they are FOUR lines, not one

- **Revelation 4:1** — "Come up hither, and I will shew thee things which must be hereafter." Each new vision (1:11 churches, 6:1 seals, 8:2 trumpets, 15:1 plagues) restarts the prophet's view, sweeping the same ground from a fresh standpoint rather than continuing where the last left off.

  > "The expression 'after this' does not denote that what is recorded in chapter 4 and onward was to take place after the fulfillment of everything recorded in the three preceding chapters, but only that after he had seen and heard what is there recorded, he had the new view which he now introduces." — DAR 384.

  > "This verse introduces a new and distinct series of events. In the seals we have had the history of the church during what is called the gospel dispensation. In the seven trumpets, now introduced, we have the principal political and warlike events which were to transpire during the same time." — DAR 453.3.

- **Revelation 11:18** — "The nations were angry, and thy wrath is come, and the time of the dead, that they should be judged." The seventh trumpet, like the seventh seal and seventh plague, lands at one and the same terminus — the judgment and reward — proving the lines are parallel, not consecutive.

  > "'And Thy Wrath Is Come.' — The wrath of God for the present generation is filled up in the seven last plagues (chapter 15:1), which consequently must here be referred to, and which are soon to be poured out upon the earth." — DAR 506.3.

- **Daniel 12:1; Revelation 22:11** — "Michael shall stand up" the moment the third angel's message closes and "mercy no longer pleads"; the unjust are sealed unjust, the holy sealed holy. This single hinge separates the first three sevens (running _through_ probation) from the fourth (poured out _after_ it).

  > "When the third angel's message closes, mercy no longer pleads for the guilty inhabitants of the earth… He lifts His hands and with a loud voice says, 'It is done;'… 'He that is unjust, let him be unjust still:… and he that is holy, let him be holy still.'" — GC 613.2.

  > "When Christ ceases His intercession in the sanctuary, the unmingled wrath threatened against those who worship the beast and his image and receive his mark (Revelation 14:9, 10), will be poured out." — GC 627.3.

> **Summary distinguishing all four.** Churches = the church's _religious_ life
> (her spiritual state), Seals = her _providential_ life (her outward
> fortunes), Trumpets = the _political_ judgments on the nations around her —
> all three running together through the gospel dispensation; while Plagues =
> the _final wrath_ on the wicked, alone among the four because it falls only
> after probation closes, "without mixture" of mercy, on those who bear the
> mark of the beast. _(DAR 453.3; 641.3; 642.3; GC 627.3-628.2.)_

---

# 25. Babylon Fallen, the Loud Cry, and "Come Out of Her"

> _The second angel's announcement of Babylon's fall (Rev. 14:8) is to be repeated at the close, swelling into the loud cry of Revelation 18: another angel comes down from heaven, "having great power," lightens the whole earth with his glory, and cries, "Babylon the great is fallen," adding the call, "Come out of her, my people." This is the final warning, the latter rain, the close of the gospel work — the swelling of the third angel's message before probation ends. Babylon is mother and daughters together: apostate Rome and the fallen Protestant churches that cling to her doctrines. (DAR 663-673; GC 603-612, 390; EW 277-279.)_

### Babylon Identified — Mother and Daughters

- **Revelation 17:5** — "Upon her forehead was a name written, MYSTERY, BABYLON THE GREAT, THE MOTHER OF HARLOTS AND ABOMINATIONS OF THE EARTH." She is a mother, therefore she has daughters — churches that cling to her doctrines and traditions.

  > "Babylon is said to be 'the mother of harlots.' By her daughters must be symbolized churches that cling to her doctrines and traditions, and follow her example of sacrificing the truth and the approval of God, in order to form an unlawful alliance with the world." — GC 382.

  > "Babylon is a term which embraces not only the Roman Catholic Church, but religious bodies which have sprung from her, bringing many of her errors and traditions along with them." — DAR 663.

- **Revelation 17:1-2** — The great whore "sitteth upon many waters," and "the kings of the earth have committed fornication with her, and the inhabitants of the earth have been made drunk with the wine of her fornication." False doctrine (wine) intoxicating the nations through union of church and state.

  > "This woman [popery] is called the mother of harlots and abominations. Who are the daughters? The Lutheran, the Presbyterian, and the Episcopalian churches are all branches of the [Roman] Catholic… They hold the distinctive principles of papacy in common with papists." — DAR 664.

- **Revelation 14:8** — "Babylon is fallen, is fallen, that great city, because she made all nations drink of the wine of the wrath of her fornication." The second angel's message, first preached in the summer of 1844 against the churches that rejected the advent truth.

  > "In Revelation 14 the first angel is followed by a second proclaiming: 'Babylon is fallen, is fallen, that great city, because she made all nations drink of the wine of the wrath of her fornication.'… It is employed in Scripture to designate the various forms of false or apostate religion." — GC 381.

  > "The second angel's message of Revelation 14 was first preached in the summer of 1844, and it then had a more direct application to the churches of the United States, where the warning of the judgment had been most widely proclaimed and most generally rejected, and where the declension in the churches had been most rapid." — GC 389.

  > "In chapter 14 we had a message announcing the fall of Babylon. Babylon is a term which embraces not only the Roman Catholic Church, but religious bodies which have sprung from her, bringing many of her errors and traditions along with them." — DAR 663.

### The Fall Is Moral, Not Yet Literal

- **Revelation 18:2** — "Babylon the great is fallen, is fallen, and is become the habitation of devils, and the hold of every foul spirit, and a cage of every unclean and hateful bird." The fall is moral — a fall by apostasy — for God's people are still in her after this fall and must be called out.

  > "The fall of Babylon here spoken of cannot be literal destruction… the people of God are there after her fall, and are called out in order that they may not receive of her plagues… The fall is therefore a moral one; for the result of it is that Babylon becomes the habitation of devils, and the hold of every foul spirit, and a cage of every unclean and hateful bird." — DAR 663.

  > "She has filled up the measure of her guilt, and destruction is about to fall upon her. But God still has a people in Babylon; and before the visitation of His judgments these faithful ones must be called out, that they partake not of her sins and 'receive not of her plagues.'" — GC 604.

- **Revelation 18:3** — "All nations have drunk of the wine of the wrath of her fornication, and the kings of the earth have committed fornication with her, and the merchants of the earth are waxed rich through the abundance of her delicacies." Worldliness, pride, and union with the kings of the earth complete the picture of apostasy.

  > "They ran well for a season, and had the approbation of God; but fencing themselves about with creeds, they have failed to keep pace with the advancing light of prophetic truth, and hence have been left in a position where they will finally develop a character as evil and odious in the sight of God as that of the church from which they first withdrew." — DAR 664.

- **Revelation 17:5 / 18:24** — "In her was found the blood of prophets, and of saints, and of all that were slain upon the earth." The accumulated guilt of persecution mounts up against her.

  > "Meanwhile, great Babylon's sins mount up to the heavens, and the remembrance of her iniquities comes up before God. The storm of vengeance gathers. The great tidal wave of supernal wrath rolls onward." — DAR 673.

- **Revelation 16:13-14** — Three unclean spirits like frogs go out of the dragon, beast, and false prophet — "spirits of devils, working miracles" — gathering the nations. Spiritualism is the agency by which Babylon becomes "the habitation of devils."

  > "The work brought to view in verse 2 is in process of accomplishment, and will soon be completed, by the work of Spiritualism. What are called in Revelation 16:14 'spirits of devils working miracles' are secretly but rapidly working their way into the religious denominations above referred to." — DAR 667.

### Another Angel — The Earth Lightened with His Glory

- **Revelation 18:1** — "And after these things I saw another angel come down from heaven, having great power; and the earth was lightened with his glory." This new agency unites with and gives power to the third angel's message; no such description of a message to man is elsewhere in the Bible.

  > "The angel who unites in the proclamation of the third angel's message is to lighten the whole earth with his glory. A work of world-wide extent and unwonted power is here foretold." — GC 611.

  > "He comes, as it were, nearer to the earth, with a message more pointed and direct; and he has 'great power,' and the earth is 'lightened with his glory.' No such description of a message from heaven to man is elsewhere to be found in all the Bible." — DAR 672.

  > "Then I saw another mighty angel commissioned to descend to the earth, to unite his voice with the third angel, and give power and force to his message… and as he descended, the earth was lightened with his glory." — EW 277.

- **Revelation 18:2** — "And he cried mightily with a strong voice, saying, Babylon the great is fallen." The second angel's message is repeated, with added mention of the corruptions entering the churches since 1844 — this is the loud cry.

  > "This scripture points forward to a time when the announcement of the fall of Babylon, as made by the second angel of Revelation 14 (verse 8), is to be repeated, with the additional mention of the corruptions which have been entering the various organizations that constitute Babylon, since that message was first given, in the summer of 1844." — GC 603.

  > "The message of the fall of Babylon, as given by the second angel, is repeated, with the additional mention of the corruptions which have been entering the churches since 1844. The work of this angel comes in at the right time to join in the last great work of the third angel's message as it swells to a loud cry." — EW 277.

- **Revelation 14:9-10** — The third angel "followed them, saying with a loud voice, If any man worship the beast and his image... the same shall drink of the wine of the wrath of God." The angel of Rev. 18 unites with this third message; together they form "the final warning to be given to the inhabitants of the earth."

  > "In connection with his message the call is heard: 'Come out of her, My people.' These announcements, uniting with the third angel's message, constitute the final warning to be given to the inhabitants of the earth." — GC 604.

  > "The advent movement of 1840-44 was a glorious manifestation of the power of God… but these are to be exceeded by the mighty movement under the last warning of the third angel." — GC 611.

### "Come Out of Her, My People"

- **Revelation 18:4** — "And I heard another voice from heaven, saying, Come out of her, my people, that ye be not partakers of her sins, and that ye receive not of her plagues." A fifth celestial agency; the divine call to separate from fallen Babylon before the plagues fall.

  > "This voice from heaven is called 'another' voice, showing that a new agency is here introduced. We now have five celestial messengers expressly mentioned as engaged in this last religious reformation." — DAR 671.

  > "Revelation 18 points to the time when, as the result of rejecting the threefold warning of Revelation 14:6-12… the people of God still in Babylon will be called upon to separate from her communion. This message is the last that will ever be given to the world; and it will accomplish its work." — GC 390.

  > "She has filled up the measure of her guilt, and destruction is about to fall upon her… these faithful ones must be called out, that they partake not of her sins and 'receive not of her plagues.'" — GC 604.

- **Revelation 18:5** — "For her sins have reached unto heaven, and God hath remembered her iniquities." She has filled up the measure of her guilt; destruction can no longer be delayed.

  > "Of Babylon, at the time brought to view in this prophecy, it is declared: 'Her sins have reached unto heaven, and God hath remembered her iniquities.' Revelation 18:5. She has filled up the measure of her guilt, and destruction is about to fall upon her." — GC 604.

  > "These are terrible descriptions of apostasy, showing that, as a consequence of her fall, she piles up an accumulation of sins even to the heavens, and becomes subject to the judgments of God, which can no longer be delayed." — DAR 664.

- **Jeremiah 51:6** — "Flee out of the midst of Babylon, and deliver every man his soul: be not cut off in her iniquity; for this is the time of the LORD'S vengeance." The Old Testament call out of literal Babylon prefigures the gospel call out of spiritual Babylon.

  > "The term Babylon, as applied to them, is not a term of reproach, but is simply expressive of the confusion and diversity of sentiment that exists among them. Babylon need not have fallen, but might have been healed (Jeremiah 51:9) by the reception of the truth; but she rejected it." — DAR 666.

  > "Suddenly another voice rings out from heaven, 'Come out of her, my people!' The humble, sincere, devoted children of God… heed the voice, wash their hands of her sins, separate from her communion, escape, and are saved, while Babylon becomes the victim of the just judgments of God." — DAR 673.

- **Jeremiah 51:7** — "Babylon hath been a golden cup in the LORD'S hand, that made all the earth drunken... therefore the nations are mad." The cup of false doctrine; the same figure John uses of mystery Babylon.

  > "their creeds have been formulated under the influence of the wine (errors) of Babylon, one of which is that the spirits of our dead friends, conscious, intelligent, and active, are all about us." — DAR 667.

- **Jeremiah 51:8-9** — "Babylon is suddenly fallen and destroyed... We would have healed Babylon, but she is not healed: forsake her." She might have been healed by receiving the truth, but rejected it; therefore "forsake her."

  > "Babylon need not have fallen, but might have been healed (Jeremiah 51:9) by the reception of the truth; but she rejected it, and confusion and dissensions still reign within her borders, and worldliness and pride are fast choking out every plant of heavenly growth." — DAR 666.

- **Jeremiah 51:45** — "My people, go ye out of the midst of her, and deliver ye every man his soul from the fierce anger of the LORD." The personal, individual nature of the call — each one must escape.

  > "The humble, sincere, devoted children of God, of whom there are some still left, and who sigh and cry over the abominations done in the land, heed the voice, wash their hands of her sins, separate from her communion, escape, and are saved." — DAR 673.

- **Jeremiah 50:8** — "Remove out of the midst of Babylon, and go forth out of the land of the Chaldeans, and be as the he goats before the flocks." God's people lead out, first and decisively.

  > "God has still a people there, and she must be entitled to some regard on their account until they are called from her communion. Nor will it be necessary to wait long for this call." — DAR 671.

- **Isaiah 52:11** — "Depart ye, depart ye, go ye out from thence, touch no unclean thing... be ye clean, that bear the vessels of the LORD." Separation joined to purity — the called-out people must be clean.

  > "The time will come when those who love God supremely can no longer remain in connection with such as are 'lovers of pleasures more than lovers of God; having a form of godliness, but denying the power thereof.'" — GC 390.

- **2 Corinthians 6:17** — "Come out from among them, and be ye separate, saith the Lord, and touch not the unclean thing; and I will receive you." Paul applies the Babylon call to the church: separation is the condition of acceptance.

  > "All the children of the Lord that remain in Babylon will heed the call: 'Come out of her, My people' (Revelation 18:4)." — GC 390.

  > "Soon Babylon will become so thoroughly leavened with the influence of these evil agents that her condition will be fully manifest to all the honest in heart, and the way be all prepared for the work which the apostle now introduces." — DAR 671.

- **Ezekiel 9:4** — A mark is set "upon the foreheads of the men that sigh and that cry for all the abominations" — these are the ones who heed the call and are sealed before the judgments fall.

  > "The humble, sincere, devoted children of God, of whom there are some still left, and who sigh and cry over the abominations done in the land, heed the voice, wash their hands of her sins, separate from her communion, escape, and are saved, while Babylon becomes the victim of the just judgments of God." — DAR 673.

### The Loud Cry — Swelling of the Third Message

- **Revelation 18:1 (its power)** — The angel "lightens the earth with his glory"; this is a "work of world-wide extent and unwonted power," exceeding even the advent movement of 1840-44.

  > "A work of world-wide extent and unwonted power is here foretold. The advent movement of 1840-44 was a glorious manifestation of the power of God… but these are to be exceeded by the mighty movement under the last warning of the third angel." — GC 611.

  > "This is the last; and as is meet, it comes with surpassing glory and unwonted power. It is an awful hour when a world's destiny is to be decided." — DAR 672.

- **Revelation 18:4 (joining the third message)** — As the midnight cry joined the second angel's message in 1844, so this message joins the third, swelling it to a loud cry that "will close with power and strength far exceeding the midnight cry."

  > "This message seemed to be an addition to the third message, joining it as the midnight cry joined the second angel's message in 1844… I saw that this message will close with power and strength far exceeding the midnight cry." — EW 277-278.

- **Revelation 13:13 / Revelation 16:14** — Satan counterfeits with "lying wonders, even bringing down fire from heaven," while genuine "miracles will be wrought, the sick will be healed," dividing the inhabitants of the earth.

  > "Miracles will be wrought, the sick will be healed, and signs and wonders will follow the believers. Satan also works, with lying wonders, even bringing down fire from heaven in the sight of men. Revelation 13:13. Thus the inhabitants of the earth will be brought to take their stand." — GC 612.

### The Latter Rain — Power for the Final Harvest

- **Joel 2:23** — "Be glad then, ye children of Zion... for he hath given you the former rain moderately, and he will cause to come down for you the rain, the former rain, and the latter rain in the first month." The latter rain ripens the harvest as the loud cry goes forth.

  > "As the 'former rain' was given, in the outpouring of the Holy Spirit at the opening of the gospel, to cause the upspringing of the precious seed, so the 'latter rain' will be given at its close for the ripening of the harvest." — GC 611.

- **Hosea 6:3** — "Then shall we know, if we follow on to know the LORD... and he shall come unto us as the rain, as the latter and former rain unto the earth." The outpouring of the Spirit at the close, paralleling Pentecost.

  > "The work will be similar to that of the Day of Pentecost… 'Then shall we know, if we follow on to know the Lord: His going forth is prepared as the morning; and He shall come unto us as the rain, as the latter and former rain unto the earth.' Hosea 6:3." — GC 611.

- **Zechariah 10:1** — "Ask ye of the LORD rain in the time of the latter rain." The latter rain is to be sought — the called-out people pray for the Spirit's outpouring.

  > "So the 'latter rain' will be given at its close for the ripening of the harvest… 'In the last days, saith God, I will pour out of My Spirit upon all flesh.'" — GC 611.

- **James 5:7** — "The husbandman waiteth for the precious fruit of the earth, and hath long patience for it, until he receive the early and latter rain." The harvest is not gathered until the latter rain has done its work.

  > "As the 'former rain' was given… to cause the upspringing of the precious seed, so the 'latter rain' will be given at its close for the ripening of the harvest." — GC 611.

- **Acts 3:19** — "Repent ye therefore, and be converted, that your sins may be blotted out, when the times of refreshing shall come from the presence of the Lord." The "times of refreshing" are the latter rain at the close of the gospel.

  > "Here are 'the times of refreshing' to which the apostle Peter looked forward when he said: 'Repent ye therefore, and be converted, that your sins may be blotted out, when the times of refreshing shall come from the presence of the Lord.'" — GC 612.

### Babylon's Final Destruction — After the Call

- **Revelation 18:6-7** — "Reward her even as she rewarded you, and double unto her double... for she saith in her heart, I sit a queen, and am no widow, and shall see no sorrow." Her self-exaltation and security precede her overthrow.

  > "It is doubtless this first intoxication of power that leads this branch of Babylon to cherish in her heart the boast, 'I sit a queen, and am no widow;'… now I rule like a queen; I shall see no sorrow." — DAR 674.

- **Revelation 18:8** — "Therefore shall her plagues come in one day, death, and mourning, and famine; and she shall be utterly burned with fire: for strong is the Lord God who judgeth her." The literal destruction follows the moral fall, after the people of God are called out.

  > "There are events to take place in Babylon after her fall which utterly forbid this idea… in these plagues is embraced her literal destruction." — DAR 663.

  > "Suddenly another voice rings out from heaven, 'Come out of her, my people!'… they heed the voice… escape, and are saved, while Babylon becomes the victim of the just judgments of God." — DAR 673.

- **Revelation 18:9-11** — The kings and merchants of the earth "shall bewail her, and lament for her, when they shall see the smoke of her burning," crying, "in one hour is thy judgment come." Those who shared her sins share her doom.
