  > "The storm of vengeance gathers. The great tidal wave of supernal wrath rolls onward… ere it will burst upon the great city of confusion, and proud Babylon will go down as a millstone sinks in the depths of the sea." — DAR 673.

- **Revelation 18:21** — "A mighty angel took up a stone like a great millstone, and cast it into the sea, saying, Thus with violence shall that great city Babylon be thrown down, and shall be found no more at all." Proud Babylon sinks like a millstone — total and final.

  > "Proud Babylon will go down as a millstone sinks in the depths of the sea… while Babylon becomes the victim of the just judgments of God. There are stirring times before the church. Let us be ready for the crisis." — DAR 673.

- **Revelation 18:20** — "Rejoice over her, thou heaven, and ye holy apostles and prophets; for God hath avenged you on her." Heaven vindicates the saints whose blood Babylon shed.

  > "As she fills up the cup of persecution to the saints, so the angel of the Lord will persecute her (Psalms 35:6); and judgments from on high will bring upon her, in a twofold degree, the evil which she thought to bring upon the humble servants of the Lord." — DAR 674.

---

# 26. The Close of Probation and the Time of Jacob's Trouble

> _When the investigative judgment ends, every case is forever decided — "He that is unjust, let him be unjust still... he that is holy, let him be holy still." Christ lays down the censer, ceases His intercession, and Michael stands up; the temple fills with smoke and no man can enter till the seven last plagues are poured out. God's people then pass through the time of Jacob's trouble — without a Mediator, yet hidden under the shadow of the Almighty, standing through the death decree, and delivered at last by the voice of God. (GC 490; GC 613-634; GC 635-652; EW 279-285; DAR 293-318.)_

### Probation Closes — Every Case Decided

- **Revelation 22:11** — "He that is unjust, let him be unjust still... he that is holy, let him be holy still." When the work of the investigative judgment closes, the destiny of all is fixed for life or death; mercy no longer pleads. This is the sentence Christ utters as probation ends, a short time before His appearing.

  > "When the work of the investigative judgment closes, the destiny of all will have been decided for life or death. Probation is ended a short time before the appearing of the Lord in the clouds of heaven." — GC 490.2.

  > "When the third angel's message closes, mercy no longer pleads for the guilty inhabitants of the earth.... Then Jesus ceases His intercession in the sanctuary above.... Every case has been decided for life or death." — GC 613.2.

- **Revelation 22:12** — "Behold, I come quickly; and my reward is with me, to give every man according as his work shall be." Character is fixed before He comes; the reward is already apportioned because probation has closed.

  > "He that is unjust, let him be unjust still... and he that is holy, let him be holy still. And, behold, I come quickly; and My reward is with Me, to give every man according as his work shall be." — GC 490.2.

- **Daniel 12:1** — "At that time shall Michael stand up... and there shall be a time of trouble, such as never was." Michael, the Archangel — Christ Himself — rises from His mediatorial throne; His "standing up" marks the close of His priestly intercession and ushers in the unequalled time of trouble.

  > "And at that time shall Michael stand up, the great prince which standeth for the children of thy people: and there shall be a time of trouble, such as never was since there was a nation even to that same time." — DAR 293.1.

  > "Who, then, is Michael? and what is his standing up? — Michael is called, in Jude 9, the archangel.... But the archangel is Michael; hence also Michael is the Son of God. The expression of Daniel, 'the great prince which standeth for the children of thy people,' is alone sufficient to identify the one here spoken of as the Saviour of men." — DAR 294.1.

  > "'At that time shall Michael stand up, the great Prince which standeth for the children of thy people: and there shall be a time of trouble, such as never was since there was a nation even to that same time.' Daniel 12:1." — GC 613.1.

- **Daniel 12:10** — "Many shall be purified, and made white, and tried; but the wicked shall do wickedly... but the wise shall understand." The decision is moral and irreversible: the righteous are purified and sealed, the wicked are confirmed in evil, before probation shuts.

  > "Here are the events, to come, as we believe, in the following order: ... (4) The standing up of Michael, or the beginning of the reign of Christ, and his coming in the clouds of heaven." — DAR 293.2.

  > "Every case had been decided for life or death.... Christ had received His kingdom, having made the atonement for His people and blotted out their sins. The subjects of the kingdom were made up." — EW 280.1.

### The Censer Laid Down — No More Intercession

- **Hebrews 9:24** — Christ "now to appear in the presence of God for us." His present work is intercession in the heavenly sanctuary; when that ministry ends, the saints must stand without an Advocate.

  > "Then Jesus ceases His intercession in the sanctuary above. He lifts His hands and with a loud voice says, 'It is done;' ... Every case has been decided for life or death." — GC 613.2.

  > "While Jesus had been standing between God and guilty man, a restraint was upon the people; but when He stepped out from between man and the Father, the restraint was removed.... In that fearful time, after the close of Jesus' mediation, the saints were living in the sight of a holy God without an intercessor." — EW 280.2.

- **Revelation 8:3-5** — The angel offers incense "with the prayers of all saints" — then "took the censer, and filled it with fire of the altar, and cast it into the earth." The casting down of the censer pictures the close of intercession; voices, thunders, and an earthquake follow, the prelude to the plagues.

  > "Then I saw Jesus, who had been ministering before the ark containing the ten commandments, throw down the censer. He raised His hands, and with a loud voice said, 'It is done.'" — EW 279.2.

  > "As Jesus moved out of the most holy place, I heard the tinkling of the bells upon His garment; and as He left, a cloud of darkness covered the inhabitants of the earth. There was then no mediator between guilty man and an offended God." — EW 280.2.

- **Revelation 15:8** — "The temple was filled with smoke... and no man was able to enter into the temple, till the seven plagues... were fulfilled." Once mercy's door shuts, no intercession can be made in the sanctuary; the saints must live "without an intercessor."

  > "When He leaves the sanctuary, darkness covers the inhabitants of the earth. In that fearful time the righteous must live in the sight of a holy God without an intercessor.... The wicked have passed the boundary of their probation." — GC 614.1.

  > "It was impossible for the plagues to be poured out while Jesus officiated in the sanctuary; but as His work there is finished, and His intercession closes, there is nothing to stay the wrath of God." — EW 280.2.

### The Plagues Fall — Wrath Without Mixture

- **Revelation 16:1-2** — "Go your ways, and pour out the vials of the wrath of God upon the earth." The first vial brings "a noisome and grievous sore upon the men which had the mark of the beast." Probation being past, the plagues fall on those who chose the beast over God's seal.

  > "Many of the wicked were greatly enraged as they suffered the effects of the plagues. It was a scene of fearful agony.... Loud, wailing cries were heard in every direction, 'It was you who kept me from receiving the truth which would have saved me from this awful hour.'" — EW 282.1.

  > "Every case has been decided for life or death. Christ has made the atonement for His people and blotted out their sins." — GC 613.2.

- **Revelation 16:8-11** — The fourth vial scorches men with fire; the fifth fills the beast's kingdom with darkness; yet they "blasphemed the name of God... and they repented not." The plagues are unmixed wrath — no mercy is mingled, because probation has closed and no repentance follows.

  > "The people turned upon their ministers with bitter hate and reproached them, saying, 'You have not warned us....' But I saw that the ministers did not escape the wrath of God. Their suffering was tenfold greater than that of their people." — EW 282.1.

  > "The wicked have passed the boundary of their probation; the Spirit of God, persistently resisted, has been at last withdrawn. Unsheltered by divine grace, they have no protection from the wicked one." — GC 614.1.

- **Revelation 14:9-10** — Whoever worships the beast "shall drink of the wine of the wrath of God, which is poured out without mixture into the cup of his indignation." The third angel's warning defines who receives the plagues: the worshipers of the beast and his image.

  > "When the third angel's message closes, mercy no longer pleads for the guilty inhabitants of the earth.... the final test has been brought upon the world, and all who have proved themselves loyal to the divine precepts have received 'the seal of the living God.'" — GC 613.2.

  > "While Jesus had been ministering in the sanctuary, the judgment had been going on for the righteous dead, and then for the righteous living.... The subjects of the kingdom were made up." — EW 280.1.

### The Time of Jacob's Trouble

- **Jeremiah 30:5-7** — "A voice of trembling, of fear, and not of peace... all faces are turned into paleness... it is even the time of Jacob's trouble; but he shall be saved out of it." The prophet names this final crisis of God's people — anguish unequalled, yet ending in deliverance.

  > "The people of God will then be plunged into those scenes of affliction and distress described by the prophet as the time of Jacob's trouble.... 'it is even the time of Jacob's trouble; but he shall be saved out of it.' Jeremiah 30:5-7." — GC 616.1.

- **Genesis 32:24-28** — Jacob wrestles alone till break of day: "I will not let thee go, except thou bless me." His night of agony, pleading the covenant while in deadly peril from Esau, typifies the saints wrestling for deliverance in the time of trouble.

  > "Jacob's night of anguish, when he wrestled in prayer for deliverance from the hand of Esau (Genesis 32:24-30), represents the experience of God's people in the time of trouble.... His only hope was in the mercy of God; his only defense must be prayer." — GC 616.2.

- **Daniel 12:1** — "Such as never was since there was a nation" — the trouble is unparalleled, falling after Michael stands up; yet "thy people shall be delivered, every one... found written in the book." The same verse that opens the trouble guarantees the deliverance.

  > "And at that time shall Michael stand up... and there shall be a time of trouble, such as never was since there was a nation even to that same time: and at that time thy people shall be delivered, every one that shall be found written in the book." — DAR 293.1.

  > "'... and at that time thy people shall be delivered, everyone that shall be found written in the book.' Daniel 12:1." — GC 613.1.

- **Revelation 13:15** — The image of the beast causes "that as many as would not worship the image... should be killed." The death decree against the commandment-keepers is the sharpest edge of Jacob's trouble.

  > "A decree will finally be issued against those who hallow the Sabbath of the fourth commandment, denouncing them as deserving of the severest punishment and giving the people liberty, after a certain time, to put them to death." — GC 615.2.

  > "Though a general decree has fixed the time when commandment keepers may be put to death, their enemies will in some cases anticipate the decree, and before the time specified, will endeavor to take their lives. But none can pass the mighty guardians stationed about every faithful soul." — GC 631.1.

### Famine of the Word — The Wicked's Despair

- **Amos 8:11-12** — "A famine... not of bread... but of hearing the words of the LORD: and they shall... run to and fro to seek the word of the LORD, and shall not find it." When probation closes, the Spirit is withdrawn from the impenitent; they seek and cannot find.

  > "The wicked have passed the boundary of their probation; the Spirit of God, persistently resisted, has been at last withdrawn. Unsheltered by divine grace, they have no protection from the wicked one." — GC 614.1.

  > "This argument will appear conclusive; and a decree will finally be issued against those who hallow the Sabbath of the fourth commandment... giving the people liberty, after a certain time, to put them to death." — GC 615.2.

- **Ezekiel 7:19** — "Their silver and their gold shall not be able to deliver them in the day of the wrath of the LORD... because it is the stumblingblock of their iniquity." Riches are worthless when the plagues fall; nothing can shield the lost in that day.

  > "God's long-suffering has ended. The world has rejected His mercy, despised His love, and trampled upon His law.... Unsheltered by divine grace, they have no protection from the wicked one." — GC 614.1.

- **Proverbs 11:4** — "Riches profit not in the day of wrath: but righteousness delivereth from death." Only the imputed righteousness of Christ, received before probation closed, avails in the time of trouble.

  > "Christ had received His kingdom, having made the atonement for His people and blotted out their sins.... And the kingdom, and the greatness of the kingdom under the whole heaven, was given to Jesus and the heirs of salvation." — EW 280.1.

### God's People Hidden and Kept

- **Psalm 91:1-11** — "He that dwelleth in the secret place of the most High shall abide under the shadow of the Almighty... There shall no evil befall thee, neither shall any plague come nigh thy dwelling." Though the plagues rage and "a thousand shall fall at thy side," the sealed are sheltered by God Himself.

  > "When He leaves the sanctuary, darkness covers the inhabitants of the earth.... Satan will then plunge the inhabitants of the earth into one great, final trouble." — GC 614.1.

  > "I saw the saints leaving the cities and villages, and associating together in companies, and living in the most solitary places. Angels provided them food and water, while the wicked were suffering from hunger and thirst." — EW 282.2.

- **Psalm 91:12-16** — "He shall give his angels charge over thee... I will be with him in trouble; I will deliver him." Angels provide food and water for the saints in their solitary retreats while the wicked perish of hunger and thirst.

  > "Angels provided them food and water, while the wicked were suffering from hunger and thirst.... But in this hour of trial the saints were calm and composed, trusting in God and leaning upon His promise that a way of escape would be made for them." — EW 282.2.

  > "The people of God—some in prison cells, some hidden in solitary retreats in the forests and the mountains—still plead for divine protection.... It is now, in the hour of utmost extremity, that the God of Israel will interpose for the deliverance of His chosen." — GC 635.2.

- **Psalm 27:5** — "In the time of trouble he shall hide me in his pavilion... he shall set me up upon a rock." God's hiding of His people through the plagues is a settled covenant promise.

  > "The people of God—some in prison cells, some hidden in solitary retreats in the forests and the mountains—still plead for divine protection.... It is now, in the hour of utmost extremity, that the God of Israel will interpose for the deliverance of His chosen." — GC 635.2.

- **Isaiah 26:20-21** — "Come, my people, enter thou into thy chambers... hide thyself... until the indignation be overpast. For, behold, the LORD cometh out of his place to punish the inhabitants of the earth." The saints are sheltered while wrath is poured out; then the Lord comes forth to judge.

  > "The people of God... still plead for divine protection, while in every quarter companies of armed men, urged on by hosts of evil angels, are preparing for the work of death. It is now, in the hour of utmost extremity, that the God of Israel will interpose for the deliverance of His chosen." — GC 635.2.

- **Zephaniah 2:3** — "Seek ye the LORD, all ye meek of the earth... it may be ye shall be hid in the day of the LORD'S anger." The call to seek righteousness and meekness now is the only preparation for being hid then.

  > "While Jesus had been ministering in the sanctuary, the judgment had been going on for the righteous dead, and then for the righteous living.... The marriage of the Lamb was consummated." — EW 280.1.

### The Death Decree and the Cry Day and Night

- **Revelation 13:15** — The image speaks and decrees death for the loyal. The "general decree" fixes a time when commandment-keepers may be slain — the supreme test of Jacob's trouble.

  > "Though a general decree has fixed the time when commandment keepers may be put to death, their enemies will in some cases anticipate the decree.... But none can pass the mighty guardians stationed about every faithful soul." — GC 631.1.

  > "When the protection of human laws shall be withdrawn from those who honor the law of God, there will be, in different lands, a simultaneous movement for their destruction.... It will be determined to strike in one night a decisive blow." — GC 635.1.

- **Luke 18:7-8** — "Shall not God avenge his own elect, which cry day and night unto him... I tell you that he will avenge them speedily." The saints cry to God under the death decree; He delays long but delivers suddenly. The closing question — "shall he find faith on the earth?" — measures the faith demanded of those who endure.

  > "Some are assailed in their flight from the cities and villages; but the swords raised against them break and fall powerless as a straw. Others are defended by angels in the form of men of war." — GC 631.1.

  > "The people of God... still plead for divine protection.... It is now, in the hour of utmost extremity, that the God of Israel will interpose for the deliverance of His chosen." — GC 635.2.

### The Deliverance — The Voice of God

- **Isaiah 26:21** — "The LORD cometh out of his place to punish the inhabitants of the earth." At the appointed hour of the death decree, God interposes; the deliverance of His people is the destruction of their enemies.

  > "When the protection of human laws shall be withdrawn from those who honor the law of God, there will be, in different lands, a simultaneous movement for their destruction. As the time appointed in the decree draws near, the people will conspire to root out the hated sect." — GC 635.1.

  > "By the people of God a voice, clear and melodious, is heard, saying, 'Look up,' and lifting their eyes to the heavens, they behold the bow of promise.... 'They come! they come! holy, harmless, and undefiled.'" — GC 636.1.

- **Revelation 16:17-18** — The seventh vial brings "a great voice out of the temple of heaven, from the throne, saying, It is done," with "a great earthquake, such as was not since men were upon the earth." At that voice God declares the day and hour of Jesus' coming, delivers the everlasting covenant to His people, and the captives are loosed.

  > "Again a voice, musical and triumphant, is heard, saying: 'They come! they come! holy, harmless, and undefiled. They have kept the word of My patience; they shall walk among the angels;' and the pale, quivering lips of those who have held fast their faith utter a shout of victory." — GC 636.1.

  > "The voice of God is heard from heaven, declaring the day and hour of Jesus' coming, and delivering the everlasting covenant to His people. Like peals of loudest thunder His words roll through the earth." — GC 640.2.

- **Daniel 12:1** — "At that time thy people shall be delivered, every one that shall be found written in the book." The trouble ends as it began — at Michael's word; the same Prince who closed probation now delivers His own.

  > "And at that time shall Michael stand up, the great prince which standeth for the children of thy people... and at that time thy people shall be delivered, every one that shall be found written in the book." — DAR 293.1.

  > "It is now, in the hour of utmost extremity, that the God of Israel will interpose for the deliverance of His chosen.... 'And the Lord shall cause His glorious voice to be heard, and shall show the lighting down of His arm.'" — GC 635.

- **Jeremiah 30:7** — "But he shall be saved out of it." The promise that closes the Jacob's-trouble prophecy is deliverance; the night of anguish breaks into the day of redemption.

  > "The people of God will then be plunged into those scenes of affliction and distress described by the prophet as the time of Jacob's trouble.... 'it is even the time of Jacob's trouble; but he shall be saved out of it.' Jeremiah 30:5-7." — GC 616.1.

  > "And when the blessing is pronounced on those who have honored God by keeping His Sabbath holy, there is a mighty shout of victory." — GC 640.2.

---

# 27. Armageddon and the Seven Last Plagues

> _When the High Priest lays off His mediatorial garments and probation closes, the seven golden vials of God's unmingled wrath — no longer mixed with mercy — are emptied upon the kingdom of the beast: sores, sea to blood, rivers to blood, scorching sun, darkness on the beast's throne, the Euphrates dried for the way of the kings of the east, and the great voice, "It is done." The sixth vial, through the three frog-spirits, gathers all the powers of earth to the battle of that great day — Armageddon — the final muster of the dragon, beast, and false prophet against God and His people, broken at last by the appearing of the King of kings on the white horse. The plagues fall only on those who bear the mark of the beast; the saints, sheltered under the Most High, behold but are not touched. (DAR 638-656; GC 627-657; EW 289-290.)_

### The Sanctuary Scene — Probation Closed, Wrath Unmingled

- **Revelation 15:1** — "Seven angels having the seven last plagues; for in them is filled up the wrath of God." This chapter is "but an introduction to the most terrific judgments of the Almighty," carrying us forward to a new series of events after the close of mercy.

  > "The whole chapter is but an introduction to the most terrific judgments of the Almighty that ever have been, or are to be, visited upon this earth in its present state; namely, the seven last plagues. The most that we here behold is a solemn preparation for the outpouring of these unmixed vials." — DAR 638.

  > "This chapter introduces the seven last plagues, a manifestation of Heaven's unmingled wrath, and the fullness of its measure, for the last generation of the wicked. The work of mercy is then forever past." — DAR 638.1.

- **Revelation 15:5-6** — The temple of the tabernacle of the testimony in heaven is opened, and the seven angels come out "clothed in pure and white linen." The sanctuary ministry is the backdrop: when the work of mercy is past, the plague-angels go forth.

  > "Verse 5 shows that these plagues fall after the close of the ministration in the sanctuary; for the temple is opened before they are poured out. They are given in charge to seven angels, and these angels are clothed in linen pure and white, a fit emblem of the purity of God's righteousness and justice in the infliction of these judgments." — DAR 638.

- **Revelation 15:7** — One of the four beasts gives the angels "seven golden vials full of the wrath of God, who liveth for ever and ever." These vials are Heaven's "unmingled wrath," the fullness of the cup for the last generation of the wicked.

  > "This chapter gives a description of the seven vials of the unmingled wrath of God, and the effects that follow as they are poured upon the earth." — DAR 641.1.

  > "The nation with which He bears long, and which He will not smite until it has filled up the measure of its iniquity in God's account, will finally drink the cup of wrath unmixed with mercy." — GC 627.

- **Revelation 15:8** — The temple is filled with smoke "and no man was able to enter... till the seven plagues... were fulfilled." No intercession remains; Christ has ceased His mediation, and probation has closed before a single vial falls.

  > "The Chronology of the Plagues... is conclusive proof that these judgments are not poured out till after this angel closes his work, and that the very class who hear his warning, and reject it, are the ones to receive the first drops from the overflowing vials of God's indignation." — DAR 641.3.

  > "I was shown that the seven last plagues will be poured out after Jesus leaves the sanctuary." — EW 52.1.

- **Revelation 14:9-11** — Whoever worships the beast and his image "shall drink of the wine of the wrath of God, which is poured out without mixture into the cup of his indignation." The identical mark warned against in the third message marks out the victims of the plagues — proof they fall after probation.

  > "It is poured out upon those who have the mark of the beast, and who worship his image, — the identical work against which the third angel warns us." — DAR 641.3.

  > "When Christ ceases His intercession in the sanctuary, the unmingled wrath threatened against those who worship the beast and his image and receive his mark (Revelation 14:9, 10), will be poured out." — GC 627.

- **Revelation 22:11-12** — "He that is unjust, let him be unjust still... he that is holy, let him be holy still." The irrevocable decree that fixes every destiny is spoken before the plagues fall; then Christ comes "quickly" with His reward.

  > "Then Jesus ceases His intercession in the sanctuary above. He lifts His hands and with a loud voice says, 'It is done;' and all the angelic host lay off their crowns as He makes the solemn announcement: 'He that is unjust, let him be unjust still... and he that is holy, let him be holy still.' Revelation 22:11. Every case has been decided for life or death." — GC 613-614.

> "When Christ ceases His intercession in the sanctuary, the unmingled wrath threatened against those who worship the beast and his image and receive his mark... will be poured out." _(GC 627.3.)_ "The seven last plagues will be poured out after Jesus leaves the sanctuary." _(EW 52.1.)_

### The First Five Vials — Poured on the Kingdom of the Beast

- **Revelation 16:1-2** — The first vial: "a noisome and grievous sore upon the men which had the mark of the beast, and upon them which worshipped his image." The very first plague is aimed precisely at the mark-bearers — the visible identification of who is smitten.

  > "The first went, and poured out his vial upon the earth; and there fell a noisome and grievous sore upon the men which had the mark of the beast, and upon them which worshiped his image." — DAR 641.2.

  > "Says the revelator, in describing those terrific scourges: 'There fell a noisome and grievous sore upon the men which had the mark of the beast, and upon them which worshiped his image.'... Terrible as these inflictions are, God's justice stands fully vindicated." — GC 627-628.

- **Revelation 16:3** — The second vial: the sea "became as the blood of a dead man: and every living soul died in the sea." A literal, terrible judgment, answering to the turning of Egypt's waters to blood.

  > "These plagues are almost identical with those which God inflicted upon the Egyptians as he was about to deliver his people from the yoke of bondage, the literality of which is seldom, if ever, called in question.... A more infectious and deadly substance can scarcely be conceived of than the blood of a dead man." — DAR 644.

  > "The sea 'became as the blood of a dead man: and every living soul died in the sea.'... Terrible as these inflictions are, God's justice stands fully vindicated." — GC 628.

- **Revelation 16:4-7** — The third vial: rivers and fountains "became blood," and the angel of the waters declares it just: "they have shed the blood of saints and prophets, and thou hast given them blood to drink; for they are worthy." Retribution measured to the persecutors.

  > "Such is the description of the terrible retribution for the 'blood of saints' shed by violent hands, which will be given to those who have done, or wish to do, such deeds.... the justice of God will stand vindicated, and his judgments approved. Even the angels are heard exclaiming, Thou art righteous, O Lord, because thou hast judged thus." — DAR 645.

- **Revelation 16:8-9** — The fourth vial: the sun given "power... to scorch men with fire," and men were scorched with great heat, yet "blasphemed the name of God... and they repented not." Drought and famine as Joel describes; still no repentance.

  > "Power is given unto the sun, and he pours upon them a flood of liquid fire, and they are scorched with great heat. Here, as the record runs, their woe first seeks utterance in fearful blasphemy." — DAR 645.4.

  > "In the plague that follows, power is given to the sun 'to scorch men with fire. And men were scorched with great heat.' Verses 8, 9. The prophets thus describe the condition of the earth at this fearful time: 'The land mourneth; ... because the harvest of the field is perished.'" — GC 628.1.

- **Revelation 16:10-11** — The fifth vial: poured "upon the seat of the beast" — the papacy — "and his kingdom was full of darkness; and they gnawed their tongues for pain." Note that those smitten by the first plague are still alive here: the plagues do not at once destroy all their victims.

  > "The plagues do not at once destroy all their victims; for some who were at first smitten with sores, we find still living under the fifth vial, and gnawing their tongues for pain.... It is poured upon the seat of the beast, the papacy." — DAR 646.1.

  > "These plagues are not universal, or the inhabitants of the earth would be wholly cut off. Yet they will be the most awful scourges that have ever been known to mortals." — GC 628.2.

### The Sixth Vial — Euphrates Dried, the Kings of the East, the Gathering to Armageddon

- **Revelation 16:12** — The sixth vial dries "the great river Euphrates... that the way of the kings of the east might be prepared." As the literal Euphrates (and the unmentioned Tigris) is dried under the fourth vial, the Euphrates here symbolizes "the power holding possession of the territory watered by that river" — the Ottoman, or Turkish, empire (cf. Rev. 9:14).

  > "What is the great River Euphrates, upon which this vial is poured out?... One view is that it is the literal River Euphrates in Asia; another is that it is a symbol of the nation occupying the territory through which that river flows. The latter opinion is preferable." — DAR 647.1.

  > "It must be understood figuratively as symbolizing the power holding possession of the territory watered by that river, which is the Ottoman, or Turkish, empire." — DAR 648.2.

  > "In this latter text [Revelation 9:14], all must concede that the Euphrates symbolizes the Turkish power; and being the first and only other occurrence of the word in the Revelation, it may well be considered as governing its use in this book." — DAR 648.3.

- **Revelation 16:12 (the way prepared)** — The consumption of the Turkish power opens the road to "the battle of the great day of God Almighty... near Jerusalem," for the Turks hold Palestine. The drying up removes the obstacle to the final muster.

  > "Where is the battle to be fought? — Near Jerusalem.... But Jerusalem is in the hands of the Turks; they hold possession of the land of Palestine and the sacred sepulchers.... when they shall withdraw their support, and leave it to itself, as they will do under the sixth plague, that symbolic river will be wholly dried up; Turkey will be no more, and the way will be all open for the nations to make their last grand rally to the Holy Land." — DAR 648.6.

- **Revelation 16:13** — "Three unclean spirits like frogs come out of the mouth of the dragon, and out of the mouth of the beast, and out of the mouth of the false prophet." These issue from "three great religious divisions of mankind... Paganism, Catholicism, and Protestantism."

  > "The sources from which these spirits issue, denote that they will work among three great religious divisions of mankind, represented by the dragon, the beast, and the false prophet, or Paganism, Catholicism, and Protestantism." — DAR 650.2.

- **Revelation 16:14** — "The spirits of devils, working miracles, which go forth unto the kings of the earth and of the whole world, to gather them to the battle of that great day of God Almighty." Spiritualism is the fitting agency, deceiving the nations into so unequal a warfare — against the Lord of hosts Himself.

  > "The agency now already abroad in the world, known as modern Spiritualism, is every way a fitting means to be employed in this work.... what fitter instruments could be employed to gather them to so rash and hopeless an enterprise?" — DAR 649.2.

  > "To many it may seem incredible that the nations should be willing to engage in such an unequal warfare as to go up to battle against the Lord of Hosts; but it is one province of these spirits of devils to deceive, for they go forth working miracles, and thereby deceive the kings of the earth, that they should believe a lie." — DAR 650.1.

  > "Little by little he has prepared the way for his masterpiece of deception in the development of spiritualism.... Except those who are kept by the power of God, through faith in His word, the whole world will be swept into the ranks of this delusion." — GC 561.

- **Revelation 16:15** — "Behold, I come as a thief. Blessed is he that watcheth, and keepeth his garments." The warning shows probation has closed and the danger now is apostasy through the working of these lying spirits.

  > "Probation must have closed, and Christ have left his mediatorial position, before the plagues begin to fall.... It will be noticed that this warning is spoken in connection with the working of the spirits." — DAR 650.3.

- **Revelation 16:16** — "He gathered them together into a place called in the Hebrew tongue Armageddon." The Greek pronoun refers back to the kings of verse 14 (so Wesley, Wakefield, the Syriac, and Sawyer render it), and the hills of Megiddo overlooking Esdraelon name the historic field of decisive battle.

  > "If the word them refers to the kings of verse 14, it is certain that no good agency would be made use of to gather them; and if the spirits are referred to by the word he, why is it in the singular number?" — DAR 651.1.

  > "The Syriac Testament reads: 'And they collected them together in a place called in Hebrew Armageddon.'" — DAR 651.6.

  > "Sawyer's translation renders it: 'And they assembled them in the place called in Hebrew Armageddon.'" — DAR 652.1.

  > "The hills of Megiddo, overlooking the plain of Esdraelon, was the place where Barak and Deborah destroyed Sisera's army, and where Josiah was routed by the Egyptian king Pharaoh-Necho." — DAR 652.6.

- **Revelation 17:12-14** — The ten kings "have one mind, and shall give their power and strength unto the beast"; then "these shall make war with the Lamb, and the Lamb shall overcome them: for he is Lord of lords, and King of kings." Armageddon is the confederated powers of earth versus the Lamb — and the Lamb prevails.

  > "These make war with the Lamb. Verse 14. Here we are carried into the future, to the time of the great and final battle; for at this time the Lamb has assumed the title of King of kings and Lord of lords, a title which he does not assume till his second coming." — DAR 661.3.

### The Seventh Vial — "It Is Done" — and the Voice of God

- **Revelation 16:17** — The seventh vial poured "into the air," and "there came a great voice out of the temple of heaven, from the throne, saying, It is done." All is finished: the cup of human guilt is full, the last soul has chosen, the number of the saved is complete, the books are closed.

  > "This plague will envelop equally the habitable globe. It will be universal. The very air will be deadly." — DAR 653.1.

  > "'There came a great voice out of the temple of heaven from the throne, saying, It is done!' Thus all is finished. The cup of human guilt has been filled up. The last soul has availed itself of the plan of salvation. The books are closed. The number of the saved is completed." — DAR 656.1.

- **Revelation 16:18** — "Voices, and thunders, and lightnings; and there was a great earthquake, such as was not since men were upon the earth." It is at midnight, by this voice, that God delivers His people; the firmament opens and shuts and the mountains shake.

  > "And there were voices, and thunders, and lightnings; and there was a great earthquake, such as was not since men were upon the earth, so mighty an earthquake, and so great." — DAR 652.7.

  > "It is at midnight that God manifests His power for the deliverance of His people.... whence comes the voice of God like the sound of many waters, saying: 'It is done.'... That voice shakes the heavens and the earth.... The firmament appears to open and shut.... The mountains shake like a reed in the wind." — GC 636.2-636.3.

- **Revelation 16:19** — "The great city was divided into three parts... and great Babylon came in remembrance before God." The great city — the false and apostate religion of the world — is rent into Paganism, Catholicism, and apostate Protestantism, and Babylon drinks the cup of the fierceness of God's wrath.

  > "The great city is divided into three parts; that is, the three grand divisions of the false and apostate religion of the world (the great city), Paganism, Catholicism, and relapsed Protestantism, seem to be set apart each to receive its appropriate doom.... and great Babylon comes in remembrance before God." — DAR 653.4.

- **Revelation 16:20** — "Every island fled away, and the mountains were not found." The convulsion of the seventh plague tears the very face of the earth.

  > "The whole earth heaves and swells like the waves of the sea. Its surface is breaking up. Its very foundations seem to be giving way. Mountain chains are sinking. Inhabited islands disappear." — GC 637.

- **Revelation 16:21** — "A great hail out of heaven, every stone about the weight of a talent" — about fifty-seven pounds each — and still "men blasphemed God." This is the bitter dregs of the seventh vial, the last instrument of wrath upon the impenitent.

  > "'And there fell upon men a great hail out of heaven.' This is the last instrumentality used in the infliction of punishment upon the wicked, — the bitter dregs of the seventh vial." — DAR 653.5.

  > "'Every stone about the weight of a talent.' A talent, according to various authorities, as a weight, is about fifty-seven pounds avoirdupois.... But mankind, at this time, will have no shelter.... Again the wicked give vent to their woe in blasphemy; for the plague of the hail is 'exceeding great.'" — DAR 654.1.

- **Joel 3:16** — "The Lord also shall roar out of Zion, and utter his voice from Jerusalem; and the heavens and the earth shall shake: but the Lord will be the hope of his people." The voice heard above all the voices of the seventh plague is the voice of God — terror to the wicked, refuge to the saints.

  > "'There were voices.' Above all will be heard the voice of God. 'The Lord also shall roar out of Zion, and utter his voice from Jerusalem... but the Lord will be the hope of his people, and the strength of the children of Israel.' Joel 3:16.... This will cause the great earthquake, such as was not since men were upon the earth." — DAR 653.3.

### The Battle Joined — and Broken by the Coming of Christ

- **Joel 3:9-12** — "Prepare war, wake up the mighty men... Beat your plowshares into swords... Assemble yourselves... let the heathen be wakened, and come up to the valley of Jehoshaphat: for there will I sit to judge all the heathen." The world's powers are summoned to the battle — but the field is God's tribunal, not their victory.

  > "The kings of the East, the nationalities, powers, and kingdoms lying east of Palestine, will act a conspicuous part in the matter; for Joel says in reference to this scene, 'Let the heathen be wakened, and come up to the valley of Jehoshaphat.'" — DAR 648.6.

  > "Now all have made their decisions; the wicked have fully united with Satan in his warfare against God. The time has come for God to vindicate the authority of His downtrodden law. Now the controversy is not alone with Satan, but with men." — GC 656.

- **Joel 3:13-14** — "Put ye in the sickle, for the harvest is ripe... Multitudes, multitudes in the valley of decision: for the day of the Lord is near in the valley of decision." The gathering is a harvest of judgment on a ripened world.

  > "'A noise shall come even to the ends of the earth; for the Lord hath a controversy with the nations, He will plead with all flesh; He will give them that are wicked to the sword.' Jeremiah 25:31. For six thousand years the great controversy has been in progress.... Now all have made their decisions." — GC 656.

- **Zephaniah 3:8** — "Wait ye upon me, saith the Lord, until the day that I rise up to the prey: for my determination is to gather the nations... to pour upon them mine indignation... for all the earth shall be devoured with the fire of my jealousy." God Himself gathers the nations — for judgment, not for their triumph.

  > "For what is the way of these kings to be prepared? Answer: To come up to the battle of the great day of God Almighty. Where is the battle to be fought? — Near Jerusalem. (Joel and Zephaniah.)" — DAR 648.6.

- **Jeremiah 25:31-33** — "The Lord hath a controversy with the nations... and the slain of the Lord shall be at that day from one end of the earth even unto the other." For six thousand years the controversy has run; now the Lord pleads with all flesh by the sword.

  > "'The Lord hath a controversy with the nations, He will plead with all flesh; He will give them that are wicked to the sword.' Jeremiah 25:31. For six thousand years the great controversy has been in progress... The time has come for God to vindicate the authority of His downtrodden law." — GC 656.1.

- **Isaiah 26:21** — "The Lord cometh out of his place to punish the inhabitants of the earth for their iniquity: the earth also shall disclose her blood." The Judge leaves His place; the long-hidden blood of the saints is requited.

  > "'The Lord cometh out of His place to punish the inhabitants of the earth for their iniquity: the earth also shall disclose her blood, and shall no more cover her slain.' Isaiah 26:21." — GC 656.3.

- **Zechariah 14:12** — "Their flesh shall consume away while they stand upon their feet, and their eyes shall consume away in their holes, and their tongue shall consume away in their mouth." The plague upon all the people that have fought against Jerusalem — the consuming of the assembled enemy.

  > "'And this shall be the plague wherewith the Lord will smite all the people that have fought against Jerusalem; Their flesh shall consume away while they stand upon their feet, and their eyes shall consume away in their holes, and their tongue shall consume away in their mouth.'... In the mad strife of their own fierce passions, and by the awful outpouring of God's unmingled wrath, fall the wicked inhabitants of the earth." — GC 656.3.

- **Revelation 19:11-13** — "Heaven opened, and behold a white horse; and he that sat upon him was called Faithful and True... clothed with a vesture dipped in blood... his name is called The Word of God." Christ Himself, no longer Priest but Conqueror, rides forth to end Armageddon.

  > "With verse 11 a new scene is introduced. We are here carried back to the second coming of Christ, this time under the symbol of a warrior riding forth to battle. Why is he represented thus? — Because he is going forth to war, — to meet 'the kings of the earth and their armies,' and this would be the only proper character in which to represent him on such a mission. His vesture is dipped in blood." — DAR 684.

- **Revelation 19:14-16** — His armies follow on white horses; out of His mouth "goeth a sharp sword, that with it he should smite the nations," and on His vesture is written "KING OF KINGS, AND LORD OF LORDS." The same title by which He overcame in chapter 17:14.

  > "Christ has at this time closed his mediatorial work, and laid off his priestly robes for kingly attire; for he has on his vesture and on his thigh a name written, King of kings and Lord of lords. This is in harmony with the character in which he here appears." — DAR 685.

- **Revelation 19:17-18** — The angel in the sun calls the fowls to "the supper of the great God," to eat "the flesh of kings, and... captains, and... mighty men." The mustered armies of Armageddon become the slain feast of the field.

  > "The angel standing in the sun, and issuing his call from thence to the fowls of heaven to come to the supper of the great God, denotes that this proclamation will go wherever the sun's rays fall upon this earth.... Thus, while the saints are partaking of the marriage supper of the Lamb, the wicked in their own persons furnish a great supper for the fowls of heaven." — DAR 685.

- **Revelation 19:19-21** — "The beast, and the kings of the earth, and their armies, gathered together to make war against him" — the same confederacy of the sixth vial — are broken: "the beast was taken, and with him the false prophet... cast alive into a lake of fire," and "the remnant were slain with the sword of him that sat upon the horse." Armageddon ends not in battle but in the brightness of His coming.

  > "The fact that these are cast alive into the lake of fire, shows that these powers will not pass away and be succeeded by others, but be living powers at the second advent of Christ." — DAR 685.

  > "These are slain by the sword of Him that sits upon the horse, which sword proceeds out of his mouth. This sword is doubtless what is spoken of elsewhere as 'the spirit of his mouth' and 'the breath of his lips,' with which the Lord shall slay the wicked at his appearing and kingdom." — DAR 686.

- **Revelation 6:15-17** — "The kings of the earth, and the great men... hid themselves... and said to the mountains and rocks, Fall on us, and hide us from the face of him that sitteth on the throne, and from the wrath of the Lamb: for the great day of his wrath is come; and who shall be able to stand?" The mark-bearers' terror at the King's appearing.

  > "The day which they thought never would come, has at last taken them as in a snare; and the involuntary language of their anguished hearts is, 'The great day of his wrath is come, and who shall be able to stand?'... Those who will not now pray to God in penitence, will then pray to the rocks and mountains in despair." — DAR 434.

- **Revelation 11:18** — "The nations were angry, and thy wrath is come... and shouldest destroy them which destroy the earth." The seven last plagues fill up the wrath of this present generation.

  > "'And Thy Wrath Is Come.' — The wrath of God for the present generation is filled up in the seven last plagues (chapter 15:1), which consequently must here be referred to, and which are soon to be poured out upon the earth." — DAR 506.3.

### The Saints Sheltered — Plagues Fall Only on the Mark-Bearers

- **Daniel 12:1** — "At that time shall Michael stand up... and there shall be a time of trouble, such as never was... and at that time thy people shall be delivered, every one that shall be found written in the book." Michael standing up = the close of probation; the plagues are the time of trouble, yet the sealed are delivered.

  > "His priestly robes are laid aside for royal vesture. The work of mercy is done, and the probation of our race is ended.... And from that time on... the nations are broken as with a rod of iron, and dashed in pieces like a potter's vessel, by a time of trouble such as never was, a series of judgments unparalleled in the world's history." — DAR 295.3.

  > "When the third angel's message closes, mercy no longer pleads for the guilty inhabitants of the earth.... all who have proved themselves loyal to the divine precepts have received 'the seal of the living God.' Then Jesus ceases His intercession in the sanctuary above." — GC 613.

- **Psalm 91:7-10** — "A thousand shall fall at thy side, and ten thousand at thy right hand; but it shall not come nigh thee... There shall no evil befall thee, neither shall any plague come nigh thy dwelling." The express promise of the saints' immunity while the overflowing scourge passes over.

  > "But what is the condition of the saints while the 'overflowing scourge' is passing over? They are the special subjects of God's protection, without whose notice not a sparrow falls to the ground. Many are the promises which come crowding in to afford them comfort, summarily contained in the beautiful and expressive language of the 91st psalm." — DAR 656.2.

  > "'A thousand shall fall at thy side, and ten thousand at thy right hand; but it shall not come nigh thee.... there shall no evil befall thee, neither shall any plague come nigh thy dwelling.' Psalms 91:2-10." — DAR 656.3.

- **Isaiah 26:20** — "Come, my people, enter thou into thy chambers... hide thyself as it were for a little moment, until the indignation be overpast." The hiding of God's people during the outpouring of wrath.

  > "It is now, in the hour of utmost extremity, that the God of Israel will interpose for the deliverance of His chosen.... a dense blackness, deeper than the darkness of the night, falls upon the earth. Then a rainbow, shining with the glory from the throne of God, spans the heavens and seems to encircle each praying company." — GC 635-636.

- **Revelation 15:2** — They "that had gotten the victory over the beast, and over his image, and over his mark" stand on the sea of glass, singing "the song of Moses... and the song of the Lamb." The victors are seen safe before the vials are even poured — a deliverance anticipated.

  > "The prophet is permitted to anticipate a little in verses 2-4, and behold them as victors upon the sea of glass as it were mingled with fire, or sparkling and refulgent with the glory of God, singing the song of Moses and the Lamb.... we have here indubitable proof... that the saints are taken to heaven to receive a portion of their reward." — DAR 639.1.

  > "The song the victors sing, the song of Moses and the Lamb, given here in epitome in these words: 'Great and marvelous are thy works, Lord God Almighty; just and true are thy ways, thou King of saints,' is a song of infinite grandeur." — DAR 640.1.

- **Joel 3:16 (latter clause)** — "But the Lord will be the hope of his people, and the strength of the children of Israel." The same voice that shakes heaven and earth is the refuge of the redeemed.

  > "'There were voices.' Above all will be heard the voice of God.... 'but the Lord will be the hope of his people, and the strength of the children of Israel.' Joel 3:16." — DAR 653.3.

  > "By the people of God a voice, clear and melodious, is heard, saying, 'Look up,' and lifting their eyes to the heavens, they behold the bow of promise.... 'They come! they come! holy, harmless, and undefiled. They have kept the word of My patience; they shall walk among the angels.'" — GC 636.

> "It is at midnight that God manifests His power for the deliverance of His people." _(GC 636.2.)_ When the plagues end, "the wrath of God in the seven last plagues had been visited upon the inhabitants of the earth," the wicked are destroyed, and "the earth looked like a desolate wilderness." _(EW 289.3; EW 290.1.)_

### The Seven Last Plagues at a Glance

| Vial           | Poured Upon                | Effect                                                  | Pioneer Note                                    |
| -------------- | -------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------- |
| 1st (16:2)     | the earth                  | noisome, grievous sore                                  | falls on mark-bearers (DAR 641.3)               |
| 2nd (16:3)     | the sea                    | becomes blood; all sea life dies                        | literal judgment (DAR 644)                      |
| 3rd (16:4-7)   | rivers, fountains          | become blood — measured retribution                     | "given them blood to drink" (DAR 645)           |
| 4th (16:8-9)   | the sun                    | scorching heat; men blaspheme                           | drought, famine (GC 628.1)                      |
| 5th (16:10-11) | seat of the beast (papacy) | darkness; pain; no repentance                           | first plague's victims still living (DAR 646.1) |
| 6th (16:12-16) | river Euphrates (Ottoman)  | dried; kings of east; frog-spirits gather to Armageddon | Spiritualism deceives (DAR 647-651)             |
| 7th (16:17-21) | the air                    | "It is done"; earthquake; talent hail; Babylon's cup    | bitter dregs of wrath (DAR 653-654)             |

---

# 28. The Second Coming of Christ in Glory

> The blessed hope is no secret rapture and no spiritual sentiment, but the personal, literal, visible, and audible return of the same Jesus who ascended. He comes in the clouds with all the holy angels; every eye shall see Him; the dead in Christ rise incorruptible, the living righteous are changed and caught up; and the wicked are slain by the brightness of His coming. This one glorious advent ends the great controversy and gathers the redeemed home. (DAR 684-686; GC 640-645; EW 15-18, 286-295.)

### He comes in person — the same Jesus, in like manner

- **Acts 1:9-11** — A cloud received Him out of their sight, and the two angels declared, "This same Jesus... shall so come in like manner as ye have seen him go." As truly, bodily, and visibly as He went up, so He returns — no spiritualizing allowed.

  > "Jesus rides forth as a mighty conqueror. Not now a 'Man of Sorrows,' to drink the bitter cup of shame and woe, He comes, victor in heaven and earth, to judge the living and the dead.… As the living cloud comes still nearer, every eye beholds the Prince of life." — GC 640-645.

- **John 14:1-3** — "I will come again, and receive you unto myself; that where I am, there ye may be also." The hope of the church is a literal return to gather His own to the Father's house.

  > "Soon there appears in the east a small black cloud, about half the size of a man's hand. It is the cloud which surrounds the Saviour.… The people of God know this to be the sign of the Son of man." — GC 640.

- **Hebrews 9:28** — "Unto them that look for him shall he appear the second time without sin unto salvation." His first coming was to bear sin; the second, to save them that wait.

  > "He comes to raise and gather his saints to him in the air. 'As in Adam all die, so in Christ shall all be made alive; but every man in his own order—Christ the first fruit, afterwards they that are Christ's at his coming.'" — MWV2 19.

- **Titus 2:11-14** — Believers are "looking for that blessed hope, and the glorious appearing of the great God and our Saviour Jesus Christ." The appearing is "glorious" — not invisible, not secret.

  > "No human pen can portray the scene; no mortal mind is adequate to conceive its splendor. 'His glory covered the heavens, and the earth was full of His praise. And His brightness was as the light.'… His countenance outshines the dazzling brightness of the noonday sun." — GC 640-645.

- **Job 19:25-27** — "I know that my redeemer liveth... in my flesh shall I see God... mine eyes shall behold, and not another." The oldest hope of the patriarchs is a personal, eyes-shall-see-Him advent.

  > "In the beginning, man was created in the likeness of God, not only in character, but in form and feature.… He will change our vile bodies and fashion them like unto His glorious body.… Christ's faithful ones will appear in 'the beauty of the Lord our God,' in mind and soul and body reflecting the perfect image of their Lord." — GC 644.

### Every eye shall see Him — visible and audible, not secret

- **Revelation 1:7** — "Behold, he cometh with clouds; and every eye shall see him, and they also which pierced him: and all kindreds of the earth shall wail because of him." Universal sight refutes every secret-coming theory.

  > "Before His presence 'all faces are turned into paleness;' upon the rejecters of God's mercy falls the terror of eternal despair.… The men who smote and spit upon the Prince of life now turn from His piercing gaze and seek to flee from the overpowering glory of His presence. Those who drove the nails through His hands and feet, the soldier who pierced His side, behold these marks with terror and remorse." — GC 641-643.

- **Matthew 24:27** — "As the lightning cometh out of the east, and shineth even unto the west; so shall also the coming of the Son of man be." Open and flashing across the whole sky — the opposite of a hidden, in-the-desert appearing.

  > "The living righteous are changed 'in a moment, in the twinkling of an eye.' At the voice of God they were glorified; now they are made immortal and with the risen saints are caught up to meet their Lord in the air." — GC 645.

- **Matthew 24:30** — "They shall see the Son of man coming in the clouds of heaven with power and great glory," and all the tribes of earth mourn.

  > "There are those who mocked Christ in His humiliation. With thrilling power come to their minds the Sufferer's words, when, adjured by the high priest, He solemnly declared: 'Hereafter shall ye see the Son of man sitting on the right hand of power, and coming in the clouds of heaven.' Matthew 26:64. Now they behold Him in His glory." — GC 641-643.

- **Mark 13:26** — "Then shall they see the Son of man coming in the clouds with great power and glory."

  > "The King of kings descends upon the cloud, wrapped in flaming fire. The heavens are rolled together as a scroll, the earth trembles before Him, and every mountain and island is moved out of its place." — GC 641.

- **Luke 21:27-28** — "Then shall they see the Son of man coming in a cloud with power and great glory.... lift up your heads; for your redemption draweth nigh." For the righteous it is deliverance, not dread.

  > "The righteous cry with trembling: 'Who shall be able to stand?'… Then the voice of Jesus is heard, saying: 'My grace is sufficient for you.' The faces of the righteous are lighted up, and joy fills every heart." — GC 640-642.

- **Daniel 7:13-14** — "One like the Son of man came with the clouds of heaven," receiving an everlasting dominion that shall not pass away — the same cloud-coming Christ described to the high priest.

  > "VERSE 13. I saw in the night visions, and, behold, one like the Son of man came with the clouds of heaven, and came to the Ancient of days, and they brought him near before him.… And there was given him dominion, and glory, and a kingdom, that all people, nations, and languages should serve him: his dominion is an everlasting dominion, which shall not pass away." — DAR 122.

### With all the holy angels and the glory of the Father

- **Matthew 25:31** — "When the Son of man shall come in his glory, and all the holy angels with him, then shall he sit upon the throne of his glory."

  > "With anthems of celestial melody the holy angels, a vast, unnumbered throng, attend Him on His way. The firmament seems filled with radiant forms—'ten thousand times ten thousand, and thousands of thousands.'" — GC 640.

- **Matthew 16:27** — "The Son of man shall come in the glory of his Father with his angels; and then he shall reward every man according to his works."

  > "Then I saw a very great number of angels bring from the city glorious crowns—a crown for every saint, with his name written thereon. As Jesus called for the crowns, angels presented them to Him, and with His own right hand, the lovely Jesus placed the crowns on the heads of the saints." — EW 288.

- **Matthew 24:31** — "He shall send his angels with a great sound of a trumpet, and they shall gather together his elect from the four winds." The trumpet and the angelic gathering are public, not silent.

  > "Then Jesus' silver trumpet sounded, as He descended on the cloud, wrapped in flames of fire.… in the same moment we were changed and caught up together with them to meet the Lord in the air." — EW 16.

  > "Angels 'gather together His elect from the four winds, from one end of heaven to the other.' Little children are borne by holy angels to their mothers' arms." — GC 645.

- **Mark 13:27** — He sends His angels to "gather together his elect from the four winds, from the uttermost part of the earth to the uttermost part of heaven."

  > "The 144,000 shouted, 'Alleluia!' as they recognized their friends who had been torn from them by death, and in the same moment we were changed and caught up together with them to meet the Lord in the air." — EW 16.

- **Jude 1:14** — Enoch prophesied, "Behold, the Lord cometh with ten thousands of his saints." The myriads attend the King in His triumph.

  > "The firmament seems filled with radiant forms—'ten thousand times ten thousand, and thousands of thousands.' No human pen can portray the scene; no mortal mind is adequate to conceive its splendor." — GC 640.

- **Revelation 19:11-16** — Heaven opened, the white-horse Warrior called Faithful and True, crowned with many crowns, "KING OF KINGS, AND LORD OF LORDS," the armies of heaven following Him. Christ comes this time as conquering King, His mediatorial work closed and kingly attire on.

  > "We are here carried back to the second coming of Christ, this time under the symbol of a warrior riding forth to battle. Why is he represented thus? — Because he is going forth to war, — to meet 'the kings of the earth and their armies'.… The armies of heaven, the angels of God, follow him." — DAR 684-685.

### The resurrection of the righteous and the translation of the living

- **1 Thessalonians 4:15-18** — "The Lord himself shall descend from heaven with a shout, with the voice of the archangel, and with the trump of God: and the dead in Christ shall rise first," then the living are "caught up together with them in the clouds, to meet the Lord in the air." The shout, voice, and trump make it audible; the catching-up is the only "rapture" Scripture knows — at the visible coming.

  > "He gazed on the graves of the sleeping saints, then raised His eyes and hands to heaven, and cried, 'Awake! awake! awake! ye that sleep in the dust, and arise.'… The graves opened, and the dead came up clothed with immortality." — EW 16.

  > "Amid the reeling of the earth, the flash of lightning, and the roar of thunder, the voice of the Son of God calls forth the sleeping saints.… 'Awake, awake, awake, ye that sleep in the dust, and arise!' Throughout the length and breadth of the earth the dead shall hear that voice, and they that hear shall live." — GC 644-645.

- **1 Corinthians 15:51-54** — "We shall not all sleep, but we shall all be changed, in a moment, in the twinkling of an eye, at the last trump.... the dead shall be raised incorruptible, and we shall be changed." Mortality puts on immortality; death is swallowed up in victory.

  > "He comes to raise and gather his saints to him in the air. 'As in Adam all die, so in Christ shall all be made alive; but every man in his own order—Christ the first fruit, afterwards they that are Christ's at his coming.' 1 Corinthians 15:22, 23." — MWV2 19.

  > "The resurrection of the saints is first as it respects order and time.… 'Christ the first fruits, then afterward they that are Christ's at his coming. Then cometh the end.'… the blessed and holy are raised a thousand years before the rest of the dead." — MWV2 29.

- **1 Corinthians 15:22-23** — "Every man in his own order—Christ the firstfruit, afterwards they that are Christ's at his coming." Christ comes to raise and gather His saints to Him in the air.

  > "He comes to raise and gather his saints to him in the air.… 'For the Lord himself shall descend from heaven with a shout, with the voice of the archangel, and with the trump of God, and the dead in Christ shall rise first. Then we, which are alive and remain, shall be caught up together with them in the clouds to meet the Lord in the air.'" — MWV2 19.

- **Philippians 3:20-21** — We "look for the Saviour," who "shall change our vile body, that it may be fashioned like unto his glorious body." Translation conforms the living saint to Christ's glorified form.

  > "The graves opened, and the dead came up clothed with immortality.… in the same moment we were changed and caught up together with them to meet the Lord in the air." — EW 16-17.

- **Colossians 3:4** — "When Christ, who is our life, shall appear, then shall ye also appear with him in glory."

  > "The living saints are changed in a moment, in the twinkling of an eye, and caught up with the risen ones, and together they meet their Lord in the air. Oh, what a glorious meeting! Friends whom death had separated were united, never more to part." — EW 287.

- **1 John 3:2** — "When he shall appear, we shall be like him; for we shall see him as he is."

  > "Those bodies that had gone down into the grave bearing the marks of disease and death came up in immortal health and vigor. The living saints are changed in a moment, in the twinkling of an eye." — EW 287.

- **Isaiah 25:9** — "Lo, this is our God; we have waited for him, and he will save us... we will be glad and rejoice in his salvation." The waiting people break into triumph at His appearing.

  > "In the midst of their terror they hear the voices of the saints in joyful strains exclaiming: 'Lo, this is our God; we have waited for Him, and He will save us.' Isaiah 25:9." — GC 644.

### The wicked slain by the brightness of His coming

- **2 Thessalonians 1:7-10** — The Lord Jesus is "revealed from heaven with his mighty angels, in flaming fire taking vengeance on them that know not God," who are "punished with everlasting destruction from the presence of the Lord," while He comes "to be glorified in his saints." The same coming that rewards the just destroys the impenitent.

  > "The destruction of the living wicked at the second coming of Christ, at the beginning of the thousand years, furnishes a scene on a smaller scale, similar… to what takes place at the close of that period. Hence in the verses before us we have this mention of both the winepress of wrath and the lake of fire." — DAR 684-685.

  > "Before His presence 'all faces are turned into paleness;' upon the rejecters of God's mercy falls the terror of eternal despair. 'The heart melteth, and the knees smite together, ... and the faces of them all gather blackness.'" — GC 641-642.

- **Revelation 6:14-17** — The heaven departs as a scroll, every mountain and island moved; kings, great men, and bondmen alike hide in the rocks crying, "Fall on us, and hide us from the face of him that sitteth on the throne, and from the wrath of the Lamb: for the great day of his wrath is come; and who shall be able to stand?" The lost dread the very face the saints long to see.

  > "'And the kings of the earth, and the great men, and the rich men… hid themselves in the dens and in the rocks of the mountains; and said to the mountains and rocks, Fall on us, and hide us from the face of Him that sitteth on the throne, and from the wrath of the Lamb.'… The wicked pray to be buried beneath the rocks of the mountains rather than meet the face of Him whom they have despised and rejected." — GC 642.

- **Revelation 19:17-21** — The beast and false prophet (the two-horned beast of chapter 13) are taken and cast into the lake of fire, and the remnant "slain with the sword of him that sat upon the horse, which sword proceeded out of his mouth" — the word of His mouth. The persecuting powers meet their end at the advent.

  > "The beast and false prophet are taken.… The fact that these are cast alive into the lake of fire, shows that these powers will not pass away and be succeeded by others, but be living powers at the second advent of Christ." — DAR 685-686.

- **2 Thessalonians 2:8** — That Wicked one "the Lord shall consume with the spirit of his mouth, and shall destroy with the brightness of his coming." The brightness itself slays.

  > "These are slain by the sword of Him that sits upon the horse, which sword proceeds out of his mouth. This sword is doubtless what is spoken of elsewhere as 'the spirit of his mouth' and 'the breath of his lips,' with which the Lord shall slay the wicked at his appearing and kingdom." — DAR 686.

- **2 Peter 3:10** — "The day of the Lord will come as a thief in the night; in the which the heavens shall pass away with a great noise, and the elements shall melt with fervent heat." The advent shakes the very frame of nature.

  > "The heavens are rolled together as a scroll, the earth trembles before Him, and every mountain and island is moved out of its place. 'Our God shall come, and shall not keep silence: a fire shall devour before Him, and it shall be very tempestuous round about Him.'" — GC 641-642.

### The signs that herald it and the cloud that announces it

- **Matthew 24:29-31** — "Immediately after the tribulation of those days shall the sun be darkened, and the moon shall not give her light, and the stars shall fall from heaven," then "the sign of the Son of man" appears — the small cloud growing into the glory-cloud of the returning King.

  > "Soon our eyes were drawn to the east, for a small black cloud had appeared, about half as large as a man's hand, which we all knew was the sign of the Son of man. We all in solemn silence gazed on the cloud as it drew nearer and became lighter, glorious, and still more glorious, till it was a great white cloud." — EW 15.

  > "Soon there appears in the east a small black cloud, about half the size of a man's hand.… until it is a great white cloud, its base a glory like consuming fire, and above it the rainbow of the covenant." — GC 640.

- **Revelation 14:14** — "A white cloud, and upon the cloud one sat like unto the Son of man, having on his head a golden crown" — the reaper come to gather the harvest of the earth.

  > "The 13th chapter closed with the people of God, a small and apparently weak and defenseless company, in deadly conflict with the mightiest powers of earth.… The very same company standing on Mount Zion with the Lamb, — a victorious company, harping on symphonious harps their triumph in the court of heaven." — DAR 582.

  > "His hair was white and curly and lay on His shoulders; and upon His head were many crowns. His feet had the appearance of fire; in His right hand was a sharp sickle." — EW 16.

- **Zechariah 14:4-5** — "His feet shall stand in that day upon the mount of Olives... and the LORD my God shall come, and all the saints with thee." The descent is to the very place of His ascension.

  > "With Jesus at our head we all descended from the city down to this earth, on a great and mighty mountain, which could not bear Jesus up, and it parted asunder, and there was a mighty plain." — EW 17-18.

- **Matthew 24:36-37** — "Of that day and hour knoweth no man... but as the days of Noe were, so shall also the coming of the Son of man be" — sudden upon a careless world, yet sure as the flood.

  > "He comes to raise and gather his saints to him in the air. 'As in Adam all die, so in Christ shall all be made alive; but every man in his own order—Christ the first fruit, afterwards they that are Christ's at his coming.'" — MWV2 19.

- **Revelation 22:12** — "Behold, I come quickly; and my reward is with me, to give every man according as his work shall be." He brings the reward with Him — the saints are not glorified until He appears.

  > "As Jesus called for the crowns, angels presented them to Him, and with His own right hand, the lovely Jesus placed the crowns on the heads of the saints.… every voice was raised in grateful, happy praise." — EW 288.

---

# 29. The Millennium and the Two Resurrections

> _The thousand years of Revelation 20 are bounded by two resurrections a thousand years apart. At the second coming the righteous dead rise (the first resurrection) and the living saints are translated — all to heaven; the wicked are slain by the brightness of His coming, and the earth is left a desolate, depopulated waste, with Satan "bound" by sheer circumstance, having no one left to tempt. The redeemed sit in judgment with Christ above for a thousand years. Then the wicked dead rise (the second resurrection), the Holy City descends, the deceived nations compass the camp of the saints, fire comes down from God — the second death — and the cleansing fire purifies the earth for the new creation, where the great controversy is ended forever. (DAR 687-728; GC 653-678; EW 51-54, 289-295.)_

### The Two Resurrections — a Thousand Years Apart

- **John 5:28-29** — "All that are in the graves shall hear his voice, and shall come forth; they that have done good, unto the resurrection of life; and they that have done evil, unto the resurrection of damnation." Two distinct resurrections to two distinct destinies — the textual key to Revelation 20.

  > "Two Resurrections. — "The rest of the dead lived not again till the thousand years were finished." Whatever may be said to the contrary, no language could more plainly prove two resurrections; the first, a resurrection of the righteous at the commencement of the thousand years; and the second, that of the wicked at the end of that period." — DAR 693.4.

  > "But a distinction is made between the two classes that are brought forth. "All that are in the graves shall hear His voice, and shall come forth; they that have done good, unto the resurrection of life; and they that have done evil, unto the resurrection of damnation."" — GC 544.

- **Revelation 20:5-6** — "The rest of the dead lived not again until the thousand years were finished. This is the first resurrection. Blessed and holy is he that hath part in the first resurrection." The righteous rise first; the wicked wait the thousand years — "no language could more plainly prove two resurrections."

  > "To avoid the doctrine of two resurrections, some claim that the passage, "But the rest of the dead lived not again until the thousand years were finished," is an interpolation… But the criticism is not true. All scholarship is against it. The Revised Version retains the passage." — DAR 693.3-693.4.

- **Revelation 20:4** — "They lived and reigned with Christ a thousand years" — the souls of them beheaded for Jesus, who had not worshipped the beast nor received his mark. The first resurrection raises the faithful of all ages, including the martyrs against Rome.

  > "From that general assembly John then selects two classes as worthy of especial attention: first, the martyrs, those who had been beheaded for the witness of Jesus; and secondly, those who had not worshiped the beast and his image. … As one class, John saw the martyrs, and as another, he saw those who had not worshiped the beast and his image." — DAR 692.1-693.2.

- **1 Thessalonians 4:16-17** — "The Lord himself shall descend... and the dead in Christ shall rise first: then we which are alive and remain shall be caught up together with them in the clouds." The first resurrection and the translation of the living happen together at the advent — all caught up to meet the Lord.

  > "On such as have part in the first resurrection, the second death will have no power. They can pass unharmed through the elements which destroy the wicked like chaff." — DAR 693.

  > "After the saints are changed to immortality and caught up together with Jesus, after they receive their harps, their robes, and their crowns, and enter the city, Jesus and the saints sit in judgment." — EW 52.2.

- **1 Corinthians 15:51-54** — "We shall not all sleep, but we shall all be changed, in a moment, in the twinkling of an eye, at the last trump... the dead shall be raised incorruptible, and we shall be changed." The righteous dead and living together "put on immortality" — "death is swallowed up in victory."

  > "From the prison house of death they come, clothed with immortal glory, crying: "O death, where is thy sting? O grave, where is thy victory?" … And the living righteous and the risen saints unite their voices in a long, glad shout of victory." — GC 644.

  > "The living saints are changed in a moment, in the twinkling of an eye, and caught up with the risen ones, and together they meet their Lord in the air." — EW 287.1.

- **1 Corinthians 15:23** — "Christ the firstfruits; afterward they that are Christ's at his coming." Resurrection is "every man in his own order" — Christ first, His people at His coming, a definite sequence, not a general rising of all at once.

  > "This has continued until the glorious truth of the resurrection has been almost wholly obscured and lost sight of by the Christian world. … "The dead are already passed into glory. They do not wait for the trump for their judgment and blessedness."" — GC 547.

- **Luke 14:14** — Thou "shalt be recompensed at the resurrection of the just." Christ Himself names a separate "resurrection of the just" — the first resurrection, the reward of the righteous.

  > "Nowhere in the Sacred Scriptures is found the statement that the righteous go to their reward or the wicked to their punishment at death. … They are represented as sleeping until the resurrection. … They sleep; they are awakened by the trump of God to a glorious immortality." — GC 549-550.

- **Job 19:25-26** — "I know that my redeemer liveth, and that he shall stand at the latter day upon the earth: and... in my flesh shall I see God." The patriarch's hope was a bodily resurrection at the latter day — the saints' confidence in the first resurrection.

  > "The patriarch Job in the night of his affliction exclaimed with unshaken trust: "I know that my Redeemer liveth, and that He shall stand at the latter day upon the earth: ... in my flesh shall I see God: whom I shall see for myself, and mine eyes shall behold, and not another."" — GC 299.

### The Wicked Slain at the Advent — the Earth Left Desolate

- **2 Thessalonians 2:8** — "That Wicked... whom the Lord shall consume with the spirit of his mouth, and shall destroy with the brightness of his coming." The living wicked are not converted but consumed at the appearing of Christ.

  > "At the coming of Christ the wicked are blotted from the face of the whole earth—consumed with the spirit of His mouth and destroyed by the brightness of His glory. Christ takes His people to the City of God, and the earth is emptied of its inhabitants." — GC 657.1.

- **Malachi 4:1** — "The day cometh, that shall burn as an oven; and all the proud, yea, and all that do wickedly, shall be stubble... it shall leave them neither root nor branch." The wicked are burned up root and branch — destroyed, not eternally tortured.

  > "The wicked receive their recompense in the earth. … In the cleansing flames the wicked are at last destroyed, root and branch—Satan the root, his followers the branches. The full penalty of the law has been visited." — GC 673.1.

  > "Said the angel, "Satan is the root, his children are the branches. They are now consumed root and branch. They have died an everlasting death. They are never to have a resurrection, and God will have a clean universe."" — EW 295.1.
