- **Jeremiah 25:33** — "The slain of the LORD shall be at that day from one end of the earth even unto the other end... they shall not be lamented, neither gathered, nor buried; they shall be dung upon the ground." The earth is left strewn with the unburied dead — no living wicked remain to bury them.

  > "At the coming of Christ the wicked are blotted from the face of the whole earth… and the earth is emptied of its inhabitants." — GC 657.

  > "The wicked had been destroyed, and their dead bodies were lying upon its surface. … The earth seemed to be deluged with blood, and dead bodies were…" — EW 289.3.

- **Isaiah 24:1** — "The LORD maketh the earth empty, and maketh it waste, and turneth it upside down, and scattereth abroad the inhabitants thereof." The earth is emptied of its inhabitants at the coming of the Lord.

  > "Christ takes His people to the City of God, and the earth is emptied of its inhabitants. "Behold, the Lord maketh the earth empty, and maketh it waste, and turneth it upside down, and scattereth abroad the inhabitants thereof."" — GC 657.1.

- **Isaiah 24:3-6** — "The land shall be utterly emptied, and utterly spoiled... the earth is defiled under the inhabitants thereof; because they have transgressed the laws, changed the ordinance, broken the everlasting covenant... the inhabitants of the earth are burned, and few men left." The curse devours the earth because of the broken law — a depopulated globe.

  > ""Because they have transgressed the laws, changed the ordinance, broken the everlasting covenant. Therefore hath the curse devoured the earth, and they that dwell therein are desolate: therefore the inhabitants of the earth are burned." Isaiah 24:1, 3, 5, 6." — GC 657.1.

  > "But we cannot believe that the saints' inheritance will ever be thus marred, or that the fair plains of the earth made new will ever be soiled with the polluting tread of the resuscitated wicked." — DAR 695.

- **Jeremiah 4:23-26** — "I beheld the earth, and, lo, it was without form, and void; and the heavens, and they had no light... and, lo, there was no man, and all the birds of the heavens were fled... the fruitful place was a wilderness, and all the cities thereof were broken down." The millennial earth returns to a chaos like the beginning — "without form, and void," no man upon it.

  > "Prophecy teaches that it will be brought back, partially at least, to this condition. … "I beheld the earth, and, lo, it was without form, and void; … I beheld, and, lo, there was no man, and all the birds of the heavens were fled." Jeremiah 4:23-26." — GC 658.2-658.3.

  > "Its use seems to be such as to show that the word denotes any place of darkness, desolation, and death. … "darkness was upon the face of the deep." The word there rendered deep is the same word that is here rendered bottomless pit." — DAR 690.3.

- **Jeremiah 4:27** — "The whole land shall be desolate; yet will I not make a full end." Desolate but not annihilated — reserved a thousand years, then renewed.

  > "The revelator foretells the banishment of Satan and the condition of chaos and desolation to which the earth is to be reduced, and he declares that this condition will exist for a thousand years." — GC 658.2.

  > "But we cannot believe that the saints' inheritance will ever be thus marred, or that the fair plains of the earth made new will ever be soiled with the polluting tread of the resuscitated wicked; for besides outraging all ideas of propriety, there is no scripture from which even an inference can be drawn to support it." — DAR 695.

### Satan Bound a Thousand Years — Bound by Circumstance

- **Revelation 20:1-3** — An angel binds "the dragon, that old serpent... and bound him a thousand years, and cast him into the bottomless pit... that he should deceive the nations no more." Satan is shut up in the abyss of a depopulated, ruined earth.

  > "And I saw an angel come down from heaven, having the key of the bottomless pit and a great chain in his hand. … And he laid hold on the dragon, that old serpent, which is the devil, and Satan, and bound him a thousand years, And cast him into the bottomless pit, and shut him up, and set a seal upon him, that he should deceive the nations no more." — DAR 687.1-690.1.

- **Revelation 20:7** — "When the thousand years are expired, Satan shall be loosed out of his prison." His "binding" is the removing of his subjects beyond his reach; his "loosing" is their being raised again so he can resume his work — bound and loosed by circumstance, not by literal chain.

  > "The "binding" of Satan means simply the placing beyond his reach of the subjects upon whom he works, and his being "loosed" means their being brought again, by a resurrection, to a position where he can again exercise his power upon them." — DAR 691.1-691.2.

- **Jeremiah 4:23** & **Genesis 1:2** — The "bottomless pit" (abyss) answers to the earth "without form, and void" — the very words of the creation chaos. Satan's prison is this dark, desolate, lifeless earth.

  > "That the expression "bottomless pit" represents the earth in a state of confusion and darkness is evident from other scriptures. Concerning the condition of the earth "in the beginning," the Bible record says that it "was without form, and void; and darkness was upon the face of the deep."" — GC 658.3.

  > "The original word signifies an abyss, bottomless, deep, profound. … "darkness was upon the face of the deep." The word there rendered deep is the same word that is here rendered bottomless pit." — DAR 690.3.

- **Isaiah 24:21-22** — "The LORD shall punish... the kings of the earth upon the earth. And they shall be gathered together, as prisoners are gathered in the pit, and shall be shut up in the prison, and after many days shall they be visited." The wicked are shut in the prison of the grave and "visited" — raised — after the long interval.

  > "The revelator foretells the banishment of Satan and the condition of chaos and desolation to which the earth is to be reduced, and he declares that this condition will exist for a thousand years." — GC 658.2.

  > "Here is to be the home of Satan with his evil angels for a thousand years. Limited to the earth, he will not have access to other worlds to tempt and annoy those who have never fallen. It is in this sense that he is bound: there are none remaining, upon whom he can exercise his power." — GC 659.

- **Isaiah 14:12-17** — "How art thou fallen from heaven, O Lucifer... Is this the man that made the earth to tremble... that made the world as a wilderness?" Satan, surveying the wreck of his rebellion, is left alone with his angels to behold the ruin he wrought.

  > "Even the wicked are now placed beyond the power of Satan, and alone with his evil angels he remains to realize the effect of the curse which sin has brought." — GC 660.1-660.2.

  > "During the thousand years of his confinement to this earth, all the saints are in heaven, beyond the power of his temptations; and all the wicked are in their graves… and thus is he bound, being condemned throughout this period to a state of hopeless inactivity." — DAR 691.

- **Jude 1:6** — The angels "he hath reserved in everlasting chains under darkness unto the judgment of the great day." Fallen angels are reserved in darkness for the judgment — Satan and his host left to "realize the effect of the curse."

  > "Even the wicked are now placed beyond the power of Satan, and alone with his evil angels he remains to realize the effect of the curse which sin has brought." — GC 660.1.

### The Saints Reign and Judge with Christ Above

- **Revelation 20:4** — "I saw thrones, and they sat upon them, and judgment was given unto them." The redeemed sit enthroned in heaven; their employment during the thousand years is to assign the wicked dead the punishment due their deeds.

  > "John now directs our attention to the saints in victory and glory, — the saints reigning with Christ — their employment being to assign to the wicked dead the punishment due their evil deeds." — DAR 692.2.

  > "The saints, in unison with Jesus, pass their judgment upon the wicked dead. "Behold ye," said the angel, "the saints, in unison with Jesus, sit in judgment, and mete out to the wicked according to the deeds done in the body."" — EW 52.2.

- **1 Corinthians 6:2-3** — "Do ye not know that the saints shall judge the world?... Know ye not that we shall judge angels?" The saints share Christ's judgment of the lost and of fallen angels — the very work of the millennial reign.

  > "John now directs our attention to the saints in victory and glory, — the saints reigning with Christ — their employment being to assign to the wicked dead the punishment due their evil deeds." — DAR 692.2.

  > "It is at this time that, as foretold by Paul, "the saints shall judge the world." … In union with Christ they judge the wicked, comparing their acts with the statute book, the Bible, and deciding every case according to the deeds done in the body." — GC 660.4.

- **Revelation 20:6** — "They shall be priests of God and of Christ, and shall reign with him a thousand years"; on such "the second death hath no power." The first-resurrection saints are forever safe — beyond the reach of the second death.

  > "Blessed and holy is he that hath part in the first resurrection: on such the second death hath no power, but they shall be priests of God and of Christ, and shall reign with him a thousand years." — DAR 692.

  > "Upon those that had part in the first resurrection, the second death has no power. While God is to the wicked a consuming fire, He is to His people both a sun and a shield." — GC 673.3.

- **Daniel 7:22** — "Judgment was given to the saints of the most High; and the time came that the saints possessed the kingdom." The prophecy of Daniel meets the vision of John: judgment given to the saints.

  > "Daniel declares that when the Ancient of Days came, "judgment was given to the saints of the Most High." Daniel 7:22. At this time the righteous reign as kings and priests unto God." — GC 660.4.

- **Revelation 5:10** — "Hast made us unto our God kings and priests: and we shall reign on the earth." The redeemed reign as kings and priests — first in heaven through the thousand years, then on the renewed earth.

  > "The saints will rest in the Holy City and reign as kings and priests one thousand years; then Jesus will descend with the saints upon the Mount of Olives, and the mount will part asunder and become a mighty plain for the Paradise of God to rest upon." — EW 51.3.

  > "Then I saw thrones, and Jesus and the redeemed saints sat upon them; and the saints reigned as kings and priests unto God. Christ, in union with His people, judged the wicked dead." — EW 290.3.

- **Psalm 149:5-9** — The saints have "the high praises of God... in their mouth, and a twoedged sword in their hand; to execute vengeance upon the heathen... to execute upon them the judgment written: this honour have all his saints." The honor of executing the written judgment belongs to all the saints.

  > "It is at this time that, as foretold by Paul, "the saints shall judge the world." … Then the portion which the wicked must suffer is meted out, according to their works; and it is recorded against their names in the book of death." — GC 660.4.

### The Second Resurrection, the Holy City, and the Second Death

- **Revelation 20:5** — "The rest of the dead lived not again until the thousand years were finished." At the close of the thousand years the wicked dead are raised — the second resurrection.

  > "The Wicked Raised to Life. — The wicked who are raised at the end of the thousand years as really live again as they have once lived on the earth. To deny this is to do violence to this scripture." — DAR 694.1.

  > "Then, in terrible, fearful majesty, Jesus called forth the wicked dead; and they came up with the same feeble, sickly bodies that went into the grave. … At the first resurrection all came forth in immortal bloom; but at the second the marks of the curse are visible on all." — EW 292.1.

- **Revelation 21:2** & **Revelation 21:10** — "The holy city, new Jerusalem, coming down from God out of heaven" — "that great city, the holy Jerusalem, descending out of heaven from God." The Holy City, with the saints within it, descends to the earth at the end of the thousand years.

  > "At the end of the one thousand years, the holy city, the New Jerusalem, in which the saints have dwelt in heaven during that period, comes down, and is located upon the earth, and becomes the camp of the saints." — DAR 694.3.

  > "As the New Jerusalem, in its dazzling splendor, comes down out of heaven, it rests upon the place purified and made ready to receive it, and Christ, with His people and the angels, enters the Holy City." — GC 662-663.

- **Revelation 20:8-9** — Satan loosed deceives the nations, "Gog and Magog, to gather them together to battle... and they went up on the breadth of the earth, and compassed the camp of the saints about, and the beloved city." The risen wicked, marshalled by Satan, surround the City — the last assault.

  > "And shall go out to deceive the nations which are in the four quarters of the earth, Gog and Magog, to gather them together to battle… And they went up on the breadth of the earth, and compassed the camp of the saints about, and the beloved city." — DAR 694.2-694.3.

  > "He proposes to lead them against the camp of the saints and to take possession of the City of God. With fiendish exultation he points to the unnumbered millions who have been raised from the dead and declares that as their leader he is well able to overthrow the city." — GC 663.

- **Revelation 20:9** — "And fire came down from God out of heaven, and devoured them." The besieging host is consumed by fire from heaven — the execution of the judgment the saints had meted out.

  > "But fire comes down from God out of heaven, and devours them. The word here rendered devoured, Professor Stuart admits is "intensive," and signifies, "to eat up, devour, so that it denotes utter excision."" — DAR 694.3.

  > "Satan rushes into the midst of his followers and tries to stir up the multitude to action. But fire from God out of heaven is rained upon them, and the great men, and mighty men, the noble, the poor and miserable, are all consumed together." — EW 294.1.

- **Revelation 20:11-12** — "I saw a great white throne... and I saw the dead, small and great, stand before God; and the books were opened... and the dead were judged out of those things which were written in the books, according to their works." The wicked stand at the great white throne to receive the sentence already recorded.

  > "It is the great white throne of judgment, before which they are assembled to receive their awful sentence of condemnation and death. Before this throne the heavens and the earth flee away, so that no place is found for them." — DAR 697.2-697.3.

  > "As soon as the books of record are opened, and the eye of Jesus looks upon the wicked, they are conscious of every sin which they have ever committed." — GC 666.

- **Revelation 20:13-14** — "Death and hell delivered up the dead... and they were judged every man according to their works. And death and hell were cast into the lake of fire. This is the second death." The grave gives up the wicked, and death itself is destroyed — this is the second death.

  > "And the sea gave up the dead which were in it; and death and hell delivered up the dead which were in them: and they were judged every man according to their works. And death and hell were cast into the lake of fire. This is the second death." — DAR 697.2.

  > "The whole wicked world stand arraigned at the bar of God on the charge of high treason against the government of heaven. … the sentence of eternal death is pronounced against them." — GC 668.2.

- **Revelation 20:15** — "Whosoever was not found written in the book of life was cast into the lake of fire." The lake of fire is the agency that closes God's controversy with sin — utter, final destruction, not endless torment.

  > "May it not be called a symbol of the agencies which God employs to close up his controversy with the living wicked at the beginning of the thousand years, and with all the hosts of the ungodly at the end of that period?" — DAR 697.1.

  > "The works that are in the world are burned up; and by the poisonous gases evolved, and the flames, the wicked are destroyed… The heat being raised to a sufficient degree of intensity, would not the whole earth be converted into gas, and become invisible." — DAR 698.1-698.3.

- **Malachi 4:1-3** — "The day that cometh shall burn them up... and ye shall tread down the wicked; for they shall be ashes under the soles of your feet." The wicked become ashes — reduced to nothing, root and branch, while the righteous tread the cleansed ground.

  > "In the cleansing flames the wicked are at last destroyed, root and branch—Satan the root, his followers the branches. The full penalty of the law has been visited; the demands of justice have been met." — GC 673.1.

  > "They are now consumed root and branch. They have died an everlasting death. They are never to have a resurrection, and God will have a clean universe." — EW 295.1.

- **2 Peter 3:10** — "The day of the Lord will come as a thief... the earth also and the works that are therein shall be burned up." The same fire that consumes the wicked dissolves the elements and burns up the works of the earth.

  > "The works that are in the world are burned up… Secondly, the heat is raised till all the material of which this globe is composed, is fused like the ores in a smelter's furnace, and the whole earth becomes a fluid, fiery, molten mass." — DAR 698.1-698.2.

  > ""I saw a new heaven and a new earth: for the first heaven and the first earth were passed away." Revelation 21:1. The fire that consumes the wicked purifies the earth. Every trace of the curse is swept away." — GC 674.1.

- **1 Corinthians 15:26** — "The last enemy that shall be destroyed is death." Death and the grave, cast into the lake of fire, are abolished — the final enemy overthrown.

  > "Satan's work of ruin is forever ended. … Now God's creatures are forever delivered from his presence and temptations. … Upon those that had part in the first resurrection, the second death has no power." — GC 673.2-673.3.

### The New Heavens and the New Earth — the Controversy Ended

- **2 Peter 3:13** — "We, according to his promise, look for new heavens and a new earth, wherein dwelleth righteousness." The same fire that purifies the earth fits it for the eternal home of the righteous.

  > "The fire that consumes the wicked purifies the earth. Every trace of the curse is swept away. No eternally burning hell will keep before the ransomed the fearful consequences of sin." — GC 674.1.

  > "By the first heaven and first earth, John unquestionably means the present heaven and earth, "the heavens and the earth which are now." 2 Peter 3:7." — DAR 702.3.

- **Revelation 21:1** — "I saw a new heaven and a new earth: for the first heaven and the first earth were passed away; and there was no more sea." The purifying fire sweeps away every trace of the curse; this very earth, renewed, becomes the inheritance of the saved.

  > "And I saw a new heaven and a new earth: for the first heaven and the first earth were passed away; and there was no more sea." — DAR 702.1-702.3.

  > "The fire that consumes the wicked purifies the earth. Every trace of the curse is swept away. No eternally burning hell will keep before the ransomed the fearful consequences of sin." — GC 674.1.

- **Revelation 21:3-4** — "The tabernacle of God is with men, and he will dwell with them... and God shall wipe away all tears from their eyes; and there shall be no more death, neither sorrow, nor crying, neither shall there be any more pain." God dwells with His people; pain, death, and sorrow are forever gone.

  > "Pain cannot exist in the atmosphere of heaven. There will be no more tears, no funeral trains, no badges of mourning. "There shall be no more death, neither sorrow, nor crying: ... for the former things are passed away."" — GC 676.1.

  > "And God shall wipe away all tears from their eyes; and there shall be no more death, neither sorrow, nor crying, neither shall there be any more pain: for the former things are passed away." — DAR 704.

- **Revelation 21:5** — "He that sat upon the throne said, Behold, I make all things new... Write: for these words are true and faithful." The Creator's own pledge of a wholly renewed creation, sworn faithful and true.

  > "The time has come to which holy men have looked with longing since the flaming sword barred the first pair from Eden, the time for "the redemption of the purchased possession." … All that was lost by sin has been restored." — GC 674-675.

- **Revelation 22:1-5** — "A pure river of water of life... the tree of life, which bare twelve manner of fruits... and there shall be no more curse... and they shall reign for ever and ever." Eden restored — the tree of life, no curse, no night, and the redeemed reigning forever.

  > "The tree of life bears twelve kinds of fruit, and yields its fruit every month. This fact throws light upon the declaration in Isaiah 66:23, that all flesh shall come up "from one new moon to another" to worship before the Lord of hosts." — DAR 710-718.

  > "In the Bible the inheritance of the saved is called "a country." … There the heavenly Shepherd leads His flock to fountains of living waters. The tree of life yields its fruit every month, and the leaves of the tree are for the service of the nations." — GC 675.1-676.4.

  > "I then saw Jesus leading His people to the tree of life… "The leaves of this tree are for the healing of the nations. Eat ye all of it." … On each side of this river was the tree of life." — EW 289.1.

- **Malachi 4:2** — "Unto you that fear my name shall the Sun of righteousness arise with healing in his wings; and ye shall go forth, and grow up as calves of the stall." The same day that burns the wicked brings healing and gladness to the righteous.

  > "There, "the wilderness and the solitary place shall be glad for them; and the desert shall rejoice, and blossom as the rose." … "They shall not hurt nor destroy in all My holy mountain," saith the Lord." — GC 675.

- **Revelation 5:13** — "Every creature... heard I saying, Blessing, and honour, and glory, and power, be unto him that sitteth upon the throne, and unto the Lamb for ever and ever." The whole clean universe joins in one anthem — "the great controversy is ended. Sin and sinners are no more. The entire universe is clean."

  > "The great controversy is ended. Sin and sinners are no more. The entire universe is clean. One pulse of harmony and gladness beats through the vast creation. … From the minutest atom to the greatest world, all things, animate and inanimate, … declare that God is love." — GC 678.2-678.3.

---

_Compiled in Haskell's manner from the Seventh-day Adventist pioneers — Uriah Smith, William Miller, Josiah Litch, Joseph Bates, A. T. Jones, E. J. Waggoner, and Samuel S. Snow — together with the writings of Ellen G. White. The texts here gathered are but suggestive way-marks on the great lines of prophetic truth. Take the Bible in hand, look up each text, and prove the chain link by link — from the year-day rule, through the four outlines of Daniel and the visions of the Revelation, to the cleansing of the sanctuary and the kingdom of glory — that the whole interlocking testimony may stand, in the mouth of many witnesses, as the more sure word of prophecy._
